sabbeva dāsā mandhātu       ye pāṇā paṭhavissitā .

[374] na kahāpaṇavassena           titti kāmesu vijjati    
appassādā dukhā kāmā      iti viññāya paṇḍito .

[375] api dibbesu kāmesu         ratiṃ so nādhigacchati  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 103 -->
<a id="page-103"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 103]*

Taṇhakkhayarato hoti          sammāsambuddhasāvakoti .

Mandhāturājajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^103-9] tiritivacchajātakaṃ [^103-1]    
[376] nayimassa vijjāmayamatthi kiñci    
na bandhavo no pana te sahāyo    
atha kena vaṇṇena tirītivaccho [^103-2]    
tedaṇḍiko bhuñjati aggapiṇḍaṃ .

[377] āpāsu me yuddhaparājitassa    
ekassa katvā vivanasmi ghore    
pasārayi kicchagatassa pāṇiṃ    
tenuddhatāriṃ dukhasampareto .

[378] etassa kicchena idhānupatto    
veyyāsino [^103-3] vīsaya jīvaloke    
lābhāraho tāta tirītivaccho    
dethassa bhogaṃ yajitañca [^103-4] yaññanti .

Tiritivacchajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

#[^103-1] ma. Tirīṭa ... .  [^103-2] ma. Tirīṭavaccho .  [^103-3] sī. Vesāyino . Ma. Vesāyino visayā .Tha  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 104 -->
<a id="page-104"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 104]*

10 dūtajātakaṃ    
[379] yassatthā dūramāyanti         amittamapi yācituṃ    
tassūdarassahaṃ dūto           mā me kujjha rathesabha .

[380] yassa divā ca ratto ca       vasamāyanti māṇavā    
tassūdarassahaṃ dūto           mā me kujjha [^104-1] rathesabha .

[381] dadāmi te brāhmaṇa rohiṇīnaṃ    
gavaṃ sahassaṃ saha puṅgavena    
dūto hi dūtassa kathaṃ na dajjaṃ    
mayampi tasseva bhavāma dūtāti .

Dūtajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Saṅkappavaggo paṭhamo .

tassuddānaṃ    
usukāravaro tilamuṭṭhi maṇi    
hayarāja vihaṅgama āsīviso    
janasandha kahāpaṇavassa puna    
tiriti [^104-2] puna dūtāvarena [^104-3] dasa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 105 -->
<a id="page-105"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 105]*

[^105-2] padumavaggo    
[^105-1] padumajātakaṃ    
[382] yathā kesā ca massu ca       chinnaṃ chinnaṃ virūhati    
evaṃ rūhatu te nāsā        padumaṃ dehi yācito .

[383] yathā sāradikaṃ vījaṃ           khette vuttaṃ virūhati    
evaṃ rūhatu te nāsā        padumaṃ dehi yācito .

[384] ubhopi vilapantete [^105-1]       api padmāni dassati    
vajjuṃ vā te na vā vajjuṃ     natthi nāsāya rūhanā    
dehi samma padumāni          padmā me dehi yācitoti [^105-2] .

Padumajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^105-2] mudupāṇijātakaṃ    
[385] pāṇi ce muduko cassa        nāgo cassa sukārito    
andhakāro ca vasseyya       atha nūna tadā siyā .

[386] analā mudusambhāsā          duppūrā tā nadīsamā    
sīdanti naṃ viditvāna          ārakā parivajjaye .

[387] yaṃ etā upasevanti         chandasā vā dhanena vā    
jātavedova saṇṭhānaṃ         khippaṃ anudahanti nanti .

Mudupāṇijātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .่  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 106 -->
<a id="page-106"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 106]*

[^106-3] cullapalobhanajātakaṃ    
[388] abhijjamāne vārismiṃ         sayaṃ āgamma iddhiyā    
missībhāvitthiyā gantvā       saṃsīdasi mahaṇṇave .

[389] āvaṭṭanī mahāmāyā         brahmacariyavikopanā    
sīdanti naṃ viditvāna          ārakā parivajjaye .

[390] yaṃ etā upasevanti         chandasā vā dhanena vā    
jātavedova saṇṭhānaṃ         khippaṃ anudahanti nanti .

Cullapalobhanajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^106-4] mahāpanādajātakaṃ    
[391] panādo nāma so rājā      yassa yūpo suvaṇṇiyo [^106-1]    
tiriyaṃ soḷasubbedho          uccamāhu [^106-2] sahassadhā .

[392] sahassakaṇḍo satageṇḍu        dhajālu haritāmayo    
anaccuṃ tattha gandhabbā        cha sahassāni sattadhā .

[393] evametaṃ tadā āsi         yathā bhāsasi bhaddaji    
sakko ahaṃ tadā āsiṃ        veyyāvaccakaro tavāti .

Mahāpanādajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^106-5] khurappajātakaṃ  
[394] disvā khurappe dhanuveganunne  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 107 -->
<a id="page-107"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 107]*

Khagge gahite [^107-1] tikhiṇe teladhote    
tasmiṃ bhayasmiṃ maraṇe virūḷhe [^107-2]    
kasmā nu te nāhu chambhitattaṃ .

[395] disvā khurappe dhanuveganunne    
khagge gahite [^107-3] tikhiṇe teladhote    
tasmiṃ bhayasmiṃ maraṇe virūḷhe [^107-4]    
vedaṃ alatthaṃ vipulaṃ uḷāraṃ .

[396] so vedajāto ajjhabhaviṃ amitte    
pubbeva me jīvitamāsi cattaṃ    
na hi jīvite ālayaṃ kubbamāno    
sūro kayirā sūrakiccaṃ kadācīti .

Khurappajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^107-6] vātaggasindhavajātakaṃ    
[397] yenāsi kīsiyā paṇḍu         yena bhattaṃ na ruccati    
ayaṃ so āgato bhatto [^107-5]    kasmādāni palāyasi .

[398] nukho [^107-6] panādikeneva       santhavo nāma jāyati    
yaso hāyati itthīnaṃ          tasmā tāta palāyihaṃ [^107-7] .

[399] yasassinaṃ kule jātaṃ          ābhataṃ [^107-8] yā na icchati    
#1-[^107-3] ma. Gahīte .  2-[^107-4] ma. Viyuḷhe .  [^107-5] sī. Tāto . Ma. Bhattā .  [^107-6] sī. Ma. Sace .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 108 -->
<a id="page-108"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 108]*

Socatī cirarattāya         vātaggamiva gadrabhīti [^108-1] .

Vātaggasindhavajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ    
[^108-7] suvaṇṇakakkaṭakajātakaṃ [^108-2]    
[400] siṅgī migo āyatacakkhunetto    
aṭṭhittaco vārisayo alomo    
tenābhibhūto kapaṇaṃ rudāmi    
māheva maṃ pāṇasamaṃ jaheyya .

[401] ayya na taṃ jahissāmi         kuñjaraṃ saṭṭhihāyanaṃ [^108-3]    
paṭhabyā cāturantāya         suppiyo hosi me tuvaṃ .

[402] ye kuḷīrā samuddasmiṃ         gaṅgāya yamunāya [^108-4] ca    
tesaṃ tvaṃ vārijo seṭṭho     muñca rodantiyā patinti .

Suvaṇṇakakkaṭakajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^108-8] ārāmadūsakajātakaṃ    
[403] yomesañca sametānaṃ [^108-5]      ahuvā seṭṭhasammato    
tassāyaṃ edisī paññā        kimeva itarā pajā .

[404] evameva tuvaṃ brahme        anaññāya vinindasi    
kathaṃ hi mūlaṃ adisvā [^108-6]       rukkhaṃ jaññā patiṭṭhitaṃ .

#[^108-1] sī. Kundalī . Ma. Bhaddalī .  [^108-2] ma. Kakkaṭakajātakaṃ .  [^108-3] sī. Yu. Kuñjarasaṭṭhihāyana .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 109 -->
<a id="page-109"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 109]*

[405] nāhaṃ tumhe vinindāmi        ye cettha [^109-1] vānarā vane    
vissaseno [^109-2] ca gārayho    yassatthā rukkharopakāti .

Ārāmadūsakajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^109-9] sujātājātakaṃ [^109-3]    
[406] nahi vaṇṇena sampannā        mañjukā piyadassanā    
kharavācā piyā honti        asmiṃ loke paramhi ca .

[407] nanu passasimaṃ kāḷiṃ           dubbaṇṇaṃ tilakāhataṃ    
kokilaṃ saṇhavācena          bahūnaṃ pāṇinaṃ piyaṃ .

[408] tasmā sakhilavācassa          mantabhāṇī anuddhato    
atthaṃ dhammañca dīpeti         madhurantassa bhāsitanti .

Sujātājātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 ulūkajātakaṃ    
[409] sabbehi kira ñātīhi          kosiyo issaro kato    
sace ñātīhi anuññāto [^109-4]    bhaṇeyyāhaṃ ekavācikaṃ .

[410] bhaṇa samma anuññāto         atthaṃ dhammañca kevalaṃ    
santi hi daharā pakkhī         paññavanto jutindharā .

[411] na me ruccati bhaddaṃ vo       ulūkassābhisecanaṃ    
#[^109-1] sī. Ma. Ye caññe .  [^109-2] ma. Visaseno va .  [^109-3] ma. Sujātajātakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 110 -->
<a id="page-110"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 110]*

Akkuddhassa mukhaṃ passa         kathaṃ kuddho karissatīti .

Ulūkajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Padumavaggo dutiyo    
tassuddānaṃ    
padumuttama nāgasirivhayano    
samahaṇṇava yūpa khurappavaro    
atha bhaddalī kuñjara rukkha puna    
kharavāca ulūkavarena dasa .

[^110-3] udapānadūsakavaggo [^110-1]    
[^110-1] udapānadūsakajātakaṃ    
[412] āraññakassa [^110-2] isino       ciraṃ rattaṃ tapassino    
kicchākataṃ udapānaṃ           kathaṃ samma apāhasi [^110-3] .

[413] esa dhammo sigālānaṃ        yaṃ pivitvā ohadāmase    
pitupitāmahaṃ dhammo           na taṃ ujjhātumarahasi .

[414] yesaṃ vo ediso dhammo      adhammo pana kīdiso    
mā vo dhammaṃ adhammaṃ vā      addasāma kudācananti .

Udapānadūsakajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 111 -->
<a id="page-111"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 111]*

[^111-2] byagghajātakaṃ    
[415] yena mittena saṃsaggā        yogakkhemo vihīyati    
pubbevajjhābhavantassa         rakkhe akkhiṃva paṇḍito .

[416] yena mittena saṃsaggā        yogakkhemo pavaḍḍhati    
kareyyattasamaṃ vuttiṃ          sabbakiccesu paṇḍito .

[417] etha byagghā nivattavho      paccupetha [^111-1] mahāvanaṃ    
mā no [^111-2] vanaṃ chindi nibyagghaṃ  byagghā māhesu nibbanāti .

Byagghajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^111-3] kacchapajātakaṃ    
[418] ko nu vaḍḍhitabhattova [^111-3]      pūrahatthova brāhmaṇo    
kahannu bhikkhaṃ acari           kaṃ saddhaṃ upasaṅkami .

[419] ahaṃ kapismi dummedho         anāmāsāni āmasiṃ    
tvaṃ maṃ mocaya bhaddante       mutto gaccheyya pabbataṃ .

[420] kacchapā kassapā honti       koṇḍaññā honti makkaṭā๊    
muñca kassapa koṇḍaññaṃ        kataṃ methunakaṃ tayāti .

Kacchapajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^111-4] lolajātakaṃ    
[421] kāyaṃ balākā sikhinī          corī laṅghīpitāmahā [^111-4]    
#[^111-1] sī. Yu. Paccametha .  [^111-2] ma. Ayaṃ saddo natthi .  [^111-3] ma. Uddhitabhattova .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 112 -->
<a id="page-112"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 112]*

Oraṃ balāke āgaccha        caṇḍo me vāyaso sakhā .

[422] nāhaṃ balākā sikhinī          ahaṃ lolasmi vāyaso    
akatvā vacanaṃ tuyhaṃ          passa lūnosmi āgato .

[423] puna pāpajjasī samma          sīlaṃ hi tava tādisaṃ    
nahi mānusikā [^112-1] bhogā      subhuñjā honti pakkhināti .

Lolajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^112-5] rucirajātakaṃ    
[424] kāyaṃ balākā rucirā         kākaniṇḍasmi [^112-2] macchati    
caṇḍo kāko sakhā mayhaṃ      yassa [^112-3] cetaṃ kulāvakaṃ .

[425] nanu maṃ samma jānāsi         dija sāmakabhojanaṃ    
akatvā vacanaṃ tuyhaṃ          passa lūnosmi āgato .

[426] puna pāpajjasī samma          sīlaṃ hi tava tādisaṃ    
nahi mānusikā [^112-4] bhogā      subhuñjā honti pakkhināti .

Rucirajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^112-6] kurudhammajātakaṃ    
[427] tava saddhañca sīlañca          viditvāna janādhipa    
vaṇṇaṃ añjanavaṇṇena          kāliṅgasmiṃ vinimhase [^112-5] .

#1-[^112-4] ma. Mānusakā .  [^112-2] sī. Ma. Kākanīḷasmimacchati .  [^112-3] sī. Yu. Tassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 113 -->
<a id="page-113"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 113]*

[428] annabhaccā ca bhaccā ca       yodha uddissa gacchati    
sabbete appaṭikkhippā       pubbācariyavaco idaṃ .

[429] dadāmi vo brāhmaṇā nāgametaṃ    
rājārahaṃ rājabhogaṃ [^113-1] yasassinaṃ    
alaṅkataṃ hemajālābhichannaṃ    
sasārathī [^113-2] gacchatha yenakāmanti .

Kurudhammajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^113-7] romajātakaṃ    
[430] vassāni paññāsa samādhikāni    
vāsimhi selassa guhāya romaka    
asaṅkamānā abhinibbutacittā [^113-3]    
hatthatthamāyanti [^113-4] mamaṇḍajā pure .

[431] tedāni vaṅkaṅga [^113-5] kimatthamussukkā [^113-6]    
bhajanti aññaṃ girikandaraṃ dijā    
nanu na maññanti mamaṃ yathā pure    
cirampavuṭṭhā athavā na te ime .

[432] jānāma taṃ na mayaṃ sammuḷhā [^113-7]    
sveva tvaṃ te mayamasma naññe [^113-8]    
#[^113-1] ma. Rājabhoggaṃ .  [^113-2] ma. Sasārathiṃ .  [^113-3] ma. Abhinibbutattā .  [^113-4] ma. Hatlattamāyanti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 114 -->
<a id="page-114"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 114]*

Cittañca te asmi jane paduṭṭhaṃ    
ājīvaka [^114-1] tena taṃ uttasāmāti .

Romajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^114-8] mahisajātakaṃ 2-่    
[433] kimatthamabhisandhāya [^114-3]         lahucittassa dubbhino [^114-4]    
sabbakāmaduhasseva [^114-5]        imaṃ dukkhaṃ titikkhasi .

[434] siṅgena nīharāsetaṃ [^114-6]       padasāva adhiṭṭhaha    
bhiyyo bālā pakuṭṭeyyuṃ [^114-7]   no cassa paṭisedhako .

[435] mamevāyaṃ maññamāno         aññaṃpevaṃ [^114-8] karissati    
te naṃ tattha vadhissanti        sā me mutti bhavissatīti .

Mahisajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^114-9] satapattajātakaṃ    
[436] yathā māṇavako panthe        sigāliṃ vanagocariṃ    
atthakāmaṃ pavedenti         anatthakāmāti maññati    
anatthakāmaṃ satapattaṃ          atthakāmoti maññati .

[437] evameva idhekacco         puggalo hoti tādiso    
hitebhi vacanaṃ vutto          paṭiggaṇhati vāmato .

#[^114-1] ma. Ājīvikā .  [^114-2] ma. Mahiṃsarājajātakaṃ .  [^114-3] sī. Yu. Kamattha ... .  [^114-4] sa. Yu. Dūbhino .

#[^114-5] sī. Sabbakāmadadasseva .  [^114-6] sī. Ma. Nihanāhetaṃ .  [^114-7] sī. Ma. Pakujjheyyuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 115 -->
<a id="page-115"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 115]*

[438] ye ca kho naṃ pasaṃsanti        bhayā ukkaṃsayanti vā    
tañhi so maññate mittaṃ       satapattaṃva māṇavoti .

Satapattajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 pūṭadūsakajātakaṃ    
[439] addhā hi nūna migarājā       pūṭakammassa kovido    
tathāhi pūṭaṃ dūseti           aññaṃ nūna karissati .

[440] na me pitā vā mātā vā    pūṭakammassa kovidā    
kataṃ kataṃ kho dūsema          evaṃ dhammamidaṃ kulaṃ .

[441] yesaṃ vo ediso dhammo      adhammo pana kīdiso    
mā vo dhammaṃ adhammaṃ vā      addasāma kudācananti .

Pūṭadūsakajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Udapānavaggo [^115-1] tatiyo    
tassuddānaṃ    
udapānavaraṃ vanabyaggha kapi    
sikhinī ca balākaruciravaro    
sujanādhipa romaka dūsa puna    
satapattavaro pūṭakamma dasa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 116 -->
<a id="page-116"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 116]*

[^116-4] abbhantaravaggo    
[^116-1] abbhantarajātakaṃ    
[442] abbhantaro nāma dumo        yassa dibyamidaṃ phalaṃ    
bhutvā dohaḷinī nārī         cakkavattiṃ vijāyati .

[443] tvampi [^116-1] bhadde mahesīsi     sā cāpi [^116-2] patino piyā    
āharissati te rājā        idaṃ abbhantaraṃ phalaṃ .

[444] bhatturatthe parakkanto        yaṃ ṭhānamadhigacchati    
sūro attapariccāgī          labhamāno bhavāmahanti .

Abbhantarajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^116-2] seyyajātakaṃ    
[445] seyyaṃso seyyaso hoti      yo seyyamupasevati    
ekena sandhiṃ katvāna        sataṃ vajjhe amocayiṃ .

[446] tasmā sabbena lokena       sandhiṃ katvāna ekako [^116-3]    
pecca saggaṃ nigaccheyya       idaṃ suṇātha kāsiyā .

[447] idaṃ vatvā mahārājā        kaṃso bārāṇasiggaho    
dhanana tuṇḍiñca [^116-4] nikkhippa      saññamaṃ ajjhupāgamīti .

Seyyajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 117 -->
<a id="page-117"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 117]*

[^117-3] vaḍḍhakīsūkarajātakaṃ    
[448] varaṃ varaṃ tvaṃ nihanaṃ pure cari    
asmiṃ padese abhibhuyya sūkare    
sodāni eko byaggha pagamma jhāyasi    
balannu te byaggha na cajja vijjati .

[449] imassutā [^117-1] yanti disodisaṃ pure    
bhayaddhitā [^117-2] leṇagavesino puthū    
tedāni saṅgamma vasanti ekato    
yatthaṭṭhitā duppasahajjime mayā .

[450] namatthu saṅghāna samāgatānaṃ    
disvā sayaṃ saṃkhya vadāmi abbhutaṃ    
byagghaṃ migā yattha jiniṃsu dāṭhino    
sāmaggiyā dāṭhabalesu muccareti .

Vaḍḍhakīsūkarajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^117-4] sirijātakaṃ    
[451] yaṃ ussukkā saṅgharanti        alakkhikā bahuṃ dhanaṃ    
sippavanto asippā vā       lakkhikā tāni bhuñjare [^117-3] .

#[^117-1] sī. Ma. Ime sudaṃ .  [^117-2] ma. Bhayaṭṭitā .  [^117-3] sī. Lakkhīva tāni bhuñjati . Ma. Lakkhivā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 118 -->
<a id="page-118"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 118]*

[452] sabbattha katapuññassa          aticcaññeva pāṇino    
uppajjanti bahū bhogā        api nāyatanesupi .

[453] kukkuṭo maṇayo daṇḍo        thiyo ca puññalakkhaṇā    
uppajjanti apāpassa         katapuññassa jantunoti .

Sirijātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^118-5] maṇisūkarajātakaṃ    
[454] dariyā satta vassāni         tiṃsamattā vasāmase    
haññāma maṇino ābhaṃ         iti no mantitaṃ ahu .

[455] yāvatā maṇi ghaṃsāma [^118-1]       bhiyyo vodāyate maṇi    
idañcidāni pucchāma          kiṃ kiccaṃ idha maññasi .

[456] ayaṃ maṇi veḷuriyo           akāco vimalo subho    
nāssa sakkā siriṃ hantuṃ       apakkamatha sūkarāti .

Maṇisūkarajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^118-6] sālukajātakaṃ    
[457] mā sālukassa pihayi          āturannāni bhuñjati    
appossukko bhusaṃ khāda       etaṃ dīghāyulakkhaṇaṃ .

[458] idāni so idhāgantvā       atithi [^118-2] yuttasevako    
atha dakkhasi sālukaṃ           sayantaṃ mūsaluttaraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 119 -->
<a id="page-119"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 119]*

[459] vikantaṃ sūkaraṃ disvā          sayantaṃ mūsaluttaraṃ    
jaraggavā acintesuṃ          varamhākaṃ bhusāmivāti .

Sālukajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^119-7] lābhagarahikajātakaṃ [^119-1]    
[460] nānummatto nāpisuṇo        nānaṭo nākutūhalo    
muḷhesu labhate lābhaṃ         esā te anusāsanī .

[461] dhiratthu taṃ yasalābhaṃ           dhanalābhañca brāhmaṇa    
yā vutti vinipātena         adhammacariyāya [^119-2] vā .

[462] api ce pattamādāya         anāgāro paribbaje    
esāva jīvikā seyyo [^119-3]    yā cādhammena esanāti .

Lābhagarahikajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^119-8] macchadānajātakaṃ [^119-4]    
[463] agghanti macchā adhikaṃ sahassaṃ    
na so atthi yo imaṃ saddaheyya    
mayhañca assū idha satta māsā    
ahampi taṃ macchadānaṃ [^119-5] kiṇeyyaṃ .

[464] macchānaṃ bhojanaṃ datvā        mama dakkhiṇamādisi    
taṃ dakkhiṇaṃ sarantiyā          kataṃ apacitiṃ tayā .

#[^119-1] sī. ... Lābhagarahajātakaṃ .  [^119-2] ma. Adhammacaraṇena .  [^119-3] sī. Yu. Seyyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 120 -->
<a id="page-120"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 120]*

[465] paduṭṭhacittassa na phāti hoti    
na cāpi naṃ devatā pūjayanti    
yo bhātaraṃ pettikaṃ sāpateyyaṃ    
avañcayī dukkaṭakammakārīti .

Macchadānajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^120-9] nānāchandajātakaṃ    
[466] nānāchandā mahārāja        ekāgāre vasāmase    
ahaṃ gāmavaraṃ icche          brāhmaṇī ca gavaṃ sataṃ .๋    
[467] putto ājaññakaṃ rathaṃ         kaññā ca maṇikuṇḍalaṃ    
yā cesā puṇṇakā [^120-1] jammī   udukkhalaṃ abhikaṅkhati .

[468] brāhmaṇassa gāmavaraṃ         brāhmaṇiyā gavaṃ sataṃ    
puttassa ājaññarathaṃ          kaññāya maṇikuṇḍalaṃ    
yañcetaṃ puṇṇakaṃ [^120-2] jammiṃ      paṭipādethudukkhalanti .

Nānāchandajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 sīlavīmaṃsajātakaṃ    
[469] sīlaṃ kireva kalyāṇaṃ          sīlaṃ loke anuttaraṃ    
passa ghoraviso nāgo        sīlavāti na haññati .

[470] sohaṃ sīlaṃ samādissaṃ          loke anumataṃ sivaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 121 -->
<a id="page-121"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 121]*

Ariyavuttisamācāro          yena vuccati sīlavā .

[471] ñātīnañca piyo hoti         mittesu ca virocati    
kāyassa bhedā sugatiṃ         upapajjati sīlavāti .

Sīlavīmaṃsajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .(    
abbhantaravaggo catuttho .

tassuddānaṃ    
duma kaṃsa varuttamabyagghamigā    
maṇayo maṇi sālukamavhayano    
anusāsaniyo pica macchavaro    
maṇikuṇḍalakena kirena dasa .

[^121-5] kumbhavaggo    
[^121-1] bhadraghaṭabhedakajātakaṃ [^121-1]    
[472] sabbakāmadadaṃ kumbhaṃ           kūṭaṃ laddhāna dhuttako    
yāva naṃ anupāleti          tāva so sukhamedhati .

[473] yadā matto ca ditto ca      pamādā kumbhamabbhidā    
tato [^121-2] naggo ca pottho ca  pacchā bālo vihaññati .

[474] evameva [3] dhanaṃ laddhā    pamatto [^121-4] paribhuñjati  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 122 -->
<a id="page-122"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 122]*

Pacchā tapati dummedho        kūṭaṃ bhitvāva [^122-1] dhuttakoti .

Bhadraghaṭabhedakajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^122-2] supattajātakaṃ    
[475] bārāṇasyaṃ [^122-2] mahārāja      kākarājā nivāsako [^122-3]    
asītiyā sahassehi           supatto  parivārito .

[476] tassa dohaḷinī bhariyā         supassā [^122-4] bhakkhitumicchati [^122-5]    
rañño mahānase pakkaṃ        paccagghaṃ rājabhojanaṃ .

[477] tesāhaṃ pahito dūto         rañño camhi idhāgato    
bhattu apacitiṃ kummi           nāsāyamakaraṃ [^122-6] vaṇanti .

Supattajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^122-3] kāyanibbindajātakaṃ    
[478] phuṭṭhassa me aññatarena byādhinā    
rogena bāḷhaṃ dukhitassa ruppato    
parisussati khippamidaṃ kaḷevaraṃ    
pupphaṃ yathā paṃsuni ātape kataṃ .

[479] ajaññaṃ jaññasaṅkhātaṃ          asuciṃ sucisammataṃ    
nānākuṇapaparipūraṃ            jaññarūpaṃ apassato .

#[^122-1] sī. Yu. Bhinno va . [^122-2] sī. Yu. Bārāṇassaṃ . [^122-3] sī. Yu. Nivāriko . [^122-4] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 123 -->
<a id="page-123"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 123]*

[480] dhiratthumaṃ āturaṃ pūtikāyaṃ    
jigucchiyaṃ asuciṃ byādhidhammaṃ    
yatthappamattā adhimucchitā pajā    
hāpenti maggaṃ sugatūpapattiyāti .

Kāyanibbindajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^123-4] jambūkhādakajātakaṃ    
[481] koyaṃ bindussaro vaggu        saravantānamuttamo    
accuto jambusākhāya         moracchāpova kujjati .

[482] kulaputto pajānāti          kulaputtaṃ [^123-1] pasaṃsituṃ    
byagghacchāpasadisavaṇṇa         bhuñja samma dadāmi te .

[483] cirassaṃ vata passāmi          musāvādī samāgate    
vantādaṃ kuṇapādañca          aññamaññaṃ pasaṃsaketi .

Jambūkhādakajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^123-5] antajātakaṃ    
[484] usabhasseva te khandho        sīhasseva vijambhitaṃ    
migarāja namo tayatthu         api kiñci labhāmase .

[485] kulaputto pajānāti          kulaputtaṃ [^123-2] pasaṃsituṃ    
mayūragīvasaṅkāsa             ito pariyāhi vāyasa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 124 -->
<a id="page-124"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 124]*

[486] migānaṃ koṭṭhako [^124-1] anto    pakkhīnaṃ pana vāyaso    
eraṇḍo anto rukkhānaṃ      tayo antā samāgatāti .

Antajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^124-6] samuddajātakaṃ    
[487] ko nvāyaṃ [^124-2] loṇatoyasmiṃ    samantā paridhāvati    
macche makare ca vāreti      ūmīsu ca vihaññati .

[488] anantapāyī sakuṇo           atittoti disā suto    
samuddaṃ pātumicchāmi          sāgaraṃ saritappatiṃ .

[489] soyaṃ hāyatī ceva           pūrateva mahodadhi    
nāssa nāyati pītanto        apeyyo kira sāgaroti .

Samuddajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^124-7] kāmavilāpajātakaṃ    
[490] ucce sakuṇa ḍemāna         pattayāna vihaṅgama    
vajjāsi kho tvaṃ vāmūruṃ       ciraṃ kho sā karissati .

[491] idaṃ kho sā na jānāti       asiṃ sattiñca oḍḍitaṃ    
sā caṇḍī kāhatī kodhaṃ        taṃ me tappati no idha [^124-3] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 125 -->
<a id="page-125"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 125]*

[492] esa uppalasannāho         nikkhañcussīsake kataṃ [^125-1]    
kāsikañca muduṃ vatthaṃ          tappetu dhanakāmiyāti [^125-2]    
kāmavilāpajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^125-8] udumbarajātakaṃ    
[493] udumbarā cime pakkā        nigrodhā ca kapitthanā    
ehi nikkhamma bhuñjassu        kiṃ jighacchāya miyyasi .

[494] evaṃ so suhito hoti        yo vuḍḍhamapacāyati    
yathāhamajja suhito           dumapakkāni māsito .

[495] yaṃ vanejo vanejassa         vañceyya kapino kapi    
daharopi na saddheyya [^125-3]      nahi jiṇṇo jarākapīti .

Udumbarajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^125-9] komāriyaputtajātakaṃ [^125-4]    
[496] pure tuvaṃ sīlavataṃ sakāse    
okkandikaṃ [^125-5] kīḷasi assamamhi    
karohare makkaṭiyāni makkaṭa    
na taṃ mayaṃ sīlavataṃ ramāma .

#[^125-1] ma. ... Nikkhañcussīsakohitaṃ . [^125-2] ma. Dhanikā piyāti . [^125-3] ma. Daharo kapi saddheyya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 126 -->
<a id="page-126"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 126]*

[497] sutā hi mayhaṃ paramā visuddhi    
komāriyaputtassa [^126-1] bahussutassa    
mādāni maṃ maññi tuvaṃ yathā pureṭha    
jhānābhiyuttā viharāma āvuso [^126-2] .

[498] sacepi selasmi vapeyya bījaṃ    
devo ca vasse na hi taṃ virūḷhe    
sutā hi te sā paramā visuddhi    
ārā tuvaṃ makkaṭa jhānabhūmyāti [^126-3] .

Komāriyaputtajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 bakajātakaṃ    
[499] parapāṇaghāte [^126-4] jīvanto     maṃsalohitabhojano    
bako vataṃ samādāya          upapajji uposathaṃ .

[500] tassa sakko vataññāya        ajarūpenupāgami    
vītatapo ajjhapatto          bhañji lohitapo tapaṃ .

[501] evameva idhekacco [^126-5]      samādānasmi dubbalo [^126-6]    
lahuṃ karoti [^126-7] attānaṃ       bakova ajakāraṇāti .

Bakajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Kumbhavaggo pañcamo .

#[^126-1] ma. Komāraputta ... . [^126-2] ma. Jhānānuyutto viharāmi āvuso . [^126-3] ma. Jhānabhūmiyāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 127 -->
<a id="page-127"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 127]*

Tassuddānaṃ    
varakumbha supattasirivhayano    
sucisammata bindussaro cusabho    
saritaṃpati caṇḍī jarākapinā    
atha makkaṭiyā vakakena dasāti .

vagguddānaṃ    
saṅkappo padumo ceva        udapānena tatiyaṃ    
abbhantaraṃ ghaṭabhedaṃ           tikanipātamhilaṅkataṃ [^127-1] .

Tikanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 128 -->
<a id="page-128"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 128]*

Catukkanipātajātakaṃ    
[^128-1] kāliṅgavaggo    
[^128-1] cullakāliṅgajātakaṃ    
[502] vivaratha imāsaṃ dvāraṃ         nagarantaṃ pavīsituṃ [^128-1]    
aruṇarājassa sīhena          susiṭṭhena [^128-2] surakkhitaṃ nandisenena .

[503] jayo kāliṅgānaṃ asayhasāhinaṃ    
parājayo ajayo [^128-3] assakānaṃ    
iccevaṃ [^128-4] te bhāsitaṃ brahmacāri    
na ujubhūtā vitathaṃ bhaṇanti .

[504] devā musāvādamupātivattā    
saccaṃ tathaṃ paramaṃ karaṃ nu sakka [^128-5]    
tante musā bhāsitaṃ devarāja    
kiṃ vā paṭicca maghavā mahinda [^128-6] .

[505] nanu te sutaṃ brāhmaṇa bhaññamāne    
devā na issanti purisaparakkamassa    
damo samādhi manaso abhejjo [^128-7]    
#[^128-1] sī. Nagaraṃ pavisantu . Ma. Virutimāsaṃ dvāraṃ nagaraṃ pavisantu .

#[^128-2] sī. Yu. Susatthena . [^128-3] sī. Ma. Anayo . [^128-4] ma. Icceva . [^128-5] sī. Ma. Saccaṃ dhanaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 129 -->
<a id="page-129"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 129]*

Abyattatā [^129-1] nikkamanañca [^129-2] kāle    
daḷhañca viriyaṃ purisaparakkamo ca    
teneva āsi vijayo assakānanti .

Cullakāliṅgajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^129-2] mahāassārohajātakaṃ    
[506] adeyyesu dadaṃ dānaṃ         deyyesu nappavecchati    
āpāsu byasanaṃ patto        sahāyaṃ nādhigacchati .

[507] adeyyesu adadaṃ [^129-3] dānaṃ     deyyesu yo pavecchati    
āpāsu byasanaṃ patto        sahāyaṃ adhigacchati .

[508] saññogasambhoga visesadassanaṃ    
anariyadhammesu saṭhesu nassati    
katañca ariyesu ca añjasesu [^129-4]    
mahapphalaṃ hoti aṇumpi tādisu .

[509] yo pubbe katakalyāṇo       akā loke sudukkaraṃ    
pacchā kayirā na vā kayirā    accantaṃ pūjanārahoti .

Mahāassārohajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

#[^129-1] sī. Abyagghatā . Ma. Abyaggatā . [^129-2] sī. Nikkhamanañca [^129-3] ma. Nādeyyesu dadaṃ ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 130 -->
<a id="page-130"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 130]*

[^130-3] ekarājajātakaṃ    
[510] anuttare kāmaguṇe samiddhe    
bhutvāna pubbe vasi [^130-1] ekarāja    
sodāni dugge narakamhi khitto    
nappajjahe vaṇṇabalaṃ purāṇaṃ .

[511] pubbeva khantī ca tapo ca mayhaṃ    
sampatthitā dubbhisena [^130-2] ahosi    
tandāni laddhāna kathannu rāja    
jahe ahaṃ vaṇṇabalaṃ purāṇaṃ .

[512] sabbā kireva [^130-3] pariniṭṭhitāni    
yasassinaṃ paññavantaṃ visayha    
yaso ca laddhā purimaṃ uḷāraṃ    
nappajjahe vaṇṇabalaṃ purāṇaṃ .

[513] panujja dukkhena sukhaṃ janinda    
sukhena vā dukkhamasayhasāhi    
ubhayattha santo abhinibbutattā    
sukhe ca dukkhe ca bhavanti tulyāti .

Ekarājajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 131 -->
<a id="page-131"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 131]*

[^131-4] daddarajātakaṃ    
[514] imāni maṃ daddara tāpayanti    
vācāduruttāni manussaloke    
maṇḍūkabhakkhā udakantasevī    
asīvisaṃ [^131-1] maṃ avisā sapanti .

[515] sakā raṭṭhā pabbājito       aññaṃ janapadaṃ gato    
mahantaṃ koṭṭhaṃ kayirātha        duruttānaṃ nidhetave .

[516] yattha posaṃ na jānanti        jātiyā vinayena vā    
na tattha mānaṃ kayirātha        vasaṃ aññātake jane .

[517] videsavāsaṃ vasato           jātavedasamenapi    
khamitabbaṃ sapaññena           api dāsassa tajjitanti .

Daddarajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^131-5] sīlavīmaṃsajātakaṃ    
[518] natthi loke raho nāma       pāpakammaṃ pakubbato    
passanti vanabhūtāni           taṃ bālo maññate [^131-2] raho .

[519] ahaṃ raho na passāmi         suññaṃ vāpi na vijjati    
yattha suññaṃ [^131-3] na passāmi     asuññaṃ hoti taṃ mayā .

[520] dujjacco ca sujacco [^131-4] ca    nando ca sukhavacchano [^131-5]    
#[^131-1] ma. Āsīvisaṃ . [^131-2] ma. Maññatī . [^131-3] ma. Aññaṃ . [^131-4] yu. Ajacco .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 132 -->
<a id="page-132"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 132]*

Vajjho [^132-1] addhuvasīlo ca      te dhammaṃ jahumatthikā .

[521] brāhmaṇo ca kathaṃ jahe       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
yo dhammaṃ anupāleti         dhitimā saccanikkamoti .

Sīlavīmaṃsajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^132-6] sujātājātakaṃ    
[522] kiṃ aṇḍakā ime deva        nikkhittā kaṃsamallake    
upalohitakā vaggū           taṃ [^132-2] me akkhāhi pucchito .

[523] yāni pure tuvaṃ devi         bhaṇḍu nantakavāsinī    
ucchaṅgahatthā pacināsi        tassā te koliyaṃ phalaṃ .

[524] udayhate [^132-3] na ramati        bhogā vippajahanti taṃ    
tatthevimaṃ paṭinetha           yattha kolaṃ pacissati .

[525] honti hete mahārāja       iddhippattāya nāriyā    
khama deva sujātāya          māssā kujjha rathesabhāti .

Sujātājātakaṃ  chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^132-7] palāsajātakaṃ  
[526] acetanaṃ brāhmaṇa assuṇantaṃ  
jāno ajānantamimaṃ palāsaṃ  
āraddhavīriyo dhuvamappamatto  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 133 -->
<a id="page-133"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 133]*

Sukhaseyyaṃ pucchasi kissa hetu .

[527] dūre suto ceva brahā ca rukkho    
dese ṭhito bhūtanivāsarūpo    
tasmā namassāmi imaṃ palāsaṃ    
ye cettha bhūtā te [^133-1] dhanassa hetu .

[528] so te karissāmi yathānubhāvaṃ    
kataññutaṃ brāhmaṇa pekkhamāno    
kathaṃ hi āgamma sataṃ sakāse    
moghā [^133-2] te assu pariphanditāni .

[529] yo tindurukkhassa [^133-3] paro pilakkho [^133-4]    
parivārito pubbayañño uḷāro    
tasseva mūlasmi nidhi nikhāto    
adāyādo gaccha naṃ uddharāhīti .

Palāsajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^133-8] javasakuṇajātakaṃ [^133-5]    
[530] akaramhāva [^133-6] te kiccaṃ      yaṃ balaṃ ahuvamhase    
migarāja namo tyatthu         api kiñci labhāmase .

[531] mama lohitabhakkhassa           niccaṃ luddāni kubbato    
dantantaragato santo         taṃ bahuṃ yampi jīvasi .

#[^133-1] sī. Yu. Te ca . [^133-2] ma. Moghāni . [^133-3] ma. Tindukarukkhassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 134 -->
<a id="page-134"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 134]*

[532] akataññumakattāraṃ            katassa appaṭikārakaṃ    
yasmiṃ kataññutā natthi         niratthā tassa sevanā .

[533] yattha [^134-1] sammukhaciṇṇena       mittadhammo na labbhati    
anussūyamanakkosaṃ [^134-2]         saṇikaṃ tamhā apakkameti .

Javasakuṇajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^134-9] chavajātakaṃ [^134-3]    
[534] sabbaṃ idañca marikataṃ [^134-4]       ubho dhammaṃ na passare    
ubho pakatiyā cutā          yo cāyaṃ mante jhāpeti    
yo ca mantaṃ adhīyati .

[535] sālīnaṃ odanaṃ bhuñje         suciṃ maṃsūpasecanaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na sevāmi       dhammaṃ isībhi sevitaṃ .

[536] paribbaja mahā loko         pacantaññepi pāṇino    
mā tvaṃ [^134-5] adhammo ācarito  tasmā kumbhamivābhidā .

[537] dhiratthu taṃ yasalābhaṃ           dhanalābhañca brāhmaṇa    
yā vutti vinipātena         adhammacaraṇena vāti .

Chavajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

#[^134-1] ma. Yassa . [^134-2] ma. Anusūya -- . Sī. Yu. Anusuyya __ . [^134-3] chavakajātakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 135 -->
<a id="page-135"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 135]*

10 sayhajātakaṃ [^135-1]    
[538] sasamuddapariyāyaṃ             mahiṃ sāgarakuṇḍalaṃ    
na icche saha nindāya        evaṃ sayha [^135-2] vijānahi .

[539] dhiratthu taṃ yasalābhaṃ           dhanalābhañca brāhmaṇa    
yā vutti vinipātena         adhammacaraṇena vā .

[540] api ce pattamādāya         anāgāro paribbaje    
sāeva jīvikā seyyo       yā cādhammena esanā .

[541] api ce pattamādāya         anāgāro paribbaje    
aññaṃ ahiṃsayaṃ loke        api rajjena taṃ varanti .

Sayhajātakaṃ dasamaṃ    
kāliṅgavaggo [^135-3] paṭhamo .

tassuddānaṃ    
vivaraṃ ca adeyya samiddhavaraṃ    
atha daddara pāpamahātiraho    
atha koli palāsavarañcakara    
carimaṃ sasamuddavarena dasa .

[^135-2] pucimandavaggo    
[^135-1] pucimandajātakaṃ    
[542] uṭṭhehi cora kiṃ sesi        ko attho supanena [^135-4] te    
#[^135-1] ma. Seyyajātakaṃ . [^135-2] ma. Seyya . [^135-3] sī. Yu. Vivaravaggo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 136 -->
<a id="page-136"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 136]*

Mā taṃ gahesu [^136-1] rājāno    gāme kibbisakārakaṃ .

[543] nanu [^136-2] coraṃ gahessanti      gāme kibbisakārakaṃ    
kiṃ tattha pucimandassa          vane jātassa tiṭṭhato .

[544] na tvaṃ assattha jānāsi       mama corassa cantaraṃ    
coraṃ gahetvā rājāno      gāme kibbisakārakaṃ    
accenti [^136-3] nimbasūlasmiṃ      tasmiṃ me saṅkate mano .

[545] saṅkeyya saṅkitabbāni        rakkheyyānāgataṃ bhayaṃ    
anāgatabhayā dhīro           ubho loke avekkhatīti .

Pucimandajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^136-2] kassapamandiyajātakaṃ    
[546] api kassapa mandiyā          yuvā sapati hanti vā    
sabbantaṃ khamate dhīro         paṇḍito taṃ titikkhati .

[547] sacepi santo vivadanti        khippaṃ sandhiyare [^136-4] puna    
bālā pattāva bhijjanti       na te samathamajjhagū .

[548] ete bhiyyo samāyanti       sandhi tesaṃ na jīrati    
yo cādhipannaṃ jānāti        yo ca jānāti desanaṃ .

[549] eso hi uttaritaro         bhārāvaho dhurandharo [^136-5]    
#[^136-1] ma. Gahesuṃ . [^136-2] ma. Yaṃ nu . [^136-3] ma. Appenti . [^136-4] ma. Sandhīyare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 137 -->
<a id="page-137"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 137]*

Yo paresādhipannānaṃ         sayaṃ sandhātumarahatīti .

Kassapamandiyajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^137-3] khantivādijātakaṃ    
[550] yo te hatthe ca pāde ca    kaṇṇanāsañca chedayi    
tassa kujjha mahāvīra          mā raṭṭhaṃ vinassa [^137-1] idaṃ .

[551] yo me hatthe ca pāde ca    kaṇṇanāsañca chedayi    
ciraṃ jīvatu so rājā         nahi kujjhanti mādisā .

[552] ahu atītamaddhāne [^137-2]        samaṇo khantidīpano    
taṃ khantiyāyeva ṭhitaṃ          kāsirājā achedayi .

[553] tassa kammapharusassa [^137-3]        vipāko kaṭuko ahu    
yaṃ kāsirājā vedesi        nirayamhi samappitoti .

Khantivādijātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^137-4] lohakumbhijātakaṃ    
[554] dujjīvitamajīvimha             yesanno na dadāmase [^137-4]    
vijjamānesu bhogesu         dīpaṃ nākamha attano .

[555] saṭṭhiṃ [^137-5] vassasahassāni       paripuṇṇāni sabbaso    
niraye paccamānānaṃ          kadā anto bhavissati .

[556] natthi anto kuto anto      na anto paṭidissati    
#[^137-1] ma. Vinasā . [^137-2] ma. Atītamaddhānaṃ . [^137-3] sī. Yu. Kammassa . [^137-4] ma. Ye sante na dadamhase .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 138 -->
<a id="page-138"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 138]*

Tadāhi pakataṃ pāpaṃ           mama tuyhañca mārisa [^138-1] .

[557] sohaṃ nūna ito gantvā       yoniṃ laddhāna mānusiṃ    
vadaññū sīlasampanno          kāhāmi kusalaṃ bahunti .

Lohakumbhijātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^138-5] maṃsajātakaṃ [^138-2]    
[558] pharusā vata te vācā        maṃsaṃ yācanako asi    
kilomasadisī vācā           kilomaṃ samma dammi te .

[559] aṅgametaṃ manussānaṃ          bhātā loke pavuccati    
aṅgassa sadisī vācā         aṅgaṃ samma dadāmi te .

[560] tātāti putto vadamāno      kampeti hadayaṃ pitu    
hadayassa sadisī vācā         hadayaṃ samma dammi te .

[561] yassa gāme sakhā natthi       yathāraññaṃ tatheva taṃ    
sabbassa sadisī vācā         sabbaṃ samma dadāmi teti .

Maṃsajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^138-6] sasapaṇḍitajātakaṃ    
[562] satta me rohitā macchā      udakā thalamubbhatā    
idaṃ brāhmaṇa me atthi       etaṃ bhutvā vane vasa .

[563] dussa me khettapālassa       rattiṃ bhattaṃ apābhataṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 139 -->
<a id="page-139"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 139]*

Maṃsasūlā ca dve godhā       ekañca dadhivārakaṃ    
idaṃ brāhmaṇa me atthi       etaṃ bhutvā vane vasa .

[564] ambapakkaṃ [^139-1] dakaṃ sītaṃ        sītacchāyaṃ [^139-2] manoramaṃ    
idaṃ brāhmaṇa me atthi       etaṃ bhutvā vane vasa .

[565] na sasassa tilā atthi         na muggā napi taṇḍulā    
iminā agginā pakkaṃ         mamaṃ bhutvā vane vasāti .

Sasapaṇḍitajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^139-7] matarodanajātakaṃ    
[566] matamatameva [^139-3] rodatha        na hi taṃ rodatha yo marissati    
sabbeva [^139-4] sarīradhārino      anupubbena jahanti jīvitaṃ .

[567] devā manussā catuppadā      pakkhigaṇā uragā ca bhogino    
yamhi [^139-5] sarīre anissarā     ramamānāva jahanti jīvitaṃ .

[568] evaṃ calitaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ          sukhadukkhaṃ manujesu apekkhiyaṃ [^139-6]    
kanditaṃ ruṇataṃ [^139-7] niratthakaṃ      kiṃ vo sokagaṇābhikīrare .

[569] dhuttā soṇḍā ca akatā      bālā sūrā [8] ayogino    
dhīraṃ maññanti bāloti         ye dhammassa akovidāti .

Matarodanajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

#[^139-1] sī. Yu. Ambapakkodakaṃ . [^139-2] ma. Sītacchāyā manoramā . [^139-3] ma. Mataṃ mataṃ eva .

#[^139-4] ma. Sabbepi . [^139-5] yu. Asmiṃ . [^139-6] ma. Manujesvapekkhiya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 140 -->
<a id="page-140"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 140]*

[^140-8] kaṇaverajātakaṃ    
[570] yantaṃ vasantasamaye           kaṇaveresu bhāṇusu    
sāmaṃ bāhāya pīḷesi         sā taṃ ārogyamabravi .

[571] ambho na kira saddheyyaṃ       yaṃ vāto pabbataṃ vahe    
pabbatañce vahe vāto       sabbampi paṭhaviṃ vahe    
yattha sāmā kālakatā        sāmaṃ ārogyamabravi .

[572] na ceva sā kālakatā        na ca sā aññamicchati    
ekabhattā [^140-1] kira sāmā     tameva abhikaṅkhati .

[573] asanthutaṃ maṃ cirasanthutena    
nimini sāmā adhuvaṃ dhuvena    
mayāpi sāmā nimineyya aññaṃ    
ito ahaṃ dūrataraṃ gamissanti .

Kaṇaverajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^140-9] tittirajātakaṃ    
[574] susukhaṃ vata jiṃvāmi            labhāmi ceva bhuñjituṃ    
paripantheva tiṭṭhāmi          kā nu bhante gatī mama .

[575] mano ce te nappaṇamati       pakkhi pāpassa kammuno    
avyāvaṭassa bhadrassa         na pāpamupalimpati .

[576] ñātako no nisinnoti        bahu āgacchate jano  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 141 -->
<a id="page-141"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 141]*

Paṭicca kammaṃ phusati           tasmiṃ me saṅkate mano .

[577] na paṭicca kammaṃ phusati         mano ce nappadussati    
appossukkassa bhadrassa       na pāpamupalimpatīti .

Tittirajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 succajajātakaṃ    
[578] succajaṃ vata naccaji           vācāya adadaṃ giriṃ    
kiñci [^141-1] tassa cajantassa      vācāya adadaṃ [^141-2] pabbataṃ .

[579] yañhi kayirā tañhi vade       yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vade    
akarontaṃ bhāsamānaṃ          parijānanti paṇḍitā .

[580] rājaputta namo tyatthu        sacce dhamme ṭhitovasi [^141-3]    
yassa te byasanaṃ patto       saccasmiṃ ramate mano .

[581] yā daliddī daliddassa         aḍḍhā aḍḍhassa kittimā [^141-4]    
sā hissa paramā bhariyā       sahiraññassa itthiyoti .

Succajajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Pucimandavaggo dutiyo .

tassuddānaṃ  
atha cora sakassapa khantivaro  
dujjīvitatā ca varā pharusā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 142 -->
<a id="page-142"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 142]*

Atha suṃ mataṃ ca vasanta sukhaṃ    
succajaṃvatanaccajinā ca dasa .

[^142-3] kuṭidūsakavaggo    
[^142-1] kuṭidūsakajātakaṃ    
[582] manussasseva te sīsaṃ         hatthapādā ca vānara    
atha kena nu vaṇṇena         agārante na vijjati .

[583] manussasseva me sīsaṃ         hatthapādā ca siṅgila    
yāhu seṭṭhā manussesu       sā me paññā na vijjati .

[584] anavaṭṭhitacittassa            lahucittassa dubbhino [^142-1]    
niccaṃ addhuvasīlassa           sukhabhāvo [^142-2] na vijjati .

[585] so karassu ānubhāvaṃ         vītivattassu sīliyaṃ    
sītavātaparittāṇaṃ            karassu kuṭikaṃ [^142-3] kapīti .

Kuṭidūsakajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^142-2] duddubhāyajātakaṃ    
[586] duddubhāyati [^142-4] bhaddante      yasmiṃ dese vasāmahaṃ    
ahampetaṃ na jānāmi         kimetaṃ duddubhāyati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 143 -->
<a id="page-143"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 143]*

[587] veluvaṃ patitaṃ sutvā          duddubhanti saso javi    
sasassa vacanaṃ sutvā          santattā migavāhinī .

[588] appatvā padaviññāṇaṃ         paraghosānusārino    
pamādaparamā [^143-1] bālā       te honti parapattiyā .

[589] ye ca sīlena sampannā       paññāyūpasame ratā    
ārakā viratā dhīrā         na honti parapattiyāti .

Duddubhāyajātakaṃ [^143-2] dutiyaṃ .

[^143-3] brahmadattajātakaṃ    
[590] dvayaṃ yācanako rāja         brahmadatta nigacchati    
alābhaṃ dhanalābhaṃ vā          evaṃdhammā hi yācanā .

[591] yācanaṃ rodanaṃ āhu          pañcālānaṃ rathesabha    
yo yācanaṃ paccakkhāti        tamāhu paṭirodanaṃ .

[592] maṃ māddasaṃsu [^143-3] rodantaṃ      pañcālā susamāgatā    
tuvaṃ vā paṭirodantaṃ          tasmā icchāmahaṃ raho .

[593] dadāmi te brāhmaṇa rohiṇīnaṃ    
gavaṃ sahassaṃ saha puṅgavena    
ariyo hi ariyassa kathaṃ na dajjā    
sutvāna gāthā tava dhammayuttāti .

Brahmadattajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 144 -->
<a id="page-144"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 144]*

[^144-4] cammasāṭakajātakaṃ    
[594] kalyāṇarūpo vatāyaṃ catuppado    
subhaddako ceva supesalo ca    
yo brāhmaṇaṃ jātimantūpapannaṃ    
apacāyati meṇḍavaro yasassī .

[595] mā brāhmaṇa ittaradassanena    
vissāsamāpajji catuppadassa    
daḷhappahāraṃ abhikattukāmo [^144-1]    
avasakkati [^144-2] dassati suppahāraṃ .

[596] uruṭṭhī bhaggā patito [^144-3] khāribhāro    
sabbaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇasseva [^144-4] bhinnaṃ    
ubhopi bāhā paggayheva [^144-5] kandati    
abhidhāvataṃ haññati brahmacāriṃ [^144-6] .

[597] evaṃ so nihato seti        yo apūjaṃ pasaṃsati [^144-7]    
yathāhamajja pahato           hato meṇḍena dummatīti .

Cammasāṭakajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

#[^144-1] sī. Ma. Abhikaṅkhamāno . [^144-2] ma. Avasakkatī . [^144-3] ma. Ūruṭṭhi bhaggaṃ pavaṭṭito .

#[^144-4] ma. Sabbañca bhaṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇassa ... . [^144-5] ma. Paggayha . [^144-6] sī. Ma. Abhidhāvatha  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 145 -->
<a id="page-145"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 145]*

[^145-5] godhajātakaṃ [^145-1]    
[598] samaṇantaṃ maññamāno          upagañchiṃ asaññataṃ    
so maṃ daṇḍena pāhāsi       yathā assamaṇo tathā .

[599] kinte jaṭāhi dummedha        kinte ajinasāṭiyā    
abbhantarante gahanaṃ          bāhiraṃ parimajjasi .

[600] ehi godha nivattassu         bhuñja sālīnamodanaṃ    
telaṃ loṇañca me atthi       pahutaṃ [^145-2] mayhaṃ pipphali .

[601] esa bhiyyo pavekkhāmi       vammikaṃ sataporisaṃ    
telaṃ loṇañca kintesi [^145-3]     ahitaṃ mayhaṃ pipphalīti .

Godhajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^145-6] kakkārujātakaṃ    
[602] kāyena yo nāvahare        vācāya na musā bhaṇe    
yaso laddhā na majjeyya      save kakkārumarahati .

[603] dhammena vittameseyya        na nikatyā dhanaṃ hare    
bhoge laddhā na majjeyya     save kakkārumarahati .

[604] yassa cittaṃ ahāliddaṃ         saddhā ca avirāginī    
eko sāduṃ na bhuñjeyya      save kakkārumarahati .

[605] sammukhā vā tirokkhā [^145-4] vā yo sante na paribhāsati  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 146 -->
<a id="page-146"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 146]*

Yathāvādī tathākārī          save kakkārumarahatīti .

Kakkārujātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^146-7] kākātijātakaṃ [^146-1]    
[606] vāti cāyaṃ tato gandho       yattha me vasatī piyā    
dūre ito hi kākāti [^146-2]     yattha me nirato mano .

[607] kathaṃ samuddamatari             kathaṃ atari kepukaṃ [^146-3]    
kathaṃ satta samuddāni          kathaṃ simbalimāruhi .

[608] tayā samuddamatariṃ            tayā atari kepukaṃ [^146-4]    
tayā satta samuddāni         tayā simbalimāruhiṃ .

[609] dhiratthu maṃ mahākāyaṃ          dhiratthu maṃ acetanaṃ    
yattha jāyāyahaṃ jāraṃ         āvahāmi vahāmi cāti .

Kākātijātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^146-8] ananusociyajātakaṃ    
[610] bahūnaṃ vijjatī bhotī           tehi me kiṃ bhavissati    
tasmā etaṃ na socāmi       piyaṃ sammillahāsiniṃ .

[611] taṃ tañce anusoceyya        yaṃ yaṃ tassa na vijjati    
attānamanusoceyya          sadā maccuvasaṃ pattaṃ [^146-5] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 147 -->
<a id="page-147"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 147]*

[612] na heva tiṭṭhaṃ nāsīnaṃ         na sayānaṃ na patthaguṃ [^147-1]    
yāvuppatti nimissati [^147-2]       tatrāpi saratī [^147-3] vayo .

[613] tatthattani vatappanthe [^147-4]      vinābhāve asaṃsaye    
bhūtaṃ sesaṃ dayitabbaṃ           cavitaṃ [^147-5] ananusociyanti .

Ananusociyajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^147-9] kāḷabāhujātakaṃ    
[614] yaṃ annapānassa pure labhāma    
tandāni sākhamigameva gacchati    
gacchāmadāni vanameva rādha    
asakkatāvamha dhanañjayāya .

[615] lābho alābho yaso ayaso ca    
nindā pasaṃsā ca sukhaṃ ca dukkhaṃ    
ete aniccā manujesu dhammā    
mā soci kiṃ socasi poṭṭhapāda .

[616] addhā tuvaṃ paṇḍitakosi rādha    
jānāsi atthāni anāgatāni    
#[^147-1] sī. Ma. Paddhaguṃ . [^147-2] sī. Yāva pāti nimisati . Ma. Yāva byāti nimisati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 148 -->
<a id="page-148"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 148]*

Kataṃ nukho sākhamigaṃ dakkhāma [^148-1]    
niddhāpitaṃ [^148-2] rājakulatova jammaṃ .

[617] cāleti kaṇṇaṃ bhakuṭiṃ karoti    
muhuṃ muhuṃ bhāyate [^148-3] kumāre    
sayameva taṃ kāhati kāḷabāhu    
yenārakā ṭhassati annapānāti .

Kāḷabāhujātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 sīlavīmaṃsajātakaṃ    
[618] sīlaṃ kireva kalyāṇaṃ          sīlaṃ loke anuttaraṃ    
passa ghoraviso nāgo        sīlavāti na haññati .

[619] yāvadevassahū kiñci          tāvadeva akhādisuṃ    
saṅgamma kulalā loke        na hiṃsanti akiñcanaṃ .

[620] sukhaṃ nirāsā supati           āsā phalavatī sukhā    
āsaṃ nirāsaṃ katvāna         sukhaṃ supati piṅgalā .

[621] na samādhiparo atthi          asmiṃ loke paramhi ca    
na paraṃ nāpi attānaṃ         vihiṃsati samāhitoti .

Sīlavīmaṃsajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Kuṭidūsakavaggo tatiyo .

#[^148-1] ma. Dakkhisāma . [^148-2] sī. Yu. Nibbāpitaṃ . Ma. Niddhāvitaṃ . [^148-3] sī. Bhāsayate .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 149 -->
<a id="page-149"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 149]*

Tassuddānaṃ    
samanussa saduddu sayācanako    
atha meṇḍavaruttama godhavaro    
atha kāya sakepuka bhotīvaro    
atha rādha susīlavarena dasa .

[^149-4] kokilavaggo    
[^149-1] kokālikajātakaṃ [^149-1]    
[622] yo ve kāle asampatte     ativelaṃ pabhāsati    
evaṃ so nihato seti        kokilāyeva [^149-2] atrajo .

[623] na hi satthaṃ sunisitaṃ           visaṃ halāhalammiva [^149-3]    
evaṃ nikkaḍhe [^149-4] pāteti     vācā dubbhāsitā yathā .

[624] tasmā kāle akāle ca [^149-5]   vācaṃ rakkheyya paṇḍito    
nātivelaṃ pabhāseyya         api attasamamhi vā .

[625] yo ca kāle mitaṃ bhāse      matipubbo vicakkhaṇo    
sabbe amitte ādeti       supaṇṇo uragammivāti [^149-6] .

Kokālikajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^149-2] rathalaṭṭhijātakaṃ    
[626] api hantvā hato brūti       jetvā jitoti bhāsati    
#[^149-1] ma. Kokilajātakaṃ . [^149-2] ma. Kokilāyiva . [^149-3] ma. Halāhalāmiva . [^149-4] ma. Nikaṭṭhe .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 150 -->
<a id="page-150"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 150]*

Pubbamakkhāyino [^150-1] rāja      etadatthuṃ [^150-2] na saddahe .

[627] tasmā paṇḍitajātiyo         suṇeyya itarassapi    
ubhinnaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā          yathā dhammo tathā kare .

[628] alaso gihī kāmabhogī na sādhu    
asaññato pabbajito na sādhu    
rājā na sādhu anisammakārī    
yo paṇḍito kodhano taṃ na sādhu .

[629] nisamma khattiyo kayirā        nānisamma disampati    
nisammakārino rañño [^150-3]      yaso kittī ca vaḍḍhatīti .

Rathalaṭṭhijātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^150-3] godhajātakaṃ [^150-4]    
[630] tadeva me tvaṃ vidito        vanamajjhe rathesabho [^150-5]    
yassa te khaggabandhassa [^150-6]     sannaddhassa tirīṭino    
assatthadumasākhāya           pakkā godhā palāyatha .

[631] name namantassa bhaje bhajantaṃ    
kiccānukubbassa kareyya kiccaṃ    
nānatthakāmassa kareyya atthaṃ    
asambhajantampi na sambhajeyya .

#[^150-1] ma. Pubbavakkhāyino . [^150-2] sī. Yu. Ekadatthu . Ma. Aññadatthu . [^150-3] sī. Ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 151 -->
<a id="page-151"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 151]*

[632] caje cajantaṃ vanathaṃ na kayirā    
apetacittena na sambhajeyya    
dijo dumaṃ khīṇaphalaṃva [^151-1] ñatvā    
aññaṃ samekkheyya mahā hi loko .

[633] so te karissāmi yathānubhāvaṃ    
kataññutaṃ khattiyo [^151-2] pekkhamā no    
sabbañca te issariyaṃ dadāmi    
yassicchasī tassa tuvaṃ dadāmīti .

Godhajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^151-4] rājovādajātakaṃ    
[634] gavañce taramānānaṃ          jimhaṃ gacchati puṅgavo    
sabbā gāvī jimhaṃ yanti [^151-3]    nette jimhaṃ gate sati .

[635] evameva manussesu          yo hoti seṭṭhasammato    
so ce adhammaṃ carati         pageva itarā pajā    
sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṃ seti        rājā ce hoti adhammiko .

[636] gavañce taramānānaṃ          ujuṃ gacchati puṅgavo    
sabbā gāvī ujuṃ yanti [^151-4]     nette ujuṃ gate sati .

[637] evameva manussesu          yo hoti seṭṭhasammato    
so cepi [^151-5] dhammaṃ carati      pageva itarā pajā    
#[^151-1] ma. Khīṇaphalanti . [^151-2] ma. Khattiye . [^151-3] ma. Sabbā tā jimhaṃ gacchanti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 152 -->
<a id="page-152"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 152]*

Sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ sukhaṃ seti         rājā ce hoti dhammikoti .

Rājovādajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^152-5] jambukajātakaṃ    
[638] brahā pavaḍḍhakāyo so       dīghadāṭhova jambuka    
na tvaṃ tamhi kule jāto      yattha gaṇhanti kuñjaraṃ .

[639] asīho sīhamānena           yo attānaṃ vikubbati    
kutthuva [^152-1] gajamāsajja        seti bhūmyā anutthunaṃ .

[640] yasassino uttamapuggalassa    
sañjātakhandhassa mahabbalassa    
asamekkhiya kāmabalūpapattiṃ    
sa seti nāgena hatoyaṃ jambuko .

[641] yo cīdha kammaṃ kurute pamāya    
thāmabalaṃ attani saṃviditvā    
jappena mantena subhāsitena    
parikkhavā so vipulaṃ jinātīti .

Jambukajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^152-6] brahāchattajātakaṃ  
[642] tiṇaṃ tiṇanti lapasi            ko nu te tiṇamāhari  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 153 -->
<a id="page-153"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 153]*

Kinnu te tiṇakiccatthi         tiṇameva pabhāsati .

[643] idhāgamā brahmacārī         brahāchatto bahussuto    
so me sabbaṃ samādāya       tiṇaṃ nikkhippa gacchati .

[644] evetaṃ hoti kattabbaṃ        appena bahumicchatā    
sabbaṃ sakassa ādānaṃ         anādānaṃ tiṇassa ca    
tiṇassa cāṭīsu gato          tattha kā paridevanā [^153-1] .

[645] sīlavanto na kubbanti         bālo sīlāni kubbati    
aniccasīlaṃ dussīlyaṃ [^153-2]        kiṃ paṇḍiccaṃ karissatīti .

Brahāchattajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^153-7] pīṭhajātakaṃ    
[646] na te pīṭhamadāsimha [^153-3]       na pānaṃ napi bhojanaṃ    
brahmacārī khamassu me        etaṃ passāmi accayaṃ .

[647] nevābhisajjāmi nacāpi kuppe    
nacāpi me appiyamāsi kiñci    
athopi me āsi manovitakko    
etādiso nūna kulassa dhammo .

[648] esasmākaṃ kule dhammo       pitupitāmaho sadā    
āsanaṃ udakaṃ pajjaṃ           sabbetaṃ nipadāmase .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 154 -->
<a id="page-154"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 154]*

[649] esasmākaṃ kule dhammo       pitupitāmaho sadā    
sakkaccaṃ upatiṭṭhāma          uttamaṃ viya ñātakanti .

Pīṭhajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^154-8] thusajātakaṃ    
[650] viditaṃ thusaṃ undurānaṃ          viditaṃ pana taṇḍulaṃ    
thusaṃ thusaṃ [^154-1] vivajjitvā       taṇḍulaṃ pana khādare .

[651] yā mantanā araññasmiṃ        yā ca gāme nikaṇṇikā    
yañcetaṃ iti cintī ca [^154-2]      etampi viditaṃ mayā .

[652] dhammena kira jātassa         pitā puttassa makkaṭo    
daharasseva santassa          dantehi phalamacchidā .

[653] yametaṃ parisappasi            ajakāṇova sāsape    
yopāyaṃ heṭṭhato seti [^154-3]    etampi viditaṃ mayāti .

Thusajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^154-9] bāverujātakaṃ    
[654] adassanena morassa          sikhino mañjubhāṇino    
kākaṃ tattha apūjesuṃ          maṃsena ca phalena ca .

[655] yadā ca sarasampanno         moro bāverumāgamā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 155 -->
<a id="page-155"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 155]*

Atha lābho ca sakkāro       vāyasassa ahāyatha .

[656] yāva nuppajjatī buddho        dhammarājā pabhaṅkaro    
tāva aññe apūjesuṃ         puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇe .

[657] yadā ca sarasampanno         buddho dhammaṃ adesayi    
atha lābho ca sakkāro       titthiyānaṃ ahāyathāti .

Bāverujātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 visayhajātakaṃ    
[658] adāsi dānāni pure visayha    
dadato ca te khayadhammo ahosi    
ito parañce na dadeyya dānaṃ    
tiṭṭheyyuṃ te saṃyamantassa bhogā .

[659] anariyamariyena sahassanetta    
suduggatenāpi akiccamāhu    
mā vo dhanaṃ taṃ ahu vā janinda [^155-1]    
yaṃ bhogahetu vijahemu saddhaṃ .

[660] yena eko ratho yāti       yāti tena paro [^155-2] ratho    
porāṇaṃ nihitaṃ vattaṃ          vattataññeva vāsava [^155-3] .

#[^155-1] ma. Ahu devarāja . [^155-2] ma. Tenāparo . [^155-3] sī. Porāṇaṃ nihitaṃ vaḍḍhaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 156 -->
<a id="page-156"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 156]*

[661] yadi hessati dassāma         asante kiṃ dadāmhase    
evaṃ bhūtāpi dassāma         mā dānaṃ pamadāmhaseti .

Visayhajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Kokilavaggo catuttho .

tassuddānaṃ    
ativelaṃ pabhāsati [^156-1] jinavaro    
vanamajjhe rathesabha jimhagamo    
atha jambu tiṇāsanapīṭhavaraṃ    
atha taṇḍulaṃ mora visayha dasa .

[^156-5] cullakuṇālavaggo    
[^156-1] kuṇḍalikajātakaṃ [^156-2]    
[662] narānamārāmakarāsu nārīsu    
anekacittāsu aniggahāsu ca    
sabbattanā pītikarāpi ce siyuṃ [^156-3]    
na vissase titthasamā hi nāriyo .

[663] yañca [^156-4] disvā kinnarakinnarīnaṃ [^156-5]    
sabbitthiyo na ramanti agāre    
#[^156-1] ma. Ativelapabhāsati . [^156-2] sī. Kinnarījātakaṃ . Ma. Kaṇḍarījātakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 157 -->
<a id="page-157"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 157]*

Taṃ tādisaṃ sāmikaṃ [^157-1] cajitvā bhariyā    
aññaṃ disvā purisaṃ pīṭhasappiṃ .

[664] bakassa ca bāvarikassa [^157-2] rañño    
accantakāmānugatassa bhariyā    
accācarī baddhavasānugassa [^157-3]    
kiṃ vā [^157-4] itthī nāticare tadaññaṃ .

[665] piyaṅgāni [^157-5] sabbalokissarassa    
rañño piyā brahmadattassa bhariyā    
accācarī baddhavasānugassa [^157-3]    
taṃ vāpi sā nājjhagā kāmakāminīti .

Kuṇḍalikajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^157-2] vānarajātakaṃ    
[666] asakkhiṃ vata attānaṃ          uṭṭhātuṃ udakā thalaṃ    
nadānāhaṃ puna tuyhaṃ          vasaṃ gacchāmi vārija .

[667] alametehi ambehi          jambūhi panasehi ca    
yāni pāraṃ samuddassa         varaṃ mayhaṃ udumbaro .

[668] yo ca uppatitaṃ atthaṃ         na khippamanubujjhati    
#[^157-1] sī. Ma. Maccaṃ . [^157-2] sī. Pāvārikassa . [^157-3] ma. Avācarī paṭṭhavasānugassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 158 -->
<a id="page-158"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 158]*

Amittavasamanveti            pacchā ca anutappati .

[669] yo ca uppatitaṃ atthaṃ         khippameva nibodhati    
muccate sattusambādhā        na ca pacchānutappatīti .

Vānarajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^158-3] kuntinījātakaṃ    
[670] avasimha tavāgāre          niccaṃ sakkatapūjitā    
tvamevadānimakari            handa rāja vajāmahaṃ .

[671] yo ve kate paṭikate        kibbise paṭikibbise    
evantaṃ sammati [^158-1] veraṃ      vasa kuntini mā gamā .

[672] na katassa ca kattā ca        mitti [^158-2] sandhiyate [^158-3] puna    
hadayaṃ nānujānāti           gacchaññeva rathesabha .

[673] katassa ceva kattā ca        mitti [^158-2] sandhiyate [^158-3] puna    
dhīrānaṃ no ca bālānaṃ        vasa kuntini mā gamāti .

Kuntinījātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^158-4] ambajātakaṃ [^158-4]    
[674] yo niliyaṃ [^158-5] maṇḍayati        saṇḍāsena vihaññati    
tassa sā vasamanvetu         yā te ambe avāhari .

[675] vīsaṃ vā pañcavīsaṃ [^158-6] vā      ūnatiṃsaṃva jātiyā    
#[^158-1] ma. Sammatī . [^158-2] ma. Metti . Yu. Mettī . [^158-3] ma. Sandhīyate .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 159 -->
<a id="page-159"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 159]*

Tādisā pati mā laddhā       yā te ambe avāhari .

[676] dīghaṃ gacchatu addhānaṃ          ekikā abhisāriyā [^159-1]    
saṅkete pati māddasa [^159-2]     yā te ambe avāhari .

Alaṅkatā suvasanā           mālinī candanussadā    
[677] ekikā sayane setu         yā te ambe avāharīti .

Ambajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^159-5] gajakumbhajātakaṃ    
[678] vanaṃ yadaggi dahati            pāvako kaṇhavattanī    
kathaṃ karosi pacalaka           evaṃ dandhaparakkamo .

[679] bahūni rukkhachiddāni           paṭhabyā vivarāni ca    
tāni ce nābhisambhoma        hoti no kālapariyāyo .

[680] yo dandhakāle tarati         taraṇīye ca dandhati    
sukkhapaṇṇaṃva akkamma          atthaṃ bhañjati attano .

[681] yo dandhakāle dandheti       taraṇīye ca tārayi    
sasīva rattiṃ vibhajaṃ            tassattho paripūratīti .

Gajakumbhajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 160 -->
<a id="page-160"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 160]*

[^160-6] kesavajātakaṃ    
[682] manussindaṃ jahitvāna          sabbakāmasamiddhinaṃ    
kathaṃ nu bhagavā kesi          kappassa ramati assame .

[683] sādhūni [^160-1] ramaṇīyāni         santi vakkhā [^160-2] manoramā    
subhāsitāni kappassa          nārada ramayanti maṃ .

[684] sālīnaṃ odanaṃ bhuñje         sucimaṃsūpasecanaṃ    
kathaṃ sāmākanivāraṃ [^160-3]        aloṇaṃ chādayanti taṃ .

[685] sādhadha vā yadivāsādhadha [^160-4]      appaṃ vā yadivā bahuṃ    
vissattho yattha bhuñjeyya      vissāsaparamā rasāti .

Kesavajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^160-7] ayakūṭajātakaṃ    
[686] sabbāyasaṃ kūṭamatippamāṇaṃ    
paggayha yo [^160-5] tiṭṭhasi antalikkhe    
rakkhāya maṃ tvaṃ vihitonusajja    
udāhu me vāyamase [^160-6] vadhāya .

[687] dūto ahaṃ rājidha rakkhasānaṃ  
vadhāya tuyhaṃ pahitohamasmi  
#[^160-1] sī. Ma. Sādūni . [^160-2] sī. Rukkhā . [^160-3] ma. Sāmāanīvāraṃ . [^160-4] ma. Sāduṃ vā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 161 -->
<a id="page-161"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 161]*

Indo ca taṃ rakkhati devarājā    
tenuttamaṅgaṃ na te [^161-1] phālayāmi .

[688] sace ca maṃ rakkhati devarājā    
devānamindo maghavā sujampati    
kāmaṃ pisācā vinadantu sabbe    
na santase rakkhasiyā pajāya .

[689] kāmaṃ kaṇḍantu [^161-2] kumbhaṇḍā    sabbe paṃsupisācakā    
nālaṃ pisācā yuddhāya        mahatī sā vibhesikāti [^161-3] .

Ayakūṭajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^161-8] araññajātakaṃ    
[690] araññā gāmamāgamma         kiṃsīlaṃ kiṃvataṃ ahaṃ    
purisaṃ tāta seveyyaṃ         taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[691] yo taṃ vissāsaye tāta       vissāsañca khameyya te    
sussūsī ca titikkhī ca          taṃ bhajehi ito gato .

[692] yassa kāyena vācāya        manasā natthi dukkaṭaṃ    
urasīva patiṭṭhāya            taṃ bhajehi ito gato .

[693] haliddarāgaṃ kapicittaṃ          purisaṃ  rāgavirāginaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 162 -->
<a id="page-162"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 162]*

Tādisaṃ tāta mā sevi        nimmanussampi ce siyāti .

Araññajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^162-9] sandhibhedajātakaṃ    
[694] neva itthīsu sāmaññaṃ         nāpi bhakkhesu sārathi    
athassa sandhibhedassa          passa yāva sucintitaṃ .

[695] asi tikkhova maṃsamhi          pesuññaṃ parivattati    
yatthūsabhañca sīhañca           bhakkhayanti migādhamā .

[696] imaṃ so sayanaṃ seti          yamimaṃ [^162-1] passasi sārathi    
yo vācaṃ sandhibhedassa        pisuṇassa nibodhati .

[697] te janā sukhamedhanti         narā saggagatāriva    
ye vācaṃ sandhibhedassa        nāvabodhanti sārathīti .

Sandhibhedajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 devatāpañhajātakaṃ    
[698] hanti hatthehi pādehi        mukhañca parisumbhati    
save rāja piyo hoti        kantena tvābhipassasi [^162-2] .

[699] akkosati yathākāmaṃ          āgamañcassa necchati [^162-3]    
save rāja piyo hoti        kantena tvābhipassasi [^162-2] .

[700] abbhakkhāti abhūtena          alikenābhisāraye  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 163 -->
<a id="page-163"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 163]*

Save rāja piyo hoti        kantena tvābhipassasi .

[701] haraṃ annañca pānañca         vatthasenāsanāni ca    
(aññadatthuṃ [^163-1] harā santā)    
teve rāja piyā honti      kantena tvābhipassasīti .

Devatāpañhajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Cullakuṇālavaggo pañcamo .

tassuddānaṃ    
narānaṃ asakkhi vasimhavaro    
niliyamaggivarañca puna    
puna rasāyasakūṭavaro    
tathārañña sārathi hanti dasa .

tatra vagguddānaṃ bhavati .

Vivaraṃ [^163-2] pucimandañca       kuṭidūsaṃ bahubhāṇakaṃ [^163-3]    
cullakuṇālavaggo so       pañcamo suppakāsitoti .

Catukkanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 164 -->
<a id="page-164"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 164]*

Pañcakanipātajātakaṃ    
[^164-1] maṇikuṇḍalavaggo    
[^164-1] maṇikuṇḍalajātakaṃ    
[702] jinno raṭṭhassamaṇikuṇḍale ca [^164-1]    
putte ca dāre ca tatheva jinno [^164-2]    
sabbesu bhogesu asesakesu [^164-3]    
kasmā na santappasi sokakāle .

[703] pubbeva maccaṃ vijahanti bhogā    
macco vā te [^164-4] pubbataraṃ jahāti    
asassatā bhogino kāmakāmi    
tasmā na socāmahaṃ sokakāle .

[704] udeti pūreti khīyati [^164-5] cando    
atthaṅgametvāna [^164-6] paleti sūriyo    
vijitā [^164-7] mayā sattuka lokadhammā    
tasmā na socāmahaṃ sokakāle .

[705] alaso gihī kāmabhogī na sādhu    
#[^164-1] sī. Ma. Jīno rathassaṃ maṇikuṇḍale ca . [^164-2] ma. Jīno . [^164-3] sī. Yu. Asesitesu .

#[^164-4] yu. Ca ne . [^164-5] sī. Ma. Āpūreti veti . [^164-6] sī. Ma. Atthaṃ tapetvāna .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 165 -->
<a id="page-165"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 165]*

Asaññato pabbajito na sādhu    
rājā na sādhu anisammakārī    
yo paṇḍito kodhano taṃ na sādhu .

[706] nisamma khattiyo kayirā        nānisamma disampati    
nisammakārino rañño [^165-1]      yaso kittī [^165-2] ca vaḍḍhatīti .

Maṇikuṇḍalajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^165-2] sujātajātakaṃ    
[707] kinnu santaramānova          lāyitvā haritaṃ tiṇaṃ    
khāda khādāti lapasi          gatasantaṃ jaraggavaṃ    
nahi annena pānena         mato goṇo samuṭṭhahe    
tvañca tucchaṃ vilapasi          yathātaṃ dummatī tathā .

[708] tatheva tiṭṭhati sīsaṃ           hatthapādā ca vāladhi    
sotā tatheva tiṭṭhanti        maññe goṇo samuṭṭhahe    
nevayyakassa sīsaṃ vā [^165-3]      hatthapādāva [^165-4] dissare    
rudaṃ mattikathūpasmiṃ            nanu tvaññeva dummati .

[709] ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ         ghatasittaṃva pāvakaṃ    
vārinā viya osiñcaṃ         sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ    
abbūḷhaṃ [^165-5] vata me sallaṃ     sokaṃ hadayanissitaṃ [^165-6]    
#[^165-1] ma. Rāja . [^165-2] ma. Kitti . [^165-3] ma. Sīsañca . [^165-4] ma. Ca . [^165-5] ma. Abbahī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 166 -->
<a id="page-166"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 166]*

Yo me sokaparetassa        pitusokaṃ apānudi .

[710] sohaṃ abbūḷhasallosmi        vītasoko anāvilo    
na socāmi na rodāmi        tava sutvāna māṇava .

[711] evaṃ karonti sappaññā       ye honti anukampakā    
vinivattenti sokamhā        sujāto pitaraṃ yathāti .

Sujātajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^166-3] venasākhajātakaṃ [^166-1]    
[712] nayidaṃ niccaṃ bhavitabbaṃ brahmadatta    
khemaṃ subhikkhaṃ sukhatā [^166-2] ca kāye    
atthaccaye mā ahu sampamūḷho    
bhinnaplavo sāgarasseva majjhe .

[713] yāni karoti puriso          tāni attani passati    
kalyāṇakārī kalyāṇaṃ         pāpakārī ca pāpakaṃ    
yādisaṃ vapate bījaṃ           tādisaṃ ruhate [^166-3] phalaṃ .

[714] idaṃ tadācariyavaco           pārācariyo [^166-4] yadabravi    
māssu [^166-5] tvaṃ akari pāpaṃ     yantaṃ [^166-6] pacchā kataṃ tape .

[715] ayameva so piṅgiyo [^166-7] venasākho [^166-8]    
#[^166-1] sī. Yu. Dhonasākhajātakaṃ . [^166-2] sī. Suhatā . [^166-3] sī. Ma. Harate . [^166-4] sī. Ma. Pārāsariyo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 167 -->
<a id="page-167"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 167]*

Yamhi ghātayiṃ khattiyānaṃ sahassaṃ    
alaṅkate candanasāralitte [^167-1]    
tameva dukkhaṃ paccāgataṃ mamaṃ .

[716] sāmāpi kho candanalittagattī [^167-2]    
siṅgūva [^167-3] sobhañjanakassa uggatā    
adisvā kālaṃ karissāmi ubbariṃ    
taṃ me ito dukkhataraṃ bhavissatīti .

Venasākhajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^167-4] uragajātakaṃ    
[717] uragova tacaṃ jiṇṇaṃ           hitvā gacchati santanana    
evaṃ sarīre nibbhoge        pete kālakate sati    
dayhamāno na jānāti        ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na socāmi       gato so tassa yā gati .

[718] anabbhito [^167-4] tato āgā     nānuññāto [^167-5] ito gato    
yathāgato tathā gato         tattha kā paridevanā    
dayhamāno na jānāti        ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na socāmi       gato so tassa yā gati .

[719] sace rode kīsā [^167-6] assaṃ    tassā me kiṃ phalaṃ siyā    
#[^167-1] ma. Candanasārānulitte . [^167-2] ma. Sāmā ca kho candanalittagattā .

#[^167-3] sī. Ma. Laṭṭhīva . [^167-4] sī. Anavhāto . Ma. Anavhito . [^167-5] ma. Ananuññāto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 168 -->
<a id="page-168"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 168]*

Ñātimittasuhajjānaṃ           bhiyyo no aratī siyā    
dayhamāno na jānāti        ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na socāmi       gato so tassa yā gati .

[720] yathāpi dārako candaṃ         gacchantaṃ anurodati    
evaṃ sampadamevetaṃ          yo petamanusocati    
dayhamāno na jānāti        ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na socāmi       gato so tassa yā gati .

[721] yathāpi udakakumbho           bhinno appaṭisandhiyo    
evaṃ sampadamevetaṃ          yo petamanusocati    
dayhamāno na jānāti        ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na socāmi       gato so tassa yā gatīti .

Uragajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^168-5] dhaṅkajātakaṃ [^168-1]    
[722] aññe socanti rodanti       aññe assumukhā janā    
pasannamukhavaṇṇosi            kasmā ghaṭa na socasi .

[723] nābbhatītaharo soko         nānāgatasukhāvaho    
tasmā dhaṅka na socāmi       natthi soke dutīyatā .

[724] socaṃ paṇḍukiso hoti         bhattañcassa na ruccati  
amittā sumanā honti        sallaviddhassa ruppato .์  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 169 -->
<a id="page-169"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 169]*

[725] gāme vā yadivāraññe       ninne vā yadivā thale    
ṭhitaṃ maṃ nāgamissati [^169-1]        evaṃ diṭṭhapado ahaṃ .

[726] yassattā nālamekova        sabbakāmarasāharo    
sabbāpi paṭhavī tassa          na sukhaṃ āvahissatīti .

Dhaṅkajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^169-6] kārandiyajātakaṃ [^169-2]    
[727] eko araññe girikandarāyaṃ    
paggayha mahantaselaṃ pavejjhasi [^169-3]    
punappunaṃ santaramānarūpo    
kārandiya [^169-4] konu tavayidhattho .

[728] ahañhimaṃ sāgarasevitantaṃ    
samaṃ karissāmi yathāpi pāṇiṃ [^169-5]    
vikīriya sānūni ca pabbatāni [6]    
tasmā selaṃ kandarāyaṃ [^169-7] pakkhipāmi .

[729] nayimaṃ mahiṃ arahati pāṇikappaṃ    
samaṃ manusso karaṇāyameko    
maññāmi maññeva dariṃ jigiṃsaṃ [^169-8]    
#[^169-1] sī. Na maṃ taṃ āgamissati . [^169-2] ma. Koraṇḍiyajātakaṃ . [^169-3] ma. Paggayha paggayha    
# silaṃ pavecchasi . Sī. Selaṃ . [^169-4] ma. Koraṇḍiya . [^169-5] ma. Pāṇi . [^169-6] ma. Ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 170 -->
<a id="page-170"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 170]*

Kārandiya hāyasi [^170-1] jīvalokaṃ .

[730] sace ayaṃ bhūtadharaṃ na sakko [^170-2]    
samaṃ manusso karaṇāyameko    
evameva tvaṃ brahme ime manusse    
nānādiṭṭhike nānayissasi te .

[731] saṅkhittarūpena bhavaṃ mamatthaṃ    
akkhāsi kārandiya [^170-3] evametaṃ    
yathā na sakkā paṭhavī samāyaṃ    
kātuṃ [^170-4] manussena tathā manussāti .

Kārandiyajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^170-7] laṭukikajātakaṃ    
[732] vandāmi taṃ kuñjaraṃ [^170-5] saṭṭhihāyanaṃ    
āraññakaṃ yūthapatiṃ yasassiṃ    
pakkhehi taṃ pañjalikaṃ karomi    
mā me vadhi puttake dubbalāya .

[733] vandāmi taṃ kuñjaraṃ [^170-5] ekacāriṃ    
āraññakaṃ pabbatasānugocaraṃ    
pakkhehi taṃ pañjalikaṃ karomi    
#[^170-1] ma. Koraṇḍiya hāhasi . [^170-2] ma. Sace ahaṃ bhūtadharaṃ na sakkā . [^170-3] ma. Koraṇḍiya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 171 -->
<a id="page-171"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 171]*

Mā me vadhi puttake dubbalāya .

[734] vadhissāmi te laṭukike puttakāni    
kiṃ me tvaṃ kāhasi dubbalāsi    
satasahassānipi tādisīnaṃ    
vāmena pādena pappothayeyyaṃ [^171-1] .

[735] naheva sabbattha balena kiccaṃ    
balaṃ hi bālassa vadhāya hoti    
karissāmi te nāgarāja anatthaṃ    
yo me vadhi puttake dubbalāya .

[736] kākañca passa laṭukikaṃ         maṇḍūkaṃ nīlamakkhikaṃ    
ete nāgaṃ aghātesuṃ        passa verassa verinaṃ    
tasmā hi veraṃ na kayirātha     appiyenapi kenacīti .

Laṭukikajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^171-8] culladhammapālajātakaṃ    
[737] ahameva dūsiyā bhūnahatā       rañño mahāpatāpassa    
etaṃ muñcatu dhammapālaṃ        hatthe me deva chedahi [^171-2] .

[738] ahameva dūsiyā bhūnahatā       rañño mahāpatāpassa    
etaṃ muñcatu dhammapālaṃ        pāde me deva chedahi [^171-2] .

[739] ahameva dūsiyā bhūnahatā       rañño mahāpatāpassa  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 172 -->
<a id="page-172"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 172]*

Etaṃ muñcatu dhammapālaṃ        sīsaṃ me deva chedahi [^172-1] .

[740] naha nūnimassa rañño          mittāmaccā ca vijjare    
suhadā ye na vadanti rājānaṃ   mā ghātayi orasaṃ puttaṃ .

[741] naha nūnimassa rañño          mittā ñātī ca vijjare    
suhadā ye na vadanti rājānaṃ   mā ghātayi atrajaṃ puttaṃ .

[742] candanasārānulittā bāhā     chijjanti dhammapālassa    
dāyādassa paṭhabyā          pāṇā me deva rujjhantīti .

Culladhammapālajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^172-9] suvaṇṇamigajātakaṃ    
[743] vikkama re mahāmiga [^172-2]       vikkama re harīpada [^172-3]    
chinda varattikaṃ [^172-4] pāsaṃ       nāhaṃ ekā vane rame .

[744] vikkamāmi na pāremi         bhūmiṃ sumbhāmi vegasā    
daḷho varattiko [^172-5] pāso    pādaṃ me parikantati .

[745] attharassu palāsāni          asiṃ nibbāha luddaka    
paṭhamaṃ maṃ vadhitvāna           hana pacchā mahāmigaṃ .

[746] na me sutaṃ vā diṭṭhaṃ vā      bhāsantiṃ mānusiṃ migiṃ    
tvañca bhadde sukhī hohi       eso cāpi mahāmigo .

[747] evaṃ luddaka nandassu         saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
#[^172-1] ma. Chedehi . [^172-2] ma. Haripāda . [^172-3] ma. Mahāmiga . [^172-4] ma. Vārattikaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 173 -->
<a id="page-173"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 173]*

Yathāhamajja nandāmi          muttaṃ disvā mahāmiganti .

Suvaṇṇamigajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 susandhījātakaṃ [^173-1]    
[748] vāti gandho timirānaṃ         kusamuddova [^173-2] ghosavā    
dūre ito hi susandhī [^173-3]      tamba kāmā tudanti maṃ .

[749] kathaṃ samuddamatari             kathaṃ addakkhi serumaṃ [^173-4]    
kathaṃ tassā ca tuyhañca        ahu agga [^173-5] samāgamo .

[750] bharukacchā [^173-6] payātānaṃ       vāṇijānaṃ dhanesinaṃva    
maṅkarehi bhidā nāvā        phalakena mahaṇṇavaṃ [^173-7] .

[751] sā maṃ saṇhena mudunā        niccaṃ candanagandhinī    
aṅgena [^173-8] uddharī bhaddā     mātā puttaṃva orasaṃ .

[752] sā maṃ annena pānena       vatthena sayanena ca    
attanāpi ca mandakkhī         evaṃ tamba vijānahīti .

Susandhījātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Maṇikuṇḍalavaggo paṭhamo .

tassuddānaṃ    
atha jinavaro haritaṃ tiṇako .

#[^173-1] ma. Suyonandījātakaṃ . [^173-2] ma. Ca . [^173-3] ma. Suyonandhī . [^173-4] ma. Sadumaṃ .

#[^173-5] ma. Sagga . [^173-6] ma. Kurukacchā . [^173-7] sī. Makarehibbhidā ... . Ma. Makarehi abhidā nāvā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 174 -->
<a id="page-174"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 174]*

Atha bhinnalivo uragova ghaṭo    
dariyā puna kuñjara bhūnahatā    
miga puttamasaggavarena dasa .

[^174-2] vaṇṇārohavaggo    
[^174-1] vaṇṇārohajātakaṃ    
[753] vaṇṇārohena jātiyā        balanikkamanena ca    
subāhu na mayā seyyo       sudāṭho [^174-1] iti bhāsasi .

[754] vaṇṇārohena jātiyā        balanikkamanena ca    
sudāṭho na mayā seyyo      subāhu iti bhāsasi .

[755] evañce maṃ viharantaṃ         subāhu samma dubbhasi    
nadānāhaṃ tayā saddhiṃ         saṃvāsamabhirocaye .

[756] yo paresaṃ vacanāni          saddahetha [^174-2] yathātathaṃ    
khippaṃ bhijjetha mittasmiṃ        verañca pasave bahuṃ .

[757] na so mitto yo sadā appamatto    
bhedāsaṅkī randhamevānupassī    
yasmiñca seti urasīva putto    
save mitto yo abhejjo parebhīti .

Vaṇṇārohajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 175 -->
<a id="page-175"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 175]*

[^175-2] sīlavīmaṃsajātakaṃ    
[758] sīlaṃ seyyo sutaṃ seyyo      iti me saṃsayo ahu    
sīlameva sutā seyyo        iti me natthi saṃsayo .

[759] moghā jāti ca vaṇṇo ca      sīlameva kiruttamaṃ    
sīlena anupetassa           sutenattho na vijjati .

[760] khattiyo ca adhammaṭṭho        vesso cādhammanissito    
te pariccajjubho loke       upapajjanti duggatiṃ .

[761] khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā    suddā caṇḍālapukkusā    
idha dhammaṃ caritvāna          bhavanti tidive samā .

[762] na vedā samparāyāya        na jāti napi [^175-1] bandhavā    
sakañca sīlasaṃsuddhaṃ            samparāyasukhāvahanti [^175-2] .

Sīlavīmaṃsajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^175-3] hirijātakaṃ    
[763] hirintarantaṃ vijigucchamānaṃ    
tavāhamasmi iti bhāsamānaṃ    
seyyāni kammāni anādiyantaṃ    
neso mamanti iti naṃ vijaññā .

[764] yañhi kayirā tañhi vade       yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vade  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 176 -->
<a id="page-176"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 176]*

Akarontaṃ bhāsamānaṃ          parijānanti paṇḍitā .

[765] na so mitto yo sadā appamatto    
bhedāsaṅkī randhamevānupassī    
yasmiñca seti urasīva putto    
save mitto yo abhejjo parebhi .

[766] pāmojjakaraṇaṃ ṭhānaṃ          pasaṃsāvahanaṃ sukhaṃ    
phalānisaṃso bhāveti          vahanto porisaṃ dhuraṃ .

[767] pavivekarasaṃ pitvā           rasaṃ upasamassa ca    
niddaro hoti nippāpo       dhammapītirasaṃ pivanti .

Hirijātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^176-4] khajjopanakajātakaṃ    
[768] konu santamhi pajjote       aggipariyesanaṃ caraṃ    
addakkhi rattiṃ [^176-1] khajjotaṃ     jātavedaṃ amaññatha .

[769] svāssa [^176-2] gomayacuṇṇāni     abhimatthaṃ tiṇāni ca    
viparītāya saññāya           nāsakkhi pajjaletave .

[770] evampi anupāyena          atthaṃ na labhate mūgo [^176-3]    
visāṇato gavaṃ dohaṃ          yattha khīraṃ na vindati .

[771] vividhehi upāyehi           atthaṃ papponti māṇavā    
niggahena amittānaṃ          mittānaṃ paggahena ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 177 -->
<a id="page-177"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 177]*

[772] senīmokkhūpalābhena [^177-1]       vallabhānaṃ nayena ca    
jagatiṃ jagatīpālā [^177-2]         āvasanti vasundharanti .

Khajjopanakajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^177-5] ahituṇḍikajātakaṃ    
[773] dhuttomhi [^177-3] samma sumukha      jūte akkhaparājito    
harehi [^177-4] ambapakkāni       vīriyante bhakkhayāmase .

[774] alikaṃ vata maṃ samma           abhūtena pasaṃsasi    
ko te suto vā diṭṭho vā   sumukho nāma makkaṭo .

[775] ajjāpi me taṃ manasi         yaṃ maṃ tvaṃ ahituṇḍika    
dhaññāpaṇaṃ pavisitvā          matto chātaṃ hanāsi maṃ .

[776] tāhaṃ saraṃ dukkhaseyyaṃ         api rajjampi kāraye    
nevāhaṃ yācito dajjaṃ        tathāhi bhayatajjito .

[777] yañca jaññā kule jātaṃ       gabbhe tittaṃ amacchariṃ    
tena sakhiñca mittañca         dhīro sandhātumarahatīti .

Ahituṇḍikajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^177-6] gumbiyajātakaṃ  
[778] madhuvaṇṇaṃ madhurasaṃ             madhugandhaṃ visaṃ ahu  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 178 -->
<a id="page-178"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 178]*

Gumbiyo ghāsamesāno        araññe odahī visaṃ .

[779] madhu iti maññamānā          ye taṃ visamasāyisuṃ [^178-1]    
tesantaṃ kaṭukaṃ āsi          maraṇaṃ tenupāgamama .

[780] ye ca kho paṭisaṅkhāya        visanti [^178-2] parivajjayuṃ    
te āturesu sukhitā         dayhamānesu nibbutā .

[781] evameva manussesu          visakāmā [^178-3] samohitā    
āmisaṃ bandhanañcetaṃ          maccuvaso [^178-4] guhāsayo .

[782] evameva ime kāme        āturā paricārike    
ye sadā parivajjanti         saṅgaṃ loke upaccagunti .

Gumbiyajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^178-7] sāliyajātakaṃ    
[783] yvāyaṃ sāliyacchāpoti        kaṇhasappaṃ agāhayi    
tena sappenayaṃ daṭṭho        hato pāpānusāsako .

[784] ahantaramahantāraṃ            yo naro hantumicchati    
evaṃ so nihato seti        yathāyaṃ puriso hato .

[785] ahanantamaghātentaṃ [^178-5]        yo naro hantumicchati    
evaṃ so nihato seti        yathāyaṃ puriso hato .

[786] yathā paṃsumuṭṭhi puriso         paṭivātaṃ paṭikkhipe    
#[^178-1] ma. Visamakhādisuṃ . [^178-2] sī. Ma. Visantaṃ . [^178-3] ma. Visaṃ kāmā . [^178-4] ma. Maccuveso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 179 -->
<a id="page-179"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 179]*

Tameva so rajo hanti        yathāyaṃ puriso hato .

[787] yo appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati    
suddhassa posassa anaṅgaṇassa    
tameva bālaṃ pacceti pāpaṃ    
sukhumo rajo paṭivātaṃva khittoti .

Sāliyajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^179-8] tacasārajātakaṃ    
[788] amittahatthatthagatā           tacasārasamappitā    
pasannamukhavaṇṇāttha           kasmā tumhe na socatha .

[789] na socanāya paridevanāya    
attho ca labbhā [^179-1] api appakopi    
socantamenaṃ dukhitaṃ viditvā    
paccatthikā attamanā bhavanti .

[790] yato ca kho paṇḍito āpadāsu    
na vedhati atthavinicchayaññū    
paccatthikāssa dukhitā bhavanti    
disvā mukhaṃ avikāraṃ purāṇaṃ .

[791] jappena mantena subhāsitena  
anuppadānena paveṇiyā vā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 180 -->
<a id="page-180"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 180]*

Yathā yathā yattha labhetha atthaṃ    
tathā tathā tattha parakkameyya .

[792] yato ca jāneyya alabbhaneyyo    
mayā vā aññena vā esa attho    
asocamāno adhivāsayeyya    
kammaṃ daḷhaṃ kinti karomidānīti .

Tacasārajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^180-9] mittavindukajātakaṃ    
[793] kyāhaṃ devānamakaraṃ          kiṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ mayā    
yaṃ me sirasmiṃ ohacca        cakkaṃ bhamati matthake .

[794] atikkamma ramaṇakaṃ            sadāmattañca dūbhakaṃ    
brahmatarañca pāsādaṃ         kenatthena idhāgato .

[795] ito bahutarā bhogā         atra maññe bhavissare    
iti etāya saññāya         passa maṃ byasanaṃ gataṃ .

[796] catubbhi aṭṭhajjhagamā          aṭṭhābhi cāpi [^180-1] soḷasa    
soḷasābhi ca dvattiṃsa         atricchaṃ cakkamāsado    
icchāhatassa posassa         cakkaṃ bhamati matthake .

[797] upari visālā duppūrā [^180-2]     icchāvisadagāminī 3  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 181 -->
<a id="page-181"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 181]*

Ye ca taṃ anugijjhanti         te honti cakkadhārinoti .

Mittavindukajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 palāsajātakaṃ    
[798] haṃso palāsamavaca            nigrodho samma jāyati    
aṅkasmiṃ te nisinnova        so te mammāni chejjati [^181-1] .

[799] vaḍḍhatāmeva nigrodho        patiṭṭhassa bhavāmahaṃ    
yathā pitā ca mātā ca       evameso [^181-2] bhavissati .

[800] yaṃ tvaṃ aṅkasmiṃ vaḍḍhesi       khīrarukkhaṃ bhayānakaṃ    
āmanta kho taṃ gacchāma       vuḍḍhimassa na ruccati .

[801] idāni kho maṃ bhāyati [^181-3]      mahānerunidassanaṃ    
haṃsassa anabhiññāya           mahā me bhayamāgataṃ .

[802] na tassa vuḍḍhi kusalappasatthā    
yo vaḍḍhamāno ghasate patiṭṭhaṃ    
tassūparodhaṃ parisaṅkamāno    
patārayī mūlavadhāya dhīroti .

Palāsajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Vaṇṇārohavaggo dutiyo [^181-4] .

#[^181-1] ma. Checchati . [^181-2] ma. Evaṃ me so . [^181-3] ma. Bhāyeti . [^181-4] idha tassuddānaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 182 -->
<a id="page-182"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 182]*

[^182-3] aḍḍhavaggo    
[^182-1] dīghītikosalajātakaṃ    
[803] evaṃ bhūtassa te rāja        āgatassa vase [^182-1] mama    
atthi nu koci pariyāyo       yo taṃ dukkhā pamocaye .

[804] evambhūtassa me tāta        āgatassa vase tava    
natthi no koci pariyāyo      yo maṃ dukkhā pamocaye .

[805] nāññaṃ sucaritaṃ rāja          nāññaṃ rāja subhāsitaṃ    
tāyate maraṇakāle          evamevitaraṃ dhanaṃ .

[806] akkocchi maṃ avadhi maṃ         ajini maṃ ahāsi me    
ye ca taṃ upanayhanti         veraṃ tesaṃ na sammati    
akkocchi maṃ avadhi maṃ         ajini maṃ ahāsi me    
ye ca taṃ nūpanayhanti         veraṃ tesūpasammati .

[807] na hi verena verāni        sammantīdha kudācanaṃ    
averena ca sammanti         esa dhammo sanantanoti .

Dīghītikosalajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^182-2] migapotakajātakaṃ    
[808] agārā paccupetassa         anāgārassa te sato    
samaṇassa na taṃ sādhu          yaṃ petamanusocasi .

[809] saṃvāsena have sakka         manussassa migassa vā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 183 -->
<a id="page-183"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 183]*

Hadaye jāyate pemaṃ         na taṃ sakkā asocituṃ .

[810] mataṃ marissaṃ rodanti          ye rudanti lapanti ca    
tasmā tvaṃ isi mā rodi      roditaṃ moghamāhu santo .

[811] roditena have brahme       mato peto samuṭṭhahe    
sabbe saṅgamma rodāma       aññamaññassa ñātake .

[812] ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ         ghatasittaṃva pāvakaṃ    
vārinā viya osiñcaṃ         sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ    
abbūḷhaṃ [^183-1] vata me sallaṃ     yamāsi hadayassitaṃ    
yo me sokaparetassa        puttasokaṃ apānudi    
sohaṃ abbūḷhasallosmi        vītasoko anāvilo    
na socāmi na rodāmi        tava sutvāna vāsavāti .

Migapotakajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^183-3] mūsikajātakaṃ    
[813] kuhiṃ gatā kattha gatā         iti lālappatī jano    
ahameveko jānāmi         udapāne mūsikā hatā .

[814] yañcetaṃ iti cintī ca [^183-2]      gadrabhova nivattasi    
udapāne mūsikaṃ hantvā       yavaṃ bhakkhitumicchasi [^183-3] .

[815] daharo cāsi dummedha         paṭhamuppattiko [^183-4] susū [^183-5]    
#[^183-1] ma. Abbahi . [^183-2] sī. Yathetaṃ iticiti ca . Ma. Yañcetaṃ iti cīti ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 184 -->
<a id="page-184"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 184]*

Dīghañcetaṃ [^184-1] samāpajja [^184-2]    na te dassāmi jīvitaṃ .

[816] nāntalikkhabhavanena           nāṅgaputtasirena [^184-3] vā    
puttena hi patthayito         silokehi pamocito .

[817] sabbaṃ sutamadhīyetha            hīnamukkaṭṭhamajjhimaṃ    
sabbassa atthaṃ jāneyya       na ca sabbaṃ payojaye    
hoti tādisako kālo        yattha atthāvahaṃ sutanti .

Mūsikajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^184-4] culladhanuggahajātakaṃ    
[818] sabbaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ samādāya         pāraṃ tiṇṇosi brāhmaṇa    
paccāgaccha lahuṃ khippaṃ         mampi tārehidāni bho [^184-4] .

[819] asanthutaṃ maṃ cirasanthutena    
nimini [^184-5] bhotī adhuvaṃ [^184-6] dhuvena    
mayāpi bhotī nimineyya aññaṃ    
ito ahaṃ dūrataraṃ gamissaṃ .

[820] kāyaṃ eḷagaṇigumbe [^184-7]       karoti ahuhāsiyaṃ    
nayidha naccaṃ vā gītaṃ vā       tālaṃ vā susamāhitaṃ    
anamhikāle [^184-8] sussoṇi      kinnu jagghasi sobhane .

[821] sigāla bāla dummedha         appapaññosi jambuka
