#[^184-1] yu. Dīghametaṃ . [^184-2] ma. Samāsajja . [^184-3] ma. Nāṅgaputtapinena vā . [^184-4] sī. Ma. Dānito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 185 -->
<a id="page-185"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 185]*

Jinno [^185-1] macchañca pesiñca    kapaṇo viya jhāyasi .

[822] sudassaṃ vajjamaññesaṃ          attano pana duddasaṃ    
jinnā patiñca jārañca        mampi tvaññeva [^185-2] jhāyasi .

[823] evametaṃ migarāja           yathā bhāsasi jambuka    
sā nūnāhaṃ ito gantvā      bhattu hessaṃ vasānugā .

[824] yo hare mattikaṃ thālaṃ        kaṃsathālampi so hare    
kataṃyeva [^185-3] tayā pāpaṃ       punapevaṃ karissasīti .

Culladhanuggahajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^185-5] kapotakajātakaṃ    
[825] idāni khomhi sukhito arogo    
nikkaṇṭako nippatito kapoto    
kāhāmidāni hadayassa tuṭṭhiṃ    
tathāhi maṃ maṃsasākaṃ baleti .

[826] kāyaṃ balākā sikhinī          corī laṅghīpitāmahā    
oraṃ balāke āgaccha        caṇḍo me vāyaso sakhā .

[827] alañhi te jagghitāye        mamaṃ disvāna edisaṃ    
vilūnaṃ sūdaputtena            piṭṭhamaṭṭhena [^185-4] makkhitaṃ .

[828] sunhāto suvilittosi         annapānena tappito    
#[^185-1] ma. Jīno . Evamuparipi . [^185-2] ma. Maññe tvaññeva . Yu. Tvampi maññeva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 186 -->
<a id="page-186"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 186]*

Kaṇṭhe ca te veḷuriyo       agamāsi [^186-1] kajaṅgalaṃ .

[829] mā te mitto amitto vā    agamāsi kajaṅgalaṃ    
piñjāni [^186-2] tattha lāyitvā    kaṇṭhe bandhanti vaṭṭanaṃ .

[830] puna pāpajjasī samma          sīlaṃ hi tava tādisaṃ    
na hi mānusakā bhogā        subhuñjā honti pakkhinoti [^186-3] .

Kapotakajātakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .

Aḍḍhavaggo tatiyo .

tassuddānaṃ    
atha vaṇṇa sasīla hiri labhate    
sumukhāsi sasāliya [^186-4] mittavaro    
atha cakka palāsa sarāja sato    
yava bāla kapotaka paṇṇarasa [^186-5] .

tatra vagguddānaṃ bhavati .

Jinañca vaṇṇaṃ asamaṃ vaguppari    
sudesitā jātakanti santi vīsati    
mahesino brahmacarittamutta    
mavoca gāthā atthavatī subyañjanā .

Pañcakanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .

#[^186-1] sī. Ma. Agamā nu . [^186-2] ma. Piñchāni . [^186-3] ma. Pakkhiṇāti . [^186-4] ma. Sumukhā visa sāḷiya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 187 -->
<a id="page-187"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 187]*

Chakkanipātajātakaṃ    
[^187-1] āvāriyavaggo [^187-1]    
[^187-1] āvāriyajātakaṃ [^187-1]    
[831] māsu kujjha [^187-2] bhūmipati        māsu kujjha [^187-2] rathesabha    
kuddhaṃ appaṭikujjhanto         rājā raṭṭhassa pūjito    
gāme vā yadivāraññe       ninne vā yadivā thale    
sabbatthamanusāsāmi [^187-3]        māsu kujjha [^187-2] rathesabha .

[832] āvāriyapitā nāma          ahu gaṅgāya nāviko    
pubbe janaṃ tārayitvā [^187-4]     pacchā yācati vetanaṃ    
tenassa bhaṇḍanaṃ hoti         na ca bhogehi vaḍḍhati .

[833] atiṇṇaññeva yācassu         apāraṃ tāta nāvika    
añño hi tiṇṇassa mano       añño hoti tiresino [^187-5] .

[834] gāme vā yadivāraññe       ninne vā yadivā thale  

sabbatthamanusāsāmi [^187-3]        māsu kujjhittha nāvika .

[835] yāyeva anusāsaniyā         rājā gāmavaraṃ adā    
tāyeva anusāsaniyā         nāviko paharī mukhaṃ .

[836] bhattaṃ bhinnaṃ hatā bhariyā       gabbho ca patito chamā    
migova jātarūpena           na tenatthaṃ avaḍḍhitunti [^187-6] .

Āvāriyajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

#[^187-1] ma. Sabbattha avāriya. - . [^187-2] sī. Yu. Māssu kujjhi . [^187-3] ma. Sabbattha anusāsāmi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 188 -->
<a id="page-188"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 188]*

[^188-2] setaketujātakaṃ    
[837] mā tāta kujjhi na hi sādhu kodho    
bahumpi te adiṭṭhaṃ assutañca    
mātāpitā disā [^188-1] tāta setaketu    
ācariyamāhu disataṃ pasatthā .

[838] agārino annapānavatthadā [^188-2]    
avhāyikā tampi disaṃ vadanti    
esā disā paramā setaketu    
yaṃ patvā dukkhī sukhino bhavanti .

[839] kharājinā jaṭilā paṅkadantā    
dumukkharūpā [^188-3] yeme japanti mante    
kacci nu te mānusake payoge    
idaṃ vidū parimuttā apāyā .

[840] pāpāni kammāni karitvā [^188-4] rāja    
bahussuto neva [^188-5] careyya dhammaṃ    
sahassavedopi na taṃ paṭicca    
dukkhā pamuñce caraṇaṃ apatvā .

[841] sahassavedopi na taṃ paṭicca    
dukkhā pamuñce caraṇaṃ apatvā    
#[^188-1] ma. Disatā . Yu. Disā tā . [^188-2] ma. Annadapānavatthadā . [^188-3] ma. Dummakkharūpā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 189 -->
<a id="page-189"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 189]*

Maññāmi vedā aphalā bhavanti    
sasaṃyamaṃ caraṇaññeva [^189-1] saccaṃ .

[842] na heva vedā aphalā bhavanti    
sasaṃyamaṃ caraṇaññeva [^189-1] saccaṃ    
kittiñca [^189-2] pappoti adhicca vede    
santiṃ puṇeti [^189-3] caraṇena dantoti .

Setaketujātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^189-3] darīmukhajātakaṃ    
[843] paṅko ca kāmā palipo ca kāmā    
bhayañca metaṃ timūlaṃ pavuttaṃ    
rajo ca dhūmo ca mayā pakāsitā    
hitvā tuvaṃ pabbaja brahmadatta .

[844] gadhito [^189-4] ca ratto ca adhimucchito ca    
kāmesvāhaṃ [^189-5] brāhmaṇa bhiṃsarūpaṃ    
taṃ nussahe jīvikattho pahātuṃ    
kāhāmi puññāni anappakāni .

[845] yo atthakāmassa hitānukampino    
ovajjamāno na karoti sāsanaṃ    
idameva seyyo iti maññamāno    
#[^189-1] ma. Caraṇameva . [^189-2] ma. Kittiṃ hi . [^189-3] sī. Yu. Santaṃ puneti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 190 -->
<a id="page-190"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 190]*

Punappunaṃ gabbhamupeti mando .

[846] so ghorarūpaṃ nirayaṃ upeti    
subhāsubhaṃ muttakarīsapūraṃ    
sattā sakāye na jahanti giddhā    
ye honti kāmesu avītarāgā .

[847] miḷhena [^190-1] littā ruhirena makkhitā    
semhena littā upanikkhamanti    
yaṃ yaṃ hi kāyena phusanti tāvade    
sabbaṃ asātaṃ dukkhameva kevalaṃ .

[848] disvā vadāmi na hi aññato savaṃ    
pubbenivāsaṃ bahukaṃ sarāmi    
citrāhi gāthāhi subhāsitāhi    
darīmukho nijjhāpayī sumedhanti .

Darīmukhajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^190-4] nerujātakaṃ    
[849] kākolā kākasaṅghā ca       mayañca patataṃ varā    
sabbeva sadisā homa         imaṃ āgamma pabbataṃ .

[850] idha sīhā ca byagghā ca       sakuṇā [^190-2] ca migādhamā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 191 -->
<a id="page-191"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 191]*

Sabbeva sadisā honti        ayaṃ ko nāma pabbato .

[851] imaṃ nerunti [^191-1] jānanti      manussā pabbatuttamaṃ    
idha vaṇṇena sampannā        vasanti sabbapāṇino .

[852] amānanā yattha siyā         santānaṃ vā vimānanā    
hīnasammānanā vāpi          na tattha vasatī [^191-2] vase .

[853] yattha alaso ca dakkho ca      sūro bhīru ca pūjiyā    
na tattha santo vasanti        avisesakare nage [^191-3] .

[854] nāyaṃ neru vibhajati           hīnaükkaṭṭhamajjhime    
avisesakaro neru           handa neruṃ jahāmaseti .

Nerujātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^191-5] āsaṅkajātakaṃ    
[855] āsāvatī nāma latā         jātā cittalatāvane    
tassā vassasahassena         ekaṃ nibbattate phalaṃ .

[856] taṃ devā payirupāsanti        tāva dūraphale sati [^191-4]    
āsiṃseva [^191-5] tuvaṃ rāja       āsā phalavatī sukhā .

[857] āsiṃsatheva [^191-6] so pakkhī      āsiṃsatheva so dijo    
tassevāsā samijjhattha [^191-7]     tāva dūragatā sati    
āsiṃseva tuvaṃ rāja          āsā phalavatī sukhā .

[858] sampesi kho maṃ vācāya       na ca sampesi kammunā    
#[^191-1] ma. Nerūti . [^191-2] ma. Vasatiṃ vase . Yu. Vasa divase . [^191-3] ma. Nare . [^191-4] ma. Dūraphalaṃ satiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 192 -->
<a id="page-192"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 192]*

Mālā sereyyakasseva       vaṇṇavantā agandhikā .

[859] aphalaṃ madhuraṃ vācaṃ            yo mittesu pakubbati    
adadaṃ avissajaṃ bhogaṃ          sandhi tenassa jīrati .

[860] yaṃ hi kayirā taṃ hi vade       yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vade    
akarontaṃ bhāsamānaṃ          parijānanti paṇḍitā .

[861] balañca vata me khīṇaṃ          pātheyyañca na vijjati    
saṅke pāṇūparodhāya         handadāni vajāmahaṃ .

[862] etadeva hi me nāmaṃ        yaṃ nāmasmiṃ rathesabha    
āgamehi mahārāja          pitaraṃ āmantayāmahanti .

Āsaṅkajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^192-6] migālopajātakaṃ    
[863] na me ruci [^192-1] migālopa      yassa te tādisī gati    
atuccaṃ tāta gacchasi [^192-2]       abhūmiṃ tāta sevasi .

[864] catukkaṇṇaṃva kedāraṃ          yathā te paṭhavī siyā    
tato tāta nivattassu         māsu etto paraṃ gami .

[865] santi aññepi sakuṇā         pattayānā vihaṅgamā    
ukkhittā [^192-3] vātavegena     naṭṭhā te sassatīsamā .

[866] akatvā aparaṇṇassa [^192-4]       pitu vuḍḍhassa [^192-5] sāsanaṃ    
#[^192-1] ma. Rucci . [^192-2] sī. Ma. Patasi . [^192-3] sī. Ma. Akkhittā . [^192-4] ma. Apanandassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 193 -->
<a id="page-193"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 193]*

Kālavāte atikkamma         verambhānaṃ vasaṃ agā .

[867] tassa puttā ca dārā ca      ye caññe anujīvino    
sabbe byasanamāpāduṃ         anovādakare dije .

[868] evampi idha vuḍḍhānaṃ         yo vākyaṃ nāvabujjhati    
atisīmaṃ caro [^193-1] ditto       gijjho vātītasāsano    
sabbe byasanaṃ papponti       akatvā buddhasāsananti [^193-2] .

Migālopajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^193-7] sirikāḷakaṇṇijātakaṃ    
[869] kā nu kāḷena vaṇṇena       na cāpi [^193-3] piyadassanā    
kā vā tvaṃ kassa vā dhītā    kathaṃ jānemu taṃ mayaṃ .

[870] mahārājassahaṃ dhītā          virūpakkhassa caṇḍiyā    
ahaṃ kāḷī alakkhikā          kāḷakaṇṇīti maṃ vidū    
okāsaṃ yācito dehi        vasemu tava santike .

[871] kiṃsīle kiṃsamācāre          purise nivise [^193-4] tuvaṃ    
puṭṭhā me kāḷi akkhāhi      kathaṃ [^193-5] jānemu taṃ mayaṃ .

[872] makkhī palāsī sārambhī         issukī maccharī satho    
so mayhaṃ puriso kanto       laddhaṃ yassa vinassati .

[873] kodhano upanāhī ca          pisuṇo ca vibhedako    
kaṇṭakavāco [^193-6] pharuso       so me kantataro tato .

#[^193-1] ma. Atisīmacaro . [^193-2] vuddhasāsananti . [^193-3] sī. Na cāsi . [^193-4] ma. Nivisase .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 194 -->
<a id="page-194"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 194]*

[874] ajja suveti puriso          sadatthaṃ nāvabujjhati    
ovajjamāno kuppati         seyyaso [^194-1] atimaññati .

[875] davappaluddho [^194-2] puriso       sabbamittehi dhaṃsati    
so mayhaṃ puriso kanto       tasmiṃ homi anāmayā .

[876] apehi etto tvaṃ kāḷi      netaṃ amhesu vijjati    
aññaṃ janapadaṃ gaccha           nigame rājadhāniyo .

[877] ahampi kho taṃ [^194-3] jānāmi     netaṃ tumhesu vijjati    
santi loke alakkhikā        saṅgharanti bahuṃ dhanaṃ    
ahaṃ devo ca me bhātā      ubho naṃ vidhamāmase .

[878] kā nu dibbena vaṇṇena       paṭhabyā supatiṭṭhitā    
kā vā tvaṃ kassa vā dhītā    kathaṃ jānemu taṃ mayaṃ .

[879] mahārājassahaṃ dhītā          dhataraṭṭhasirīmato [^194-4]    
ahaṃ sirī ca lakkhī ca          bhūripaññāti maṃ vidū    
okāsaṃ yācito dehi        vasemu tava santike .

[880] kiṃsīle kiṃsamācāre          purise nivise [^194-5] tuvaṃ    
puṭṭhā me lakkhi akkhāhi      yathā jānemu taṃ mayaṃ .

[881] yo cāpi sīte athavāpi uṇhe    
vātātape ḍaṃsasiriṃsape ca    
khudda [^194-6] pipāsaṃ abhibhuyya sabbaṃ    
rattindivaṃ yo satataṃ niyutto    
#[^194-1] ma. Seyyaṃ so . [^194-2] yu. Davappaladdho . [^194-3] sī. Cetaṃ . [^194-4] ma. Dhataraṭṭhassa sirīmato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 195 -->
<a id="page-195"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 195]*

Kālāgatañca na hāpeti atthaṃ    
so me manāpo nivise vatamhi [^195-1] .

[882] akkodhano mittavā cāgavā ca    
sīlūpapanno asathojubhūto [^195-2]    
saṅgāhako sakhilo saṇhavāco    
mahattapattopi nivātavutti    
tasmiṃha [^195-3] pose vipulā bhavāmi    
ummī [^195-4] samuddassa yathāpi vaṇṇaṃ .

[883] yo cāpi mitte athavā amitte    
seṭṭhe sarikkhe athavāpi hīne    
atthaṃ carantaṃ athavā anatthaṃ    
āvī raho saṅgahameva vatto [^195-5]    
vācaṃ na vajjā pharusaṃ kadāci    
matassa jīvassa ca tassa homi .

[884] etesa yo aññataraṃ labhitvā    
kantaṃ siriṃ [^195-6] majjati appapañño    
taṃ dittarūpaṃ visamaṃ carantaṃ    
karīsajātaṃva [^195-7] vivajjayāmi .

[885] attanā kurute lakkhiṃ         alakkhiṃ kurutattanā    
#[^195-1] ma. Ca tamhi . [^195-2] yu. Asaṭho ujjubhūto . [^195-3] ma. Tasmiṃhaṃ . Sī. Yu. Tasmāhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 196 -->
<a id="page-196"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 196]*

Na hi lakkhiṃ alakkhiṃ vā        añño aññassa kārakoti .

Sirikāḷakaṇṇijātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^196-8] kukkuṭajātakaṃ    
[886] sucittapattachādana [^196-1]         tambacūḷa vihaṅgama    
oroha dumasākhāya          mudhā bhariyā bhavāmi te .

[887] catuppadī tvaṃ kalyāṇi         dvipadāhaṃ manorame    
migī pakkhī asaṃyuttā [^196-2]       aññaṃ pariyesa sāmikaṃ .

[888] komārikā te hessāmi      mañjukā piyabhāṇinī    
vinda maṃ ariyena vedena      kalyāṇiṃ brahmacāriniṃ [^196-3] .

[889] kuṇapādi [^196-4] lohitape        cori kukkuṭapothini    
na tvaṃ ariyena vedena       mamaṃ bhattāramicchasi .

[890] evampicāturā [^196-5] nārī      disvāna pavaraṃ [^196-6] naraṃ    
nenti saṇhāhi vācāhi       viḷārī viya kukkuṭaṃ .

[891] yo ca uppatitaṃ atthaṃ         na khippamanubujjhati    
amittavasamanveti            pacchā ca anutappati .

[892] yo ca uppatitaṃ atthaṃ         khippameva nibodhati    
#[^196-1] ma. Sucittapattachadana . [^196-2] ma. Asaññuttā . [^196-3] sī. Ma. Sāvaya maṃ yadicchasi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 197 -->
<a id="page-197"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 197]*

Muccate sattusambādhā        kukkuṭova viḷāriyāti .

Kukkuṭajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^197-9] dhammaddhajajātakaṃ    
[893] dhammaṃ caratha ñātayo          dhammaṃ caratha bhadda vo [^197-1]    
dhammacārī sukhaṃ seti          asmiṃ loke paramhi ca .

[894] bhaddako vatāyaṃ pakkhī         dijo paramadhammiko    
ekapādena tiṭṭhanto        dhammamevānusāsati .

[895] nāssa sīlaṃ vijānātha         anaññāya pasaṃsatha    
bhutvā aṇḍañca chāpe ca [^197-2]   dhammo dhammoti bhāsati .

[896] aññaṃ bhaṇati vācāya          aññaṃ kāyena kubbati    
vācāya no ca kāyena       na taṃ dhammaṃ adhiṭṭhito .

[897] * vācāya  sakhilo hoti      manopaviduggo siyā  
paṭicchanno kūpassayo         kaṇhasappova dhammaddhajo  
gāmanigamāsu sādhu           sammato dujjāno ayaṃ  
#[^197-1] ma. Bhaddaṃ vo . [^197-2] ma. Yu. Potañca .  * sī. Ma. Evaṃ dissati  
#              vācāya sakhilo manoviduggo  
#              channo kupassayova kaṇhasappo  
#              dhammaddhajo gāmanigamāsu sādhu  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 198 -->
<a id="page-198"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 198]*

Purisena bālisena *  .

[898] imaṃ tuṇḍehi pakkhehi         pādā cimaṃ viheṭhatha [^198-1]    
chavaṃ himaṃ vināsetha           nāyaṃ saṃvāsanārahoti .

Dhammaddhajajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 nandiyamigarājajātakaṃ    
[899] sace brāhmaṇa gacchasi [^198-2]     sāketaṃ añjanaṃ [^198-3] vanaṃ    
vajjāsi nandiyaṃ nāma         puttaṃ asmākamorasaṃ    
mātāpitā ca te vuḍḍhā      te taṃ icchanti passituṃ .

[900] bhuttā mayā nivāpāni        rājino pānabhojanaṃ    
taṃ rājapiṇḍaṃ avabhottuṃ        nāhaṃ brāhmaṇamussahe .

[901] odahissāmahaṃ passaṃ          khurappānissa [^198-4] rājino    
tadāhaṃ sukhito mutto         api passeyya mātaraṃ .

[902] migarājā pure āsiṃ         kosalassa niketave [^198-5]    
nandiyo nāma nāmena        abhirūpo catuppado .

[903] taṃ maṃ vadhitumāgacchi           dāyasmiṃ añjanāvane [^198-6]    
dhanana ārajjaṃ katvāna         usuṃ sandhāya [^198-7] kosalo .

[904] tassāhaṃ odahiṃ passaṃ         khurappānissa rājino    
#[^198-1] ma. Vipothuṃ . [^198-2] ma. Gacchesi . [^198-3] ma. Sākete ajjunaṃ . [^198-4] sī. Khurappāṇissa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 199 -->
<a id="page-199"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 199]*

Tadāhaṃ sukhito mutto         mātaraṃ daṭṭhumāgatoti .

Nandiyamigarājajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Āvāriyavaggo paṭhamo .

tassuddānaṃ    
atha kujjharathesabha ketuvaro    
darīmukha sanerutā ca puna [^199-1]    
apananda sirī ca sucittavaro    
atha dhammika nandimigena dasa .

[^199-2] khuraputtavaggo [^199-2]    
[^199-1] khuraputtajātakaṃ [^199-2]    
[905] saccaṃ kirevamāhaṃsu           kalakaṃ [^199-3] bāloti paṇḍitā    
passa bālo rahokammaṃ        āvikubbaṃ na bujjhati .

[906] tvaṃ nu kho [^199-4] samma bālosi   khuraputta vijānahi    
rajjuyāhi [^199-5] parikkhitto      vaṅkoṭṭho ohitomukho .

[907] aparaṃpi samma te bālyaṃ       yo mutto na palāyasi    
so ca bālataro samma        yaṃ tvaṃ vahasi senakaṃ .

[908] yannu samma ahaṃ bālo        ajarāja vijānahi    
#[^199-1] ma. Sadarīmukha neru latā ca puna . [^199-2] ma. Kharaputta ... . [^199-3] ma. Vastaṃ . Sī. Yu. Bhastaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 200 -->
<a id="page-200"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 200]*

Atha kena senako bālo      taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[909] uttamatthaṃ labhitvāna          bhariyāya [^200-1] yo padassati    
tena jahissatattānaṃ          sā cevassa na hessati .

[910] na piyammeti [^200-2] janinda tādiso    
attaṃ niraṅkatva piyāni sevati    
attāva seyyo paramāva [^200-3] seyyo    
labbhā piyā ocitattena [^200-4] pacchāti .

Khuraputtajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^200-2] sūcijātakaṃ    
[911] akakkasaṃ apharusaṃ             kharadhotaṃ supāsiyaṃ    
sukhumaṃ tikhiṇaggañca            ko sūciṃ ketumicchati .

[912] sumajjañca supāsañca          anupubbasuvattitaṃ [^200-5]    
ghanaghātimaṃ paṭitthaddhaṃ          ko sūciṃ ketumicchati .

[913] itodāni patāyanti          sūciyo balisāni ca    
koyaṃ kammāragāmasmiṃ         sūciṃ vikketumicchati .

[914] ito satthāni gacchanti        kammantā vividhā puthū    
koyaṃ kammāragāmasmiṃ         sūciṃ vikketumarahati [^200-6] .

#[^200-1] yu. Bhariyā . [^200-2] ma. Nave piyammeti . [^200-3] ma. Paramā ca . [^200-4] ma. Ocitatthena .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 201 -->
<a id="page-201"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 201]*

[915] sūci [^201-1] kammāragāmasmiṃ       vikketabbā pajānatā    
ācariyā pajānanti [^201-2]       kammaṃ sukatadukkaṭaṃ .

[916] imañca [^201-3] te pitā bhadde    sūciṃ jaññā mayā kataṃ    
tayā ca maṃ nimanteyya        yañcatthaññaṃ ghare dhananti .

Sūcijātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^201-3] tuṇḍilajātakaṃ    
[917] navacchaddake [^201-4] dāni diyyati    
puṇṇāyaṃ doṇi suvāminī ṭhitā    
bahuko jano pāsapāṇiko [^201-5]    
no ca kho me paṭibhāti bhuñjituṃ .

[918] tasasi bhamasi leṇamicchasi    
atāṇosi kuhiṃ gamissasi    
appossuko bhuñja tuṇḍila    
maṃsatthāya [6] posiyāmase [^201-7] .

[919] ogaha rahadaṃ akaddamaṃ    
sabbaṃ sedamalaṃ pavāhaya    
gaṇhāhi navaṃ vilepanaṃ    
#[^201-1] ma. Sūcī .  [^201-2] ma. Ācariyāva jānanti .  [^201-3] ma. Imañce .  [^201-4] navachannake .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 202 -->
<a id="page-202"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 202]*

Yassa gandho na kadāci chijjati .

[920] katamo nu rahado akaddamo    
kiṃsu sedamalanti vuccati    
katamañca navaṃ vilepanaṃ    
kassa [^202-1] gandho na kadāci chijjati .

[921] dhammo rahado akaddamo    
pāpaṃ sedamalanti vuccati    
sīlañca navaṃ vilepanaṃ    
tassa gandho na kadāci chijjati .

[922] nandanti sarīraghātino    
na ca nandanti sarīradhārino    
puṇṇāya [2] puṇṇamāsiyā    
ramamānāva jahanti jīvitanti .

Tuṇḍilajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^202-4] suvaṇṇakakkaṭakajātakaṃ    
[923] siṅgī migo āyatacakkhunetto    
aṭṭhittaco vārisayo alomo    
tenābhibhūto kapaṇaṃ rudāmi    
hare sakhā kissa nu maṃ jahāsi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 203 -->
<a id="page-203"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 203]*

[924] so assasanto mahatā phaṇena    
bhujaṅgamo kakkaṭakajjhapatto    
sakhā sakhāraṃ paritāyamāno    
bhujaṅgamaṃ kakkaṭako aggahesi [^203-1] .

[925] na vāyasaṃ no pana sapparājaṃ [^203-2]    
ghāsatthiko kakkaṭako adeyya    
pucchāmi taṃ āyatacakkhunetta    
atha kissa hetumha ubho gahītā .

[926] ayaṃ puriso mama atthakāmo    
yo maṃ gahetvā udakāya [^203-3] neti    
tasmiṃ mate dukkhamanappakaṃ me    
ahañca eso ca ubho na homa .

[927] mamañca disvāna pavaḍḍhakāyaṃ    
sabbo jano hiṃsitumevamicche    
sāduñca thūlañca muduñca maṃsaṃ    
kākāpi maṃ disvāna [^203-4] viheṭhayeyyuṃ .

[928] sace tassa hetumha ubho gahītā    
uṭṭhātu poso visamāvamāmi [^203-5]    
mamañca kākañca pamuñca khippaṃ    
#[^203-1] ma. Gahessasi .  [^203-2] ma. Kaṇhasappaṃ .  [^203-3] ma. Gahetvāna dakāya .  [^203-4] sī. Yu. Disvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 204 -->
<a id="page-204"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 204]*

Pure visaṃ gāḷhamupeti maccaṃ .

[929] sappaṃ pamokkhāmi na tāva kākaṃ    
paṭibaddhako [^204-1] hoti [^204-2] hi tāva kāko    
purisañca disvāna sukhiṃ arogaṃ    
kākaṃ pamokkhāmi yatheva sappaṃ .

[930] kāko *  tadā devadatto ahosi    
vāraṇo [^204-3] pana kaṇhasappo ahosi    
ānandabhadro kakkaṭako ahosi    
ahaṃ tadā brāhmaṇo homi satthāti [^204-4] .

Suvaṇṇakakkaṭakajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^204-5] mayhakasakuṇajātakaṃ [^204-5]    
[931] sakuṇo mayhako nāma         girisānudarīcaro    
pakkaṃ pipphalimāruyha          mayhaṃ mayhanti kandati .

[932] tassevaṃ vilapantassa          dijasaṅghā samāgatā    
bhutvāna pipphaliṃ yanti         vilapateva [^204-6] so dijo .

[933] evameva idhekacco         saṅgharitvā bahuṃ dhanaṃ    
nevattano na ñātīnaṃ         yathodhiṃ paṭipajjati .

[934] na so acchādanaṃ bhattaṃ        na mālaṃ na vilepanaṃ    
#[^204-1] ma. Paṭibandhako .  [^204-2] ma. Hohiti . * sī. Kāko .Pe. Satthāti ime pāṭhā natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 205 -->
<a id="page-205"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 205]*

Nānubhoti [^205-1] sakiṃ kiñci       na saṅgaṇhāti ñātake .

[935] tassevaṃ vilapantassa          mayhaṃ mayhanti rakkhato    
rājāno athavā corā       dāyādā ye ca [^205-2] appiyā    
dhanamādāya gacchanti          vilapateva so naro .

[936] dhīro bhoge adhigamma         saṅgaṇhāti ca ñātake    
tena so kittiṃ pappoti       pecca sagge pamodatīti [^205-3] .

Mayhakasakuṇajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^205-6] pabbajitaviheṭhakajātakaṃ [^205-4]    
[937] dubbaṇṇarūpaṃ tvamariyavaṇṇī    
purakkhitvā [^205-5] pañjaliko namassasi    
seyyo nu te so udāhu [^205-6] sarikkho    
nāmaṃ parassattano cāpi brūhi .

[938] na nāmagottaṃ gaṇhanti rāja    
samaggatānujjugatāna [^205-7] devā    
ahañca te nāmadheyyaṃ vadāmi    
sakkohamasmī tidasānamindo .

[939] yo disvā bhikkhuṃ caraṇūpapannaṃ    
purakkhitvā [^205-5] pañjaliko namassati    
pucchāmi taṃ devarājetamatthaṃ    
#[^205-1] ma. Anubhoti .  [^205-2] ma. Yeva .  [^205-3] sī. Yu. Sagge ca modatīti .

#[^205-4] ma. Vijjādharajātakaṃ . Sī. Yu. Dhajaviheṭhakajātakaṃ .  [^205-5] ma. Purakkhatvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 206 -->
<a id="page-206"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 206]*

Ito cuto kiṃ labhate sukhaṃ so .

[940] yo disvā bhikkhuṃ caraṇūpapannaṃ    
purakkhitvā pañjaliko namassati    
diṭṭheva dhamme labhate pasaṃsaṃ    
saggañca so yāti sarīrabhedā .

[941] lakkhī vata me udapādi ajja    
yaṃ vāsavaṃ bhūtipatiddasāma [^206-1]    
bhikkhuñca disvāna tuvañca sakka    
kāhāmi puññāni anappakāni .

[942] addhā have sevitabbā sapaññā    
bahussutā ye bahuṭhānacintino    
bhikkhuñca disvāna mamañca rāja    
karohi puññāni anappakāni .

[943] akkodhano niccapasannacitto    
sabbātithīyācayogo bhavitvā    
nihañña [^206-2] mānaṃ abhivādayissaṃ    
sutvāna devinda subhāsitānīti .

Pabbajitaviheṭhakajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 207 -->
<a id="page-207"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 207]*

[^207-7] upasiṅghapupphajātakaṃ [^207-1]    
[944] yametaṃ [^207-2] vārijaṃ pupphaṃ       adinnaṃ upasiṅghasi    
ekaṅgametaṃ theyyānaṃ        gandhathenosi mārisa .

[945] na harāmi na bhañjāmi         ārā siṅghāmi vārijaṃ    
atha kena nu vaṇṇena         gandhathenoti vuccati .

[946] yoyaṃ bhisāni khaṇati           puṇḍarīkāni bhañjati    
evaṃ ākiṇṇakammanto        kasmā eso na vuccati    
[947] ākiṇṇaluddho puriso         dhāticolaṃva [^207-3] makkhito    
tasmiṃ me vacanaṃ natthi         tañcārahāmi vattave [^207-4] .

[948] anaṅgaṇassa posassa          niccaṃ sucigavesino    
vālaggamattapāpassa [^207-5]       abbhāmattaṃva khāyati .

[949] addhā maṃ yakkha jānāsi       atho maṃ anukampasi    
punapi yakkha vajjāsi          yadā passasi edisaṃ .

[950] neva taṃ upajīvāmi           napi te bhatikamhase [^207-6]    
tvameva bhikkhu jāneyya       yena gaccheyya suggatinti .

Upasiṅghapupphajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^207-8] vighāsādajātakaṃ    
[951] susukhaṃ vata jīvanti            ye janā vighāsādino    
#[^207-1] ma. Siṅghapupphajātakaṃ . Sī. Yu. Bhisapupphajātakaṃ .  [^207-2] yu. Yamekaṃ .  [^207-3] ma.

#dhāticelaṃva . [^207-4] sī. Vaṇṇave .  [^207-5] ma. Vālaggamattaṃ pāpassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 208 -->
<a id="page-208"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 208]*

Diṭṭheva dhamme pāsaṃsā       samparāye ca suggati [^208-1] .

[952] sukassa [^208-2] bhāsamānassa       na nisāmetha paṇḍitā    
idaṃ suṇātha sodariyā         amhevāyaṃ pasaṃsati .

[953] nāhaṃ tumhe pasaṃsāmi         kuṇapādā suṇātha me    
ucchiṭṭhabhojino tumhe        na tumhe vighāsādino    
[954] sattavassā pabbajitā         majjharaññe sikhaṇḍino    
vighāseneva yāpentā       mayaṃ ce bhoto gārayhā    
ke nu bhoto pasaṃsiyā .

[955] tumhe sīhānaṃ byagghānaṃ       vāḷānañcāvasiṭṭhakaṃ    
ucchiṭṭheneva yāpentā      maññivho vighāsādino .

[956] ye brāhmaṇassa samaṇassa      aññassa ca vaṇibbino [^208-3]    
datvāvasesaṃ [^208-4] bhuñjanti      te janā vighāsādinoti .

Vighāsādajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^208-9] vaṭṭakajātakaṃ    
[957] paṇītaṃ bhuñjase bhattaṃ          sappitelañca mātula    
atha kena nu vaṇṇena         kīso [^208-5] tvamasi vāyasa .

[958] amittamajjhe vasato          tesu āmisamesato    
niccaṃ ubbiggahadayassa         kuto kākassa daḷhiyaṃ .

[959] niccaṃ ubbiggino [^208-6] kākā    dhaṅkā pāpena kammunā    
#[^208-1] ma. Suggatī .  [^208-2] sī. Yu. Suvassa .  [^208-3] ma. Uññassa vā vanibbano .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 209 -->
<a id="page-209"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 209]*

Laddho piṇḍo na pīṇeti       kīso tenasmi vaṭṭaka .

[960] lūkhāni tiṇavījāni            appasnehāni bhuñjasi    
atha kena nu vaṇṇena         thūlo tvamasi vaṭṭaka .

[961] appicchā appacintāya        avidūragamena [^209-1] ca    
laddhāladdhena yāpento      thūlo tenasmi vāyasa .

[962] appicchassa hi posassa        appacintisukhassa [^209-2] ca    
susaṅgahitappamāṇassa [^209-3]       vuttī susamudāniyāti .

Vaṭṭakajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 maṇijātakaṃ [^209-4]    
[963] cirassaṃ vata passāmi          sahāyaṃ maṇidhārinaṃ    
sukatā [^209-5] massukuttiyā       sobhate vata me sakhā .

[964] parūḷhakacchanakhalomo          ahaṃ kamme subyāvaṭo    
cirassaṃ nahāpitaṃ laddhā        lomantamajjahārayi [^209-6] .

[965] yaṃ nu lomaṃ ahāresi         dullabhaṃ laddhakappakaṃ    
atha kiñci hi te samma        kaṇṭhe kiṃnikilāyati [^209-7] .

[966] manussasukhumālānaṃ            maṇi kaṇṭhesu lambati    
#[^209-1] ma. Adūragamanena ca .  [^209-2] ma. Appacintasukhassa ca .  [^209-3] ma. Susaṅgahitamānassa .

#[^209-4] sī. Yu. Kākajātakaṃ . Ma. Pārāvatajātakaṃ .  [^209-5] sī. Yu. Sukatāya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 210 -->
<a id="page-210"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 210]*

Tesāhaṃ anusikkhāmi          mā tvaṃ maññi davā kataṃ .

[967] sacepimaṃ pihayasi             massukuttiṃ sukāritaṃ    
kārayissāmi te samma        maṇiṃ cāpi dadāmi te .

[968] tvaññeva maṇinā channo       sukatāya ca massuyā    
āmanta kho taṃ gacchāmi       piyaṃ me tava dassananti .

Maṇijātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Khuraputtavaggo [^210-1] dutiyo .

tassuddānaṃ    
atha passa sasūci ca tuṇḍilako    
miga mayhakapañcamapakkhivaro    
atha pañjali vārija majjha puna    
atha vaṭṭaka kapoṭavarena dasa .

tatra vagguddānaṃ bhavati .

Atha vaggaṃ pakittissaṃ          chanipātaṃ varuttame    
āvāriyā ca sūci [^210-2] ca      dve ca vuttā subyañjanā .

Chakkanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 211 -->
<a id="page-211"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 211]*

Sattakanipātajātakaṃ    
[^211-1] kukkuvaggo    
[^211-1] kukkujātakaṃ    
[969] diyaḍḍhakukkū udayena kaṇṇikā    
vidatthiyo aṭṭha parikkhipanti naṃ    
sā sīsapā [^211-1] sāramayā apheggukā    
kuhiṃ ṭhitā uparito [^211-2] na dhaṃsati .

[970] yā tiṃsati sāramayā apheggukā [^211-3]    
parikiriya gopāṇasiyo samaṭṭhitā [^211-4]    
tāhi susaṅgahitā [^211-5] balasā ca [^211-6] pīḷitā    
samaṭṭhitā [^211-4] uparito [^211-2] na dhaṃsati .

[971] evampi mittehi daḷhehi paṇḍito    
abhejjarūpehi sucīhi mantibhi    
susaṅgahīto siriyā na dhaṃsati    
gopānasībhāravahāva kaṇṇikā .

[972] kharattacaṃ bellaṃ yathāpi satthavā    
anāmasantopi karoti tittakaṃ    
samāharaṃ sādhukaroti patthavā [^211-7]    
asādhukayirā [^211-8] tanubandhamuddharaṃ [^211-9] .

#[^211-1] ma. Sā siṃsapā . Sī. Yu. Sasiṃsapā .  [^211-2] ma. Upparito .  [^211-3] ma. Anujjukā .

#[^211-4] ma. Samaṃ ṭhitā .  [^211-5] sī. Tā saṅgahitā ... .  [^211-6] ma. Casaddo natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 212 -->
<a id="page-212"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 212]*

[973] evampi gāmanigamesu paṇḍito    
asāhasaṃ rāja dhanāni saṅgharaṃ    
dhammānuvattī paṭipajjamāno    
phātiṃ [^212-1] kayirā aviheṭhayaṃ paraṃ    
[974] odātamūlaṃ sucivārisambhavaṃ    
jātaṃ yathā pokkharaṇīsu ambujaṃ    
padumaṃ yathā agginibhāsiphālimaṃ [^212-2]    
na kaddamo na rajo na vāri limpati .

[975] evampi vohārasuciṃ asāhasaṃ    
visuddhakammantamapetapāpakaṃ    
na limpati kammakilesa tādiso    
jātaṃ yathā pokkharaṇīsu ambujanti .

Kukkujātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^212-2] manojajātakaṃ    
[976] yathā cāpo ninnamati         jiyā cāpi nikujjati [^212-3]    
haññate nūna manojo         migarājā sakhā mama .

[977] handadāni vanantāni          pakkamāmi yathāsukhaṃ    
netādisā sakhā honti       labbhā me jīvato sakhā .

[978] na pāpajanasaṃsevī            accantaṃ sukhamedhati  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 213 -->
<a id="page-213"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 213]*

Manojaṃ passa semānaṃ         giriyassānusāsanī [^213-1] .

[979] na pāpasampavaṅkena          mātā puttena nandati    
manojaṃ passa semānaṃ         acchannaṃ samhi lohite .

[980] evamāpajjate poso        pāpiyo ca nigacchati    
yo ve hitāna vacanaṃ         na karoti atthadassinaṃ .

[981] evañca so hoti tatova [^213-2] pāpiyo    
yo uttamo adhamajanūpasevī    
passuttamaṃ adhamajanūpaseviṃ [^213-3]    
migādhipaṃ saravaraveganiddhutaṃ .

[982] nihīyati puriso nihīnasevī    
na ca hāyetha kadāci tulyasevī    
seṭṭhamupagamañca udeti khippaṃ    
tasmā attano uttaraṃ [^213-4] bhajethāti .

Manojajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^213-3] sutanajātakaṃ [^213-5]    
[983] rājā te bhattaṃ pāhesi      suciṃ maṃsūpasecanaṃ    
maghadevasmiṃ adhivatthe         ehi nikkhamma bhuñjassu .

[984] ehi māṇava orohi [^213-6]      bhikkhamādāya sūpitaṃ    
#[^213-1] sī. Yu. ... Ariyassānusāsanī .  [^213-2] ma. Tato ca .  [^213-3] ma. Adhamajanūpasevitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 214 -->
<a id="page-214"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 214]*

Tvañca māṇava bhakkhosi [^214-1]     ubho bhakkhā bhavissatha .

[985] appakena tuvaṃ yakkha          thullamatthaṃ jahissasi    
bhikkhante nāharissanti        janā maraṇasaṅkino [^214-2] .

[986] laddhāyaṃ yakkha [^214-3] tava niccabhikkhaṃ    
suciṃ paṇītaṃ rasasādhupetaṃ [^214-4]    
bhikkhañca te āhariyo naro idha    
sudullabho hohiti khādite [^214-5] mayi .

[987] mameva [^214-6] sutanaṃ [^214-7] attho    yathā bhāsasi māṇava    
mayā tvaṃ samanuññāto        sotthiṃ passāhi mātaraṃ .

[988] khaggaṃ chattañca pātiñca        gacchevādāya [^214-8] māṇava    
sotthiṃ passatu te mātā      tvañca passāhi mātaraṃ .

[989] evaṃ yakkha sukhī hohi         saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
dhanañca me adhigataṃ           rañño ca vacanaṃ katanti .

Sutanajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^214-4] mātuposakagijjhajātakaṃ    
[990] te kathannu karissanti         vuḍḍhā giridarīsayā    
ahaṃ bandhosmi [^214-9] pāsena     niliyassa vasaṃ gato .

[991] kiṃ gijjha paridevesi          kā nu te parivedanā    
#[^214-1] ma. Bhikkhā ca .  [^214-2] sī. Ma. Maraṇasaññino .  [^214-3] ma. Laddhāya yakkhā .

#[^214-4] ma. Rasasā upetaṃ . [^214-5] ma. Hehiti bhikkhite .  [^214-6] sī. Yu. Mamesa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 215 -->
<a id="page-215"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 215]*

Name suto vā diṭṭho vā     bhāsanto mānusiṃ dijo .

[992] bharāmi mātāpitaro          vuḍḍhe giridarīsaye    
te kathannu karissanti         ahaṃ vasaṃ gato tava .

[993] yannu gijjho yojanasataṃ        kuṇapāni avekkhati    
kasmā jālañcapāsañca        āsajjāpi na bujjhasi .

[994] yadā parābhavo hoti         poso jīvitasaṅkhaye    
atha jālañca pāsañca         āsajjāpi na bujjhati .

[995] bharassu mātāpitaro          vuḍḍhe giridarīsaye    
mayā tvaṃ samanuññāto        sotthiṃ passāhi ñātake .

[996] evaṃ luddaka nandassu         saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
bharissaṃ mātāpitaro          vuḍḍhe giridarīsayeti .

Mātuposakagijjhajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^215-5] dabbapupphajātakaṃ [^215-1]    
[997] anutīracārī bhaddante         sahāyamanudhāva maṃ    
mahā me gahito maccho       so maṃ harati vegasā .

[998] gambhīracārī bhaddante         daḷhaṃ gaṇhāhi thāmasā    
ahaṃ taṃ uddharissāmi          supaṇṇo uragammiva [^215-2] .

[999] vivādo no samuppanno       dabbapuppha suṇohi me  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 216 -->
<a id="page-216"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 216]*

Samehi medhagaṃ [^216-1] samma     vivādo vūpasammatu [^216-2] .

[1000] dhammaṭṭhohaṃ pure āsiṃ      bahuaṭṭaṃva tīritaṃ [^216-3]    
samemi medhagaṃ [^216-1] samma     vivādo vūpasammatu  [^216-2] .

[1001] anutīracārī naṅguṭṭhaṃ        sīsaṃ gambhīracārino    
accāyaṃ [^216-4] majjhimokhaṇḍo   dhammaṭṭhassa bhavissati .

[1002] ciraṃpi bhakkho abhavissa       sace na vivademase    
asīsakaṃ anaṅguṭṭhaṃ          sigālo harati rohitaṃ .

[1003] yathāpi rājā nandeyya     rajjaṃ laddhāna khattiyo    
evāhamajja nandāmi       disvā puṇṇamukhaṃ patiṃ .

[1004] kathannu thalajo santo       udake macchaṃ parāmasi    
puṭṭho me samma akkhāhi    kathaṃ adhigataṃ tayā .

[1005] vivādena kisā honti      vivādena dhanakkhayā    
jinā uddā vivādena          bhuñja māyāvi rohitaṃ .

[1006] evameva manussesu        vivādo yattha jāyati .

Dhammaṭṭhaṃ paṭidhāvanti        so hi nesaṃ vināyako    
dhanāpi tattha jīyanti        rājakoso pavaḍḍhatīti .

Dabbapupphajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^216-6] dasaṇṇakajātakaṃ [^216-5]    
[1007] dasaṇṇakaṃ [^216-6] tikhiṇadhāraṃ      asiṃ sampannapāyinaṃ    
#[^216-1] yu. Medhakaṃ .  [^216-2] ma. Vūpasammataṃ .  [^216-3] ma. Bahū aṭṭā me tīritā .  [^216-4] sī. Yu. Athāyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 217 -->
<a id="page-217"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 217]*

Parisāyaṃ puriso gilati       kiṃ dukkarataraṃ tato    
yadaññaṃ dukkaraṃ ṭhānaṃ        taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[1008] gileyya puriso lobhā      asiṃ sampannapāyinaṃ    
yo ca vajjā dadāmīti      taṃ dukkarataraṃ tato    
sabbaññaṃ sukaraṃ ṭhānaṃ        evaṃ jānāhi māgadha [^217-1] .

[1009] byākāsi āyuro pañhaṃ     atthaṃ dhammassa kovido    
pukkusaṃdāni pucchāmi        kiṃ dukkarataraṃ tato    
yadaññaṃ dukkaraṃ ṭhānaṃ        taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[1010] na vācamupajīvanti          aphalaṃ giramudīritaṃ    
yo [^217-2] datvā avākayirā   taṃ dukkarataraṃ tato    
sabbaññaṃ sukaraṃ ṭhānaṃ        evaṃ jānāhi māgadha [^217-1] .

[1011] byākāsi pukkuso pañhaṃ     atthaṃ dhammassa kovido    
senakaṃdāni pucchāmi        kiṃ dukkarataraṃ tato    
yadaññaṃ dukkaraṃ ṭhānaṃ        taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[1012] dadeyya puriso dānaṃ       appaṃ vā yadivā bahuṃ    
yo ca datvā nānutappe    taṃ dukkarataraṃ tato    
sabbaññaṃ sukaraṃ ṭhānaṃ        evaṃ jānāhi māgadha [^217-1] .

[1013] byākāsi āyuro pañhaṃ     atho pukkusaporiso  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 218 -->
<a id="page-218"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 218]*

Sabbe pañhe abhibhoti [^218-1]     yathā bhāsati senakoti .

Dasaṇṇakajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^218-7] senakajātakaṃ [^218-2]    
[1014] vibbhantacitto kupitindriyosi    
nettehi te vārigaṇā savanti    
kinte naṭṭhaṃ kiṃ pana patthayāno    
idhāgamā brāhmaṇa iṅgha [^218-3] brūhi .Ba    
[1015] miyyetha bhariyā vajato mamajja    
agacchato maraṇamāha yakkho    
etena dukkhena pavedhitosmi    
akkhāhi me senaka etamatthaṃ .

[1016] bahūni ṭhānāni vicintayitvā    
yamettha vakkhāmi tadeva saccaṃ    
maññāmi te brāhmaṇa sattubhastaṃt    
ajānato kaṇhasappo paviṭṭho .

[1017] ādāya daṇḍaṃ parisumbha bhastaṃ    
passelamūgaṃ uragaṃ dujivhaṃ    
chindajja kaṅkhaṃ vicikicchitāni    
bhujaṅgamaṃ passa pamuñca bhastaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 219 -->
<a id="page-219"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 219]*

[1018] saṃviggarūpo parisāya majjhe    
so brāhmaṇo sattubhastaṃ pamuñci    
atha nikkhami urago uggatejo    
āsīviso sappo phaṇaṃ karitvā .

[1019] suladdhalābhā janakassa rañño    
yo passati senakaṃ sādhupaññaṃ    
vivaṭṭacchado [^219-1] nusi sabbadassī    
ñāṇaṃ nu te brāhmaṇa bhiṃsarūpaṃ    
imāni me sattasatāni atthi    
gaṇhāhi sabbāni dadāmi tuyhaṃ    
tayā hi me jīvitamajja laddhaṃ    
athopi bhariyāyamakāsi sotthiṃ .

[1020] na paṇḍitā vettanamādiyanti    
citrāhi gāthāhi subhāsitāhi    
itopi te brāhmaṇa dadantu vittaṃ    
ādāya tvaṃ gaccha sakaṃ niketanti .

Senakajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 220 -->
<a id="page-220"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 220]*

[^220-8] aṭṭhisenajātakaṃ [^220-1]    
[1021] yeme ahaṃ na jānāmi        aṭṭhisena vanibbake    
te maṃ saṅgamma yācanti       kasmā maṃ tvaṃ na yācasi .

[1022] yācako appiyo hoti        yācaṃ adadamappiyo    
tasmāhantaṃ na yācāmi        mā me viddessanā ahu .

[1023] yo ca [^220-2] yācanajīvāno      kāle yācaṃ na yācati    
parañca puññaṃ [^220-3] dhaṃseti       attanāpi na jīvati .

[1024] yo ca yācanajīvāno         kāle yācaṃpi [^220-4] yācati    
parañca puññaṃ labbheti         attanāpi ca jīvati .

[1025] na ve dussanti [^220-5] sappaññā   disvā yācakamāgate    
brahmacārī piyo mesi        vara bhaññitamicchasi [^220-6] .

[1026] na ve yācanti sappaññā      dhīro ca viditumarahati    
uddissa ariyā tiṭṭhanti       esā ariyāna yācanā .

[1027] dadāmi te brāhmaṇa rohiṇīnaṃ    
gavaṃ sahassaṃ saha puṅgavena    
ariyo hi ariyassa kathaṃ na dajjā    
sutvāna gāthā tava dhammayuttāti .

Aṭṭhisenajātakaṃ [^220-1] aṭṭhamaṃ .

#[^220-1] ma. Aṭṭhisenakajātakaṃ .  [^220-2] ma. Yo ve .  [^220-3] ma. Puññā .  [^220-4] ma. Yācaṃ hi .

#[^220-5] ma. Dessanti . Sī. Yu. Dissanti .  [^220-6] sī. Vara taṃ bhaññamīcchasi . Ma. Vada tvaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 221 -->
<a id="page-221"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 221]*

[^221-9] kapijātakaṃ    
[1028] yattha verī nivisati [^221-1]        na vase tattha paṇḍito    
ekarattaṃ dvirattaṃ vā        dukkhaṃ vasati verisu .

[1029] diso ve lahucittassa         posassānuvidhiyyato    
ekassa kapino hetu         yūthassa anayo kato .

[1030] bālo ca paṇḍitamānī         yūthassa parihārako    
sacittassa vasaṃ gantvā        sayethāyaṃ yathā kapi .

[1031] na sādhu balavā bālo        yūthassa parihārako    
ahito bhavati ñātīnaṃ          sakuṇānaṃva cetako .

[1032] dhīro ca balavā sādhu         yūthassa parihārako    
hito bhavati ñātīnaṃ           tidasānaṃva vāsavo .

[1033] yo ca sīlañca paññañca        sutañcattani passati    
ubhinnamatthaṃ carati            attano ca parassa ca .

[1034] tasmā tuleyyamattānaṃ        sīlaṃ paññaṃ sutaṃpiva [^221-2]    
gaṇaṃ vā parihare dhīro        eko vāpi paribbajeti .

Kapijātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 bakabrahmajātakaṃ [^221-3]  
[1035] dvāsattati gotama puññakammā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 222 -->
<a id="page-222"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 222]*

Vasavattino jātijaraṃ atītā    
ayamantimā vedagū brahmupapatti [^222-1]    
asmābhijappanti janā anekā .

[1036] appañca hetaṃ [^222-2] na hi dīghamāyu    
yaṃ tvaṃ baka maññasi dīghamāyuṃ    
sataṃ sahassāna [^222-3] nirabbudānaṃ    
āyuṃ pajānāmi tavāha brahme .

[1037] anantadassī bhagavāhamasmi    
jātijjaraṃ sokamupātivatto    
kiṃ me purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ    
ācikkha metaṃ yamahaṃ vijaññaṃ .

[1038] yaṃ tvaṃ apāyesi bahū manusse    
pipāsite ghammani samparete    
tante purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ    
suttappabuddhova anussarāmi .

[1039] yaṃ eṇikūlasmiṃ janataṃ gahītaṃ    
amocayī gayhaka nīyamānaṃ    
tante purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ    
suttappabuddhova anussarāmi .

[1040] gaṅgāya sotasmiṃ gahītanāvaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 223 -->
<a id="page-223"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 223]*

Luddena nāgena manussakappā    
amocayī tvaṃ balasā pasayha    
tante purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ    
suttappabuddhova anussarāmi .

[1041] kappo ca te patthacaro ahosiṃ [^223-1]    
sambuddhivantaṃ vatitaṃ [^223-2] amaññaṃ    
tante purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ    
suttappabuddhova anussarāmi .

[1042] addhā pajānāsi mametamāyuṃ    
aññaṃpi jānāsi tathāhi buddho    
tathāhi tāyaṃ jalitānubhāvo    
obhāsayaṃ tiṭṭhati brahmalokanti .

Bakabrahmajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Kukkuvaggo paṭhamo .

tassuddānaṃ    
varakaṇṇika cāpavaro sutano    
atha gijjha sarohitamacchavaro    
puna dasaṇṇaka [^223-3] senaka yācanako    
atha verī sabrahmabakena dasa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 224 -->
<a id="page-224"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 224]*

[^224-2] gandhāravaggo    
[^224-1] gandhārajātakaṃ    
[1043] hitvā gāmasahassāni         paripuṇṇāni soḷasa    
koṭṭhāgārāni phītāni        sannidhindāni kubbasi .

[1044] hitvā gandhāravisayaṃ          pahūtadhanapāniyaṃ [^224-1]    
pasāsanito [^224-2] nikkhanto      idhadāni pasāsasi .

[1045] dhammaṃ bhaṇāmi vedeha         adhammo me na ruccati    
dhammaṃ me bhaṇamānassa         na pāpamupalimpati .

[1046] yenakenaci vaṇṇena          paro labhati ruppanaṃ    
mahatthiyampi ce vācaṃ         na taṃ bhāseyya paṇḍito .

[1047] kāmaṃ ruppatu vā mā vā      bhūsaṃ vā vikiriyyatu    
dhammaṃ me bhaṇamānassa         na pāpamupalimpati .

[1048] no ce assa sakā buddhi      vinayo vā susikkhito    
vane andhamahiṃsova [^224-3]        careyya bahuko jano .

[1049] yasmā ca panidhekacce        āceramhi [^224-4] susikkhitā    
tasmā vinītavinayā dhīrā [^224-5]    caranti susamāhitāti .

Gandhārajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

#[^224-1] sī. Yu. Pahutadhanadhāniyaṃ . Ma. Pahūtadhanadhāriyaṃ .  [^224-2] ma. Pasāsanato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 225 -->
<a id="page-225"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 225]*

[^225-2] mahākapijātakaṃ    
[1050] attānaṃ saṅkamaṃ katvā        yo sotthiṃ samatārayi    
kiṃ tvaṃ tesaṃ kiṃme [^225-1] tuyhaṃ    honti hete [^225-2] mahākapi .

[1051] rājāhaṃ issaro tesaṃ        yūthassa parihārako    
tesaṃ sokaparetānaṃ          bhiṃtānante arindama .

[1052] ullaṅghayitvā attānaṃ        vissaṭṭhadhanuno sataṃ    
tato aparapādesu           daḷhaṃ bandhaṃ latāgulaṃ [^225-3] .

[1053] chinnabbhamiva vātena          nuṇṇo rukkhamupāgamiṃ    
sohaṃ appabhavaṃ tattha          sākhaṃ hatthehi aggahiṃ .

[1054] taṃ maṃ vīṇāyataṃ santaṃ          sākhāya ca latāya ca    
samanukkamantā pādehi        sotthiṃ sākhamigā gatā .

[1055] taṃ maṃ na tappatī [^225-4] bandho     vadho [^225-5] me na tapessati่    
sukhamāharitaṃ tesaṃ            yesaṃ rajjamakārayiṃ .

[1056] esā te upamā rāja       taṃ suṇāhi arindamaṃ [^225-6]    
raññā raṭṭhassa yoggassa      balassa nigamassa ca    
sabbesaṃ sukhametabbaṃ          khattiyena pajānatāti .

Mahākapijātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

#[^225-1] ma. Kime . Sī. Yu. Kimo .  [^225-2] ma. Ete .  [^225-3] ma. Latāgunaṃ .  [^225-4] ma. Tapate .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 226 -->
<a id="page-226"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 226]*

[^226-3] kumbhakārajātakaṃ    
[1057] ambāhamaddaṃ vanamantarasmiṃ    
nīlobhāsaṃ phalitaṃ [^226-1] saṃvirūḷhaṃ    
tamaddasaṃ phalahetu vibhaggaṃ    
taṃ disvā bhikkhācariyaṃ carāmi .

[1058] selaṃ sumaṭṭhaṃ naravīraniṭṭhitaṃ    
nārī yugaṃ dhārayi appasaddaṃ    
dutiyañca āgamma ahosi saddo    
taṃ disvā bhikkhācariyaṃ carāmi .

[1059] dijā dijaṃ kuṇapamāharantaṃ    
ekaṃ samānaṃ bahukā samecca    
āhārahetu paripātayiṃsu    
taṃ disvā bhikkhācariyaṃ carāmi .

[1060] usabhāhamaddaṃ yūthassa majjhe    
valakkakaka [^226-2] vaṇṇabalūpapannaṃ    
tamaddasaṃ kāmahetu vitunnaṃ    
taṃ disvā bhikkhācariyaṃ carāmi .

[1061] karaṇḍako [^226-3] kaliṅgānaṃ       gandhārānañca naggaji    
nimirājā videhānaṃ          pañcālānañca dummukho    
ete raṭṭhāni hitvāna       pabbajiṃsu akiñcanā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 227 -->
<a id="page-227"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 227]*

[1062] sabbepime devasamā samāgatā    
aggi yathā pajjalito tathevime    
ahaṃpi eko carissāmi bhaggavi    
hitvāna kāmāni yathodhikāni .

[1063] ayameva kālo na hi añño atthi    
anusāsito me na bhaveyya pacchā [^227-1]    
ahampi ekā carissāmi bhaggava    
sakuṇīva muttā purisassa hatthā .

[1064] āmaṃ pakkañca jānanti        atho loṇaṃ aloṇakaṃ    
tamahaṃ disvāna pabbajiṃ         careva tvaṃ carāmahanti .

Kumbhakārajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^227-4] daḷhadhammajātakaṃ    
[1065] ahañca [^227-2] daḷhadhammassa [^227-3]    vahantī nābhirādhayiṃ    
nudantī [^227-4] urasi sallaṃ        yuddhe vikkantacārinī .

[1066] nūna [^227-5] rājā na jānāti     mama vikkamaporisaṃ    
saṅgāme sukatantāni         dūtavippahitāni ca .

[1067] sā nūnāhaṃ marissāmi         abandhu aparāyinī    
#[^227-1] sī. Anusāsitā ye na bhaveyya pacchā .  [^227-2] ma. Ahaṃ ce .  [^227-3] yu. Daḷhadhammāya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 228 -->
<a id="page-228"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 228]*

Tadā hi [^228-1] kumbhakārassa      dinnā chakaṇahārikā .

[1068] yāvatāsiṃsatī poso          tāvadeva pavīṇati    
atthāpāye jahanti naṃ        oṭṭhibyādhiṃva khattiyo .

[1069] yo pubbe katakalyāṇo       katattho nāvabujjhati    
atthā tassa palujjanti        ye honti abhipatthitā .

[1070] yo pubbe katakalyāṇo       katattho manubujjhati    
atthā tassa pavaḍḍhanti        ye honti abhipatthitā .

[1071] taṃ vo vadāmi bhaddaṃ vo [^228-2]    yāvantettha samāgatā    
sabbe kataññuno hotha        ciraṃ saggamhi ṭhassathāti .

Daḷhadhammajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^228-5] somadattajātakaṃ    
[1072] yo maṃ pure paccudeti [^228-3]     araññe dūramāyato [^228-4]    
so na dissati mātaṅgo       somadatto kuhiṃ gato .

[1073] ayaṃ vā so mato seti       allapitaṃva vicchito [^228-5]    
bhūmyā nipatito seti         amarā vata kuñjaro .

[1074] anagāriyupetassa            vippamuttassa te sato    
samaṇassa na taṃ sādhu          yaṃ petamanusocati .

[1075] saṃvāsena have sakka         manussassa migassa vā    
#[^228-1] yu. Tathāhi .  [^228-2] ma. Bhaddante .  [^228-3] ma. Paccuḍḍeti .  [^228-4] sī. Dūramāgato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 229 -->
<a id="page-229"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 229]*

Hadaye jāyatī pemaṃ          na taṃ sakkā asocituṃ .

[1076] mataṃ marissaṃ rodanti          ye rudanti lapanti ca    
tasmā tvaṃ isi mā rodi      roditaṃ moghamāhu santo .

[1077] kanditena have brahme       mato peto samuṭṭhahe    
sabbe saṅgamma rodāma       aññamaññassa ñātake .

[1078] ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ         ghatasittaṃva pāvakaṃ    
vārinā viya osiñci [^229-1]      sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ .

Abbūḷhaṃ [^229-2] vata me sallaṃ     yamāsi hadayassitaṃ    
yo me sokaparetassa        puttasokaṃ apānudi .

Sohaṃ abbūḷhasallosmi        vītasoko anāvilo    
na socāmi na rodāmi        tava sutvāna vāsavāti .

Somadattajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^229-6] susīmajātakaṃ    
[1079] kāḷāni kesāni pure ahesuṃ    
jātāni sīsamhi yathāpadese    
tānijja [^229-3] setāni susīma disvā    
dhammaṃ cara brahmacariyassa kālo .

[1080] mameva deva palitaṃ na tuyhaṃ  
mameva sīsaṃ mama uttamaṅgaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 230]*

Atthaṃ karissanti musā abhāṇiṃ    
ekāparādhaṃ khamatha [^230-1] rājaseṭṭha .

[1081] daharo tvaṃ dassanīyosi rāja    
paṭhamuggato hosi yathā kalīro    
rajjañca kārehi mamañca passa    
mā kālikaṃ anudhāvi janinda .

[1082] passāmi vohaṃ dahariṃ kumāriṃ    
sāmaṭṭhapassaṃ sutanana sumajjhaṃ    
kālappallavāva [^230-2] pavellamānā    
palobhayantīva [^230-3] naresu gacchati .

[1083] tamena passāmi parena nāriṃ    
āsītikaṃ nāvutikañca jaccā    
daṇḍaṃ gahetvāna pavedhamānaṃ    
gopāṇasībhaggasamaṃ carantaṃ [^230-4] .

[1084] sohaṃ tamevānuvicintayanto    
eko sayāmi sayanassa majjhe    
ahampi evaṃ iti pekkhamāno    
gehe na rame [^230-5] brahmacariyassa kālo .

[1085] rajju vālambanī cesā        yā gehe vasato rati    
#[^230-1] ma. Khama .  [^230-2] sī. Kālāpavālāva . Ma. Kālappavāḷāva .  [^230-3] yu. Sā lobhayantīva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 231]*

Etaṃpi chetvāna vajanti dhīrā    
anapekkhino kāmasukhaṃ pahāyāti .

Susīmajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^231-7] koṭasimbalijātakaṃ    
[1086] ahaṃ dasasatabyāmaṃ            uragamādāya āgato    
tañca mañca mahākāyaṃ         dhārayaṃ nappavedhasi .

[1087] athimaṃ khuddakaṃ pakkhiṃ           appamaṃsataraṃ mayā    
dhārayaṃ byathasi bhīto [^231-1]       kimatthaṃ [^231-2] koṭasimbali [^231-3] .

[1088] maṃsabhakkho tuvaṃ rāja          phalabhakkho ayaṃ dijo    
ayaṃ nigrodhavījāni           milakkhudumbarāni ca    
assatthāni ca bhakkhetvā [^231-4]   khandhe me odahissati .

[1089] te rukkhā saṃvirūhanti         mama passe nivātajā    
te maṃ pariyonaddhissanti       arukkhaṃ maṃ karissare .

[1090] santi aññepi rukkhāse       mūlino khandhino dumā    
iminā sakuṇajātena          vījamāharitvā hatā .

[1091] ajjhārūhā hi [^231-5] vaḍḍhanti     brahantaṃpi vanappatiṃ    
tasmā rāja pavedhāmi        sampassaṃ nāgataṃ bhayaṃ .

[1092] saṅkeyya saṅkitabbāni        rakkheyyānāgataṃ bhayaṃ    
#[^231-1] ma. Bhītā .  [^231-2] ma. Kamatthaṃ .  [^231-3] sī. Yu. Koṭisimbali .  [^231-4] ma. Bhakkhitvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 232 -->
<a id="page-232"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 232]*

Anāgatabhayā dhīro           ubho loke avekkhatīti .

Koṭasimbalijātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^232-8] dhūmakārijātakaṃ    
[1093] rājā apucchi vidhūraṃ          dhammakāmo yudhiṭṭhilo    
api brāhmaṇa jānāsi        ko eko bahu socati .

[1094] brāhmaṇo ajayūthena         bahutendo [^232-1] vane vasaṃ    
dhūmaṃ akāsi vāseṭṭho        rattindivamatandito .

[1095] tassa taṃdhūmagandhena           sarabhā makasadditā [^232-2]    
vassāvāsaṃ upagacchacha          dhūmakārissa santike .

[1096] sarabhesu manaṃ katvā          ajā so nāvabujjhatha    
āgacchanti vajanti vā        tassa tā vinasuṃ [^232-3] ajā .

[1097] sarabhā sarade kāle         pahīnamakase vane    
pāviṃsu giriduggāni           nadīnaṃ pabhavāni ca .

[1098] sarabhe ca gate disvā        ajā ca vibhavaṃ gatā    
kīso ca vivaṇṇo āsi        paṇḍurogī ca brāhmaṇo .

[1099] evaṃ yo sanniraṅkatvā       āgantuṃ kurute piyaṃ    
so eko bahu socati        dhūmakārīva brāhmaṇoti .

Dhūmakārijātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 233]*

[^233-9] jāgarajātakaṃ    
[1100] kodha jāgarataṃ sutto         kodha suttesu jāgaro    
ko metaṃ nu [^233-1] vijānāti     ko taṃ paṭibhaṇāti me .

[1101] ahaṃ jāgarataṃ sutto          ahaṃ suttesu jāgaro    
ahametaṃ vijānāmi           ahaṃ paṭibhaṇāmi te .

[1102] kathaṃ jāgarataṃ sutto          kathaṃ suttesu jāgaro    
kathaṃ etaṃ vijānāsi          kathaṃ paṭibhaṇāsi me .

[1103] ye dhammaṃ nappajānanti        saññamoti damoti ca    
tesu suttappamādesu [^233-2]      ahaṃ jaggāmi devate .

[1104] yesaṃ rāgo ca doso ca      avijjā ca virājitā    
tesu jāgaramānesu          ahaṃ suttosmi devate .

[1105] evaṃ jāgarataṃ sutto         evaṃ suttesu jāgaro    
evametaṃ vijānāmi          evaṃ paṭibhaṇāmi te .

[1106] sādhu jāgarataṃ sutto         sādhu suttesu jāgaro    
sādhu metaṃ vijānāsi         sādhu paṭibhaṇāsi meti .

Jāgarajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 kummāsapiṇḍajātakaṃ [^233-3]    
[1107] na kiratthi anomadassīsu    
pāricariyā buddhesu appakā [^233-4]    
#[^233-1] ma. Ko mametaṃ ... .  [^233-2] ma. Suppamāṇesu .  [^233-3] ma. Kummāsapiṇḍi ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 234]*

Sukkhāya aloṇikāya ca    
passa phalaṃ kummāsapiṇḍiyā .

Hatthī gavāssā cime bahū    
dhanadhaññā paṭhavī ca kevalā    
nāriyo cimā accharūpamā    
passa phalaṃ kummāsapiṇḍiyā .

[1108] abhikkhaṇaṃ rājakuñjara    
gāthā bhāsasi kosalādhipa    
pucchāmi taṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhana    
bāḷhaṃ pītimano pabhāsasi .

[1109] imasmiṃyeva nagare           kule aññatare ahuṃ    
parakammakaro āsiṃ           bhatako sīlasaṃvuto .

Kammāya nikkhamantāhaṃ [^234-1]      caturo samaṇaddasaṃ    
ācārasīlasampanne          sītibhūte anāsave .

Tesu cittaṃ pasādetvā       nisīdetvā paṇṇasanthate    
adaṃ buddhāna kummāsaṃ         pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Tassa kammassa kusalassa        idaṃ me edisaṃ phalaṃ    
anubhomi idaṃ rajjaṃ           phītaṃ dharaṇimuttamaṃ .

[1110] dadaṃ bhuñja ca mā ca pamādo  
cakkaṃ vattaya kosalādhipa  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 235 -->
<a id="page-235"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 235]*

mā rāja adhammiko ahu    
dhammaṃ pālaya kosalādhipa .

[1111] sohaṃ tadeva punappunaṃ    
vaṭumaṃ ācarissāmi sobhane    
ariyācaritaṃ sukosale    
arahanto me manāpāva passituṃ .

[1112] devī viya accharūpamā    
majjhe nārigaṇassa sobhasi    
kiṃ kammamakāsi bhaddakaṃ    
kenāsi vaṇṇavatī sukosale .

[1113] ambaṭṭhakulassa khattiya    
dāsyāhaṃ parapesiyā ahuṃ    
saññatā ca dhammajīvinī    
sīlavatī ca apāpadassanā .

Uddhaṭabhattaṃ ahaṃ tadāๆ    
caramānassa adāsi bhikkhuno    
cittā sumanā sayaṃ ahaṃ    
tassa kammassa phalaṃ mamedisanti .

Kummāsapiṇḍajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 236]*

1[^236-1] parantapajātakaṃ    
[1114] āgamissati me pāpaṃ         āgamissati me bhayaṃ    
tadā hi calitā sākhā        manussena migena vā .

[1115] bhīruyā nūna me kāmo        avidūre vasantiyā    
karissati kisaṃ paṇḍaḍa           sāva sākhā parantapaṃ .

[1116] socayissati maṃ kantā         gāme vasaṃ aninditā    
karissati kisaṃ paṇḍaḍa           sāva sākhā parantapaṃ .

[1117] tayā maṃ hasitāpaṅgī [^236-1]       mihitāni [^236-2] bhaṇitāni ca    
kisaṃ paṇḍaḍa karissati           sāva sākhā parantapaṃ .

[1118] agamā nūna so saddo        asaṃsi nūna so tava    
akkhātaṃ nūna taṃ tena         yo taṃ sākhamakampayi .

[1119] idaṃ kho taṃ samāgamma         mama bālassa cintitaṃ    
tadā hi calitā sākhā        manussena migena vā .

[1120] tatheva tvaṃ avedasi          avañci pitaraṃ mama    
hantvā sākhāhi chādento    āgamissati te [^236-3] bhayanti .

Parantapajātakaṃ ekādasamaṃ .

Gandhāravaggo dutiyo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 237 -->
<a id="page-237"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 237]*

tassuddānaṃ    
varagāma mahākapi bhaggava ca    
daḷhadhamma sakuñjara kesavaro    
urago vidhūro puna jāgarataṃ    
atha kosalādhipa parantapa ca .

tatra vagguddānaṃ bhavati .

Atha sattanipātamhi          vaggaṃ me bhaṇato suṇa    
kukku ca puna gandhāro       dve ca vuttā mahesinā .

Sattakanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 238 -->
<a id="page-238"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 238]*

Aṭṭhakanipātajātakaṃ    
[^238-1] kaccānivaggo    
[^238-1] kaccānijātakaṃ    
[1121] odātavatthā suci allakesā    
kaccāni kiṃ kumbhimadhissayitvā [^238-1]    
piṭṭhā tilā dhovasi taṇḍulāni    
tilodano hehiti kissa hetu .

[1122] na kho ayaṃ brāhmaṇa bhojanatthaṃ [^238-2]    
tilodano hehiti sādhupakko8    
dhammo mato tassa pahūnamajja [^238-3]    
ahaṃ karissāmi susānamajjhe .

[1123] anuvicca kaccāni karohi kiccaṃ    
dhammo mato ko nu tavetasaṃsi [^238-4]    
sahassanetto atulānubhāvo    
na miyyatī dhammavaro kadāci .

[1124] daḷhappamāṇaṃ mama ettha brahme    
dhammo mato natthi mamettha kaṅkhā    
ye yevadāni pāpā bhavanti    
#[^238-1] yu. Kumbhimapassayitvā .  [^238-2] sī. Ma. Bhojanatthā .  [^238-3] sī. Pahūtamajja .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 239 -->
<a id="page-239"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 239]*

Te tevadāni sukhitā bhavanti .

Suṇisā hi mayhaṃ vajjhā ahosi    
sā maṃ vadhitvāna vijāyi puttaṃ    
sādāni sabbassa kulassa issarā    
ahaṃ vasāmi [^239-1] apaviṭṭhā ekikā .

[1125] jīvāmi vohaṃ na matohamasmi [^239-2]    
taveva atthāya idhāgatosmi    
yā taṃ vadhitvāna vijāyi puttaṃ    
sahāva puttena karomi bhasmaṃ .

[1126] evañca te ruccati devarāja    
mameva atthāya idhāgatosi    
ahañca putto suṇisā ca nattā    
sammodamānā gharamāvasema .

[1127] evañca te ruccati kātiyāni    
hatāpi santā na jahāsi dhammaṃ    
tuvañca putto suṇisā ca nattā    
sammodamānā gharamāvasetha .

[1128] sā kātiyānī suṇisāya saddhiṃ  
sammodamānā gharamāvasittha  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 240 -->
<a id="page-240"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 240]*

Putto ca nattā ca upaṭṭhahiṃsu .

Devānamindena adhiggahītāti .

Kaccānijātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^240-2] aṭṭhasaddajātakaṃ    
[1129] idaṃ pure ninnamāhu          bahumacchaṃ mahodakaṃ    
āvāso bakarājassa         pettikaṃ bhavanaṃ mama    
tyajja bhiṅgena [^240-1] yāpema    okaṃ na vijjahāmase [^240-2]    
[1130] ko dutiyaṃ asīlissa           bandhurassakkhi bhejjati [^240-3]    
ko me putte kulāvakaṃ       mañca sotthiṃ karissati .

[1131] sabbā parikkhatā pheggu       yāva tassā gatī ahu    
khīṇabhakkho mahārāja          sāre na ramatī ghuṇo .

[1132] sā nūnāhaṃ ito gantvā      rañño muttā nivesanā    
attānaṃ ramayissāmi          dumasākhāniketinī [^240-4] .

[1133] so nūnāhaṃ ito gantvā      rañño mutto nivesanā    
aggodakāni pivissāmi        yūthassa purato vajaṃ .

[1134] taṃ maṃ kāmehi sammattaṃ        rattaṃ kāmesu mucchitaṃ    
ānayī bharato luddho [^240-5]      bāhiko bhaddamatthu te .

[1135] andhakāratimisāyaṃ            tuṅge upari pabbate    
#[^240-1] sī. Ma. Bhekena .  [^240-2] ma. Vajahāmase .  [^240-3] ma. Bandharassakkhi bhecchati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 241 -->
<a id="page-241"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 241]*

Sā maṃ saṇhena mudunā        mā pādaṃ khaṇiyasmani [^241-1] .

[1136] asaṃsayaṃ jātikhayantadassī    
na gabbhaseyyaṃ punarāvaṭissaṃ [^241-2]    
ayamantimā pacchimagabbhaseyyā [^241-3]    
khīṇo me saṃsāro punabbhavāyāti .

Aṭṭhasaddajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^241-3] sulasājātakaṃ    
[1137] idaṃ suvaṇṇakāyūraṃ            muttā veḷuriyā bahū    
sabbaṃ harassu bhaddante        mañca dāsīti sāvaya .

[1138] oropayassu kalyāṇiṃ [^241-4]      mā bahuṃ [^241-5] paridevasi    
na cāhaṃ abhijānāmi          āgantvā [^241-6] dhanamābhataṃ .

[1139] yato sarāmi attānaṃ         yato pattāsmi viññutaṃ    
nevāhaṃ [^241-7] abhijānāmi       aññaṃ piyataraṃ tayā .

[1140] ehi taṃ upagūhissaṃ           karissaṃ ca padakkhiṇaṃ    
na hidāni puna atthi          mama tuyhañca saṅgamo .

[1141] na hi sabbesu ṭhānesu        puriso hoti paṇḍito    
itthīpi paṇḍitā hoti         tattha tattha vicakkhaṇā .

[1142] na hi sabbesu ṭhānesu        puriso hoti paṇḍito    
#[^241-1] ma. Khali yasmani .  [^241-2] ma. Punarāvajissaṃ .  [^241-3] sī. Yu. Ayaṃ hime antimā    
#gabbhaseyyā . [^241-4] ma. Kalyāṇi .  [^241-5] ma. Mā bāḷhaṃ .  [^241-6] ma. Ahantvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 242 -->
<a id="page-242"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 242]*

Itthīpi paṇḍitā hoti         lahumatthaṃ vicintikā .

[1143] lahuñca vata khippañca          nikaṭṭhe samacetayi    
migaṃ puṇṇāyataneva [^242-1]        sulasā sattukaṃ vadhi .

[1144] yodha uppatitaṃ atthaṃ          na khippamanubujjhati    
so haññate mandamatī         corova girigabbhare .

[1145] yodha [^242-2] uppatitaṃ atthaṃ       khippameva nibodhati    
muccate sattusambādhā        sulasā sattukāmivāti .

Sulasājātakaṃ tatiyaṃ    
[^242-4] sumaṅgalajātakaṃ    
[1146] bhusamhi kuddhoti apekkhiyāna    
na tāva daṇḍaṃ panayeyya issaro    
aṭṭhānaso appaṭirūpamattano    
parassa dukkhāni bhusaṃ udīraye .

[1147] yato ca jāneyya pasādamattano    
atthaṃ niyuñjeyya parassa dukkaṭaṃ    
tadāyamatthoti sayaṃ avekkhiya    
athassa daṇḍaṃ sadisaṃ nivesaye .

[1148] na cāpi jhāpeti paraṃ na attanaṃ  
amucchito yo nayate nayānayaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 243 -->
<a id="page-243"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 243]*

Yo daṇḍadhāro bhavatīdha issaro    
sa vaṇṇagutto siriyā na dhaṃsati .

[1149] ye khattiyāse anisammakārino    
panenti daṇḍaṃ sahasā pamucchitā    
avaṇṇasaṃyuttā [^243-1] jahanti jīvitaṃ    
ito vimuttāpica yanti duggatiṃ .

[1150] dhamme ca ye ariyappavedite ratā    
anuttarā te vacasā manasā kammunā ca    
te khantisoraccasamādhisaṇṭhitā [^243-2]    
vajanti lokaṃ dubhayaṃ tathāvidhā .

[1151] rājāhamasmi narapamudānamissaro    
sacepi kujjhāmi ṭhapemi attanaṃ    
nisedhayanto janataṃ tathāvidhaṃ    
panemi daṇḍaṃ anukampa yoniso .

[1152] sirī ca lakkhī ca taveva khattiya    
janādhipa mā vijahi kudācanaṃ    
akkodhano niccapasannacitto    
anigho tuvaṃ vassasatāni pālaya .

[1153] guṇehi etehi upehi [^243-3] khattiya  
ṭhitamariyavutti [^243-4] suvaco akodhano  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 244 -->
<a id="page-244"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 244]*

Sukhī anuppīḷa pasāsa medaniṃ    
ito vimuttopi vajāhi suggatiṃ .

[1154] evaṃ sunītena [^244-1] subhāsitena    
dhammena ñāyena upāyaso nayaṃ    
nibbāpaye saṅkhubhitaṃ mahājanaṃ    
mahāva megho salilena medaninti [^244-2] .

Sumaṅgalajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^244-5] gaṅgamālajātakaṃ    
[1155] aṅgārajātā paṭhavī          kukkulānugatā mahī    
atha gāyasi vattāni          na taṃ tapati ātapo .

Uddhaṃ tapati ādicco         adho tapati vālukā    
atha gāyasi vattāni          na taṃ tapati ātapo .

[1156] na maṃ tapati ātapo          ātappā tapayanti maṃ    
atthā hi vividhā rāja        te tapanti na ātapo .

[1157] addasaṃ kāma te mūlaṃ         saṅkappā kāma jāyasi    
na taṃ saṅkappayissāmi         evaṃ kāma na hehisi .

[1158] appāpi kāmā na alaṃ        bahūhipi na tappati    
aho bālānalapanā [^244-3]       paṭivajjetha [^244-4] jaggato .

#[^244-1] yu. Surinītena .  [^244-2] ma. Medininti .  [^244-3] sī. Ma. Ahahā bālalapanā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 245 -->
<a id="page-245"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 245]*

[1159] appassa kammassa phalaṃ mamedaṃ    
udayo ajjhagamā mahattapattaṃ    
suladdhalābho vata māṇavassa    
yo pabbaji kāmarāgaṃ pahāya .

[1160] tapasā pajahanti pāpakammaṃ    
tapasā nhāpitakujjhakārabhāvaṃ [^245-1]    
tapasā abhibhuyya gaṅgamāla    
nāmenālapasijja brahmadattaṃ .

[1161] sandiṭṭhikameva amma passatha    
khantisoraccassa yo [^245-2] vipāko    
so [^245-3] sabbajanassa vandito ahu    
taṃ vandāma sarājikā samaccā .

[1162] mā kiñci avacuttha gaṅgamālaṃ    
muninaṃ monapathesu sikkhamānaṃ    
eso hi atari aṇṇavaṃ    
yaṃ taritvā vicaranti [^245-4] vītasokāti .

Gaṅgamālajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 246 -->
<a id="page-246"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 246]*

[^246-6] cetiyarājajātakaṃ    
[1163] dhammo have hato hanti       nāhato hanti kiñcinaṃ [^246-1]    
tasmā hi dhammaṃ na hane       mā taṃ [^246-2] dhammo hato hani .

[1164] alikaṃ bhāsamānassa           apakkamanti devatā    
pūtikañca mukhaṃ vāti           sakaṭṭhānā ca dhaṃsati    
yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ       aññathā naṃ viyākare    
sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi          hohi rāja yathā pure    
musā ce bhāsase rāja       bhūmiyaṃ tiṭṭha cetiya .

[1165] akāle vassatī tassa         kāle tassa na vassati    
yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ       aññathā naṃ viyākare    
sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi          hohi rāja yathā pure    
musā ce bhāsase rāja       bhūmiṃ pavisa cetiya .

[1166] jivhā tassa dvidhā hoti      uragasseva disampati    
yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ       aññathā naṃ viyākare    
sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi          hohi rāja yathā pure    
musā ce bhāsase rāja       bhiyyo pavisa cetiya .

[1167] jivhā tassa na bhavati         macchasseva disampati    
yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ       aññathā naṃ viyākare    
sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi          hohi rāja yathā pure    
musā ce bhāsase rāja       bhiyyo pavisa cetiya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 247 -->
<a id="page-247"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 247]*

[1168] thiyova tassa jāyanti         na pumā jāyare kule    
yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ       aññathā naṃ viyākare    
sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi          hohi rāja yathā pure    
musā ce bhāsase rāja       bhiyyo pavisa cetiya .

[1169] puttā tassa na bhavanti        pakkamanti disodisaṃ    
yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ       aññathā naṃ viyākare    
sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi          hohi rāja yathā pure    
musā ce bhāsase rāja       bhiyyo pavisa cetiya .

[1170] sa rājā iminā patto [^247-1]    antalikkhacaro pure    
pāvekkhi paṭhaviṃ pecco       hīnatto attapariyāyaṃ [^247-2]    
tasmā hi chandāgamanaṃ         nappasaṃsanti paṇḍitā    
aduṭṭhacitto bhāseyya        giraṃ saccūpasañhitanti .

Cetiyarājajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^247-7] indriyajātakaṃ    
[1171] yo indriyānaṃ kāmena       vasaṃ nārada gacchati    
so pariccajjubho loke       jīvantopi [^247-3] visussati .

[1172] sukhassānantaraṃ dukkhaṃ          dukkhassānantaraṃ sukhaṃ    
sopi patto sukhadukkhaṃ         pāṭikaṅkha varaṃ sukhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 248 -->
<a id="page-248"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 248]*

[1173] kicchakāle kicchasaho         yo kicchaṃ nātivattati    
sa kicchantaṃ sukhaṃ dhīro         yogaṃ samadhigacchati .

[1174] na heva kāmānaṃ kāmā       nānatthā natthakāraṇā    
na katañca niraṅkatvā         dhammā cavitumarahasi .

[1175] dakkhaṃ gahapataṃ [^248-1] sādhu        saṃvibhajjañca bhojanaṃ    
ahāso atthalābhesu         atthabyāpatti abyatho .

[1176] ettāvatetaṃ paṇḍiccaṃ        asito [^248-2] devilo bravi    
nayito kiñci pāpiyo [3]    indriyānaṃ vasaṃ vaje .

[1177] amittānaṃva hatthatthaṃ          sīvi pappoti māmiva    
kammaṃ vijjañca dakkheyyaṃ       vivāhaṃ sīlamaddavaṃ    
ete ca yase hāpetvā     nibbatto sehi kammehi .

[1178] sohaṃ sahassajīnova           abandhu aparāyano    
ariyadhammā apakkanto        yathā peto tathevahaṃ .

[1179] sukhakāme dukkhāpetvā       āpannosmi padaṃ imaṃ    
so sukhaṃ nādhigacchāmi         ṭhito [^248-4] bhāṇumahimivāti [^248-5] .

Indriyajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^248-8] ādittajātakaṃ    
[1180] ādittasmiṃ agārasmiṃ         yaṃ nīharati bhājanaṃ    
#[^248-1] ma. Gahapatī .  [^248-2] ma. Apiso .  [^248-3] ma. Yo. .  [^248-4] sī. Yu. Cito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 249 -->
<a id="page-249"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 249]*

Taṃ tassa hoti atthāya        no ca yaṃ tattha ḍayhati .

[1181] evamādipito loko         jarāya maraṇena ca    
nīharetheva dānena          dinnaṃ hoti sunībhataṃ [^249-1] .

[1182] yo dhammaladdhassa dadāti dānaṃ    
uṭṭhānaviriyādhigatassa jantu    
atikkamma so vetaraṇiṃ yamassa    
dibbāni ṭhānāni upeti macco .

[1183] dānañca yuddhañca samānamāhu    
appāpi santā bahuke jinanti    
appampi ce saddahāno dadāti    
teneva so hoti sukhī parattha .

[1184] viceyya dānaṃ sugatappasatthaṃ    
ye dakkhiṇeyyā idha jīvaloke    
etesu dinnāni mahapphalāni    
bījāni vuttāni yathā sukhette .

[1185] yo pāṇabhūtāni aheṭhayaṃ caraṃ    
parūpavādā na karoti pāpaṃ    
bhīruṃ pasaṃsanti na tattha sūraṃ    
bhayā hi santo na karonti pāpaṃ .

[1186] hīnena brahmacariyena         khattiye upapajjati  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 250 -->
<a id="page-250"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 250]*

Majjhimena ca devattaṃ         uttamena visujjhati .

[1187] addhā hi dānaṃ bahudhā pasatthaṃ    
dānā ca kho dhammapadaṃva seyyo    
pubbeva hi pubbatareva santo    
nibbānamevajjhagamama sapaññāti .

Ādittajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^250-9] aṭṭhānajātakaṃ    
[1188] gaṅgā kumudinī santā         saṅkhavaṇṇā ca kokilā    
jambu tālaphalaṃ dajjā         atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1189] yadā kacchapalomānaṃ          pāvāro tividho siyā    
hemantikaṃ pāpuraṇaṃ [^250-1]        atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1190] yadā makasapādānaṃ [^250-2]        aṭṭālo sukato siyā    
daḷho ca avikampī [^250-3] ca      atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1191] yadā sasavisāṇānaṃ           nisseṇi sukatā siyā    
saggassārohaṇatthāya         atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1192] yadā nissenimāruyha         candaṃ khādeyyu mūsikā    
rāhuñca paribāheyyuṃ [^250-4]      atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1193] yadā surāghaṭaṃ pitvā         makkhikā gaṇacārinī    
#[^250-1] ma. Pāvuraṇaṃ .  [^250-2] sī. Yu. Makasadāṭhānaṃ .  [^250-3] sī. Yu. Akampica .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 251 -->
<a id="page-251"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 251]*

Aṅgāre vāsaṃ kappeyyuṃ      atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1194] yadā bimboṭṭhasampanno       gadrabho sumukho siyā    
kusalo naccagītassa           atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1195] yadā kākā ulūkā ca        mantayeyyuṃ rahogatā    
aññamaññaṃ pihayeyyuṃ          atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1196] yadā mūlāsapattānaṃ [^251-1]       chattaṃ thirataraṃ siyā    
vassassa paṭighātāya          atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1197] yadā kuluko [^251-2] sakuṇo       pabbataṃ gandhamādanaṃ    
tuṇḍenādāya gaccheyya       atha nūna tadā siyā .

[1198] yadā sāmuddikaṃ nāvaṃ         sayantaṃ savaṭākaraṃ    
ceto ādāya gaccheyya      atha nūna tadā siyāti .

Aṭṭhānajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 dīpijātakaṃ    
[1199] khamanīyaṃ yāpanīyaṃ             kacci mātula te sukhaṃ    
sukhaṃ te ammā avaca         sukhakāmā hi [^251-3] te mayaṃ .

[1200] naṅguṭṭhaṃ me apakkamma [^251-4]     heṭhayitvāna eḷaki [^251-5]    
sājja mātulavādena         muñcitabbā nu maññasi .[^251-4]    
[1201] puratthābhimukho [^251-6] nisinnosi    ahante mukhamāgatā    
pacchato tuyhaṃ naṅguṭṭhaṃ        kathaṃ khohaṃ avakkami .

#[^251-1] ma. Muḷālapatutānaṃ . Sī. Yu. Pulasapattānaṃ .  [^251-2] ma. Kulako . Sī. Yu. Kluṅko .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 252 -->
<a id="page-252"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 252]*

[1202] yāvatā caturo dīpā         sasamuddā sapabbatā    
tāvatā mayhaṃ naṅguṭṭhaṃ        kathaṃ kho tvaṃ vivajjasi [^252-1] .

[1203] pubbeva metaṃ akkhiṃsu         mātā pitā ca bhātaro    
dīghaṃ duṭṭhassa naṅguṭṭhaṃ         sāmhi vehāyasāgatā .

[1204] tañca disvāna āyantiṃ        antalikkhasmi eḷaki [^252-2]    
migasaṅgho palāyittha          bhakkho me nāsito tayā .

[1205] iccevaṃ vilapantiyā          eḷakiyā ruhagghaso    
galakaṃ anvāvamaddi           natthi duṭṭhe subhāsitaṃ .

[1206] neva duṭṭhe nayo atthi       na dhammo na subhāsitaṃ    
nikkamaṃ duṭṭhe yuñjetha        so ca sabbhi na rajjatīti [^252-3] .

Dīpijātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

Kaccānivaggo .

tassuddānaṃ    
parisuddhāmanāvilā vatthadharā    
bakarājassa kāyuraṃ daṇḍavaro    
atha aṅgāra cetiya devilinā    
atha āditta gaṅgā daseḷakinā .

Aṭṭhakanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 253 -->
<a id="page-253"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 253]*

Navakanipātajātakaṃ    
[^253-1] gijjhajātakaṃ    
[1207] parisaṅkupatho nāma           gijjhapatho [^253-1] sanantano    
tatrāsi mātāpitaro         gijjho posesi jiṇṇake .

[1208] tesaṃ ajagaraṃ medaṃ           accahāsi bahutaso [^253-2]    
pitā ca puttaṃ avaca          jānaṃ uccaṃ papātinaṃ    
supattaṃ pakkhasampannaṃ [^253-3]       tejasiṃ dūragāminaṃ .

[1209] pariplavantaṃ paṭhaviṃ            yadā tāta vijānahi    
sāgarena parikkhittaṃ          cakkaṃva parimaṇḍalaṃ    
tato tāta nivattassu         māssu etto paraṃ gami .

[1210] udapattosi [^253-4] vegena       balī pakkhī dijuttamo    
olokayanto vaṅkaṅgo       pabbatāni vanāni ca .

[1211] addasa paṭhaviṃ gijjho          yathāssāsi pitussutaṃ    
sāgarena parikkhittaṃ          cakkaṃva parimaṇḍalaṃ .

[1212] tañca so samatikkamma         parameva pavattatha [^253-5]    
tañca vātasikhā tikkhā        accahāsi baliṃ dijaṃ .

[1213] nāsakkhātigato poso        punadeva nivattituṃ    
dijo byasanamāpādi          verabbhānaṃ [^253-6] vasaṃ gato .

#[^253-1] ma. Gijjhapantho .  [^253-2] sī. Pahuttato . Ma. Bahuttaso .  [^253-3] ma. Thāmasampannaṃ .

#[^253-4] sī. Uddhaṃ pattosi .  [^253-5] ma. Paramevaccavattatha .  [^253-6] ma. Verambhānaṃ . Sī. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 254 -->
<a id="page-254"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 254]*

[1214] tassa puttā ca dārā ca      ye caññe anujīvino    
sabbe byasanamāpāduṃ         anovādakare dije .

[1215] evampi idha vuḍḍhānaṃ         yo vākyaṃ nāvabujjhati    
atisīmacaro ditto           gijjho vātītasāsano    
sabbe byasanaṃ papponti [^254-1]    akatvā vuḍḍhasāsananti .

Gijjhajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^254-2] kosambiyajātakaṃ    
[1216] puthusaddo samajano           na bālo koci maññatha    
saṅghasmiṃ bhijjamānasmiṃ         nāññaṃ bhiyyo amaññaruṃ .

[1217] parimuṭṭhā paṇḍitābhāsā       vācāgocarabhāṇino    
yāvicchanti mukhāyāmaṃ         yena nītā na taṃ vidū .

[1218] akkocchi maṃ avadhi maṃ         ajini maṃ ahāsi me    
ye ca taṃ upanayhanti         veraṃ tesaṃ na sammati .

[1219] akkocchi maṃ avadhi maṃ         ajini maṃ ahāsi me    
ye ca taṃ nūpanayhanti         veraṃ tesūpasammati .

[1220] na hi verena verāni        sammantīdha kudācanaṃ    
averena ca sammanti         esa dhammo sanantano .

[1221] pare ca na vijānanti         mayamettha yamāmase    
ye ca tattha vijānanti        tato sammanti medhagā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 255 -->
<a id="page-255"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 255]*

[1222] aṭṭhicchinnā pāṇaharā        gavāssadhanahārino    
raṭṭhaṃ vilumpamānānaṃ          tesampi hoti saṅgati    
kasmā tumhākaṃ no siyā .

[1223] sace labhetha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ    
saddhiñcaraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ    
abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni    
careyya tenattamano satimā .

[1224] no ce labhetha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ    
saddhiñcaraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ    
rājāva raṭṭhaṃ vijitaṃ pahāya    
eko care mātaṅgaraññeva nāgo .

[1225] ekassa caritaṃ seyyo        natthi bāle sahāyatā    
eko care na ca [^255-1] pāpāni kayirā    
appossukko mātaṅgaraññeva nāgoti .

Kosambiyajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^255-3] mahāsuvarājajātakaṃ  
[1226] dumo yadā hoti phalūpapanno  
bhuñjanti naṃ vihaṅgamā [^255-2] sampatantā  
khīṇanti ñatvāna dumaṃ phalaccaye  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 256 -->
<a id="page-256"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 256]*

Disodisaṃ yanti tato vihaṅgamā .

[1227] cara cārikaṃ lohitatuṇḍa mā mari    
kiṃ tvaṃ suva sukkhadumamhi jhāyasi    
tadiṅgha [1] brūhi vassantasannibha    
kasmā suva sukkhadumaṃ na riñcasi .

[1228] ye ve sakhīnaṃ sakhāro bhavanti    
pāṇāccaye sukhadukkhesu haṃsa    
khīṇaṃ akhīṇanti na taṃ jahanti    
santo sataṃ dhammamanussarantā .

[1229] sohaṃ sataṃ aññatarosmi haṃsa    
ñātī ca me hoti sakhā ca rukkho    
taṃ nussahe jīvikattho pahātuṃ    
khīṇanti ñatvāna na esa dhammo .

[1230] sādhu pakkhi [^256-2] kataṃ hoti      metti saṃsati santhavo    
sacetaṃ dhammaṃ rocesi         pāsaṃsosi vijānataṃ .

[1231] so te suva varaṃ dammi        pattayāna vihaṅgama    
varaṃ varassu vaṅkaṅga          yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[1232] varañca me haṃsa tuvaṃ dadeyya  
ayañca rukkho punarāyuṃ labhetha  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 257 -->
<a id="page-257"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 257]*

So sākhavā phalimā saṃvirūḷho    
madhutthiko tiṭṭhatu sobhamāno .

[1233] taṃ passa samma phalimaṃ uḷāraṃ    
sahāva te hotu udumbarena    
so sākhavā phalimā saṃvirūḷho    
madhutthiko tiṭṭhatu sobhamāno .

[1234] evaṃ sakka sukhī hohi         saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
yathāhamajja sukhito           disvāna saphalaṃ dumaṃ .

[1235] suvassa vacanaṃ sutvā [^257-1]       katvāna saphalaṃ dumaṃ    
pakkāmi saha bhariyāya         devānaṃ nandanaṃ vananti .

Mahāsuvarājajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^257-4] cullasuvakarājajātakaṃ    
[1236] santi rukkhā haritapattā [^257-2]    dumānekaphalā bahū    
kasmā nu sukkhe koḷāpe     suvassa nirato mano .

[1237] phalassa upabhuñjimhā          nekavassagaṇe bahū    
aphalampi viditvāna           sāva mitti [^257-3] yathā pure .

[1238] sukkhañca rukkhakoḷāpaṃ         opattamaphalaṃ dumaṃ    
ohāya sakuṇā yanti         kiṃ dosaṃ maññase [^257-4] dija .

#[^257-1] sī. Ma. Suvassa ca varaṃ datvā . Evamuparipi .  [^257-2] ma. Haripattā .  [^257-3] ma. Metti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 258 -->
<a id="page-258"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 258]*

[1239] ye phalatthā sambhajanti        aphaloti jahanti naṃ    
attatthapaññā dummedhā       te honti pakkhapātino .

[1240] sādhu pakkhi kataṃ hoti         mitti [^258-1] saṃsati santhavo    
sacetaṃ dhamma rocesi         pāsaṃsosi vijānataṃ .

[1241] so te suva varaṃ dammi        pattayāna vihaṅgama    
varaṃ varassu vaṅkaṅga          yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[1242] api nāma naṃ puna passe [^258-2]    sapattaṃ saphalaṃ dumaṃ    
daliddova nidhiṃ laddhā         nandeyyāhaṃ punappunaṃ .

[1243] tato amatamādāya           abhisiñci mahīruhaṃ    
tassa sākhā virūhiṃsu          sītacchāyā manoramā .

[1244] evaṃ sakka sukhī hohi         saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
yathāhamajja sukhito           disvāna saphalaṃ dumaṃ .

[1245] suvassa vacanaṃ sutvā          katvāna saphalaṃ dumaṃ    
pakkāmi saha bhariyāya         devānaṃ nandanaṃ vananti .

Cullasuvakarājajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^258-5] haritacajātakaṃ    
[1246] sutametaṃ mahābrahme         kāme bhuñjati hārito    
kaccetaṃ vacanaṃ tucchaṃ          kacci suddho irīyasi .

[1247] evametaṃ mahārāja          yathā te vacanaṃ sutaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 259 -->
<a id="page-259"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 259]*

Kummaggaṃ paṭipannosmi         mohaneyyesu mucchito .

[1248] adu [^259-1] paññā kimatthikā      nipuṇā sādhucintinī [^259-2]    
yāya uppatitaṃ rāgaṃ          kiṃ mano na vinodaye .

[1249] cattārome mahārāja        loke atibalā bhusā    
rāgo doso mado moho     yattha paññā na gādhati .

[1250] arahā sīlasampanno          suddho carati hārito    
medhāvī paṇḍito ceva        iti no sammato bhavaṃ .

[1251] medhāvinampi hiṃsanti          isidhammaguṇe rataṃ    
vitakkā pāpakā rāja        subhā rāgūpasañhitā .

[1252] uppannoyaṃ [^259-3] sarīrajo       rāgo vaṇṇavidūsano [^259-4]    
tava taṃ pajaha namatthu [^259-5]       bahunāsi medhāvi sammato .

[1253] te andhakaraṇe [^259-6] kāme     bahudukkhe mahāvise    
tesaṃ mūlaṃ gavesissaṃ          chejjaṃ [^259-7] rāgaṃ sabandhanaṃ .

[1254] idaṃ vatvāna hārito         isi saccaparakkamo    
kāmarāgaṃ virājetvā        brahmalokūpago ahūti .

Haritacajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^259-6] padakusalamāṇavajātakaṃ    
[1255] bahussutaṃ cittakathaṃ            gaṅgā vahati pāṭaliṃ [^259-8]    
#[^259-1] sī. Yu. Ādu .  [^259-2] sī. Yu. Sādhucintanī .  [^259-3] ma. Uppannāyaṃ .

#[^259-4] ma. Rāgo vaṇṇavidūsano tava .  [^259-5] ma. Taṃ pajaha bhaddamatthu te .  [^259-6] ma. Andhakārake .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 260 -->
<a id="page-260"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 260]*

Vuyhamānaka bhaddante         ekaṃ me dehi gāthakaṃ .

[1256] yena siñcanti dukkhitaṃ         yena siñcanti āturaṃ    
tassa majjhe marissāmi        jātaṃ saraṇato bhayaṃ .

[1257] yattha vījāni rūhanti          sattā yattha patiṭṭhitā    
sā me sīsaṃ nipīḷeti         jātaṃ saraṇato bhayaṃ .

[1258] yena bhattāni paccanti        sītaṃ yena vihaññati    
so maṃ ḍahati gattāni         jātaṃ saraṇato bhayaṃ .

[1259] yena bhattena [^260-1] yāpenti    puthū brāhmaṇakhattiyā    
so maṃ bhutto byāpādi       jātaṃ saraṇato bhayaṃ .

[1260] gimhānaṃ pacchime māse       vātamicchanti paṇḍitā    
so maṃ bhañjati gattāni        jātaṃ saraṇato bhayaṃ .

[1261] yaṃ nissitā jagatiruhaṃ (vihaṅgamā) svāyaṃ aggiṃ pamuñcati    
disā bhajatha vaṅkaṅgā         jātaṃ saraṇato bhayaṃ .

[1262] yamānayiṃ somanassaṃ           māliniṃ candanussadaṃ    
sā maṃ gharā nīharati [^260-2]       jātaṃ saraṇato bhayaṃ .

[1263] yena jātena nandissaṃ        yassa ca bhavamicchisaṃ    
so maṃ gharā nīharati [^260-2]       jātaṃ saraṇato bhayaṃ .

[1264] suṇantu me jānapadā         negamā ca samāgatā  
yatodakaṃ tadādittaṃ           yato khemaṃ tato bhayaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 261 -->
<a id="page-261"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 261]*

Rājā vilumpate raṭṭhaṃ        brāhmaṇo ca purohito    
attaguttā viharatha           jātaṃ saraṇato bhayanti .

Padakusalamāṇavajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^261-7] lomasakassapajātakaṃ    
[1265] assa indasamo rāja         accantaṃ ajarāmaro    
sace tvaṃ yaññaṃ yajeyya       isiṃ lomasakassapaṃ .

[1266] sasamuddapariyāyaṃ             mahiṃ sāgarakuṇḍalaṃ    
na icche saha nindāya        evaṃ seyha [^261-1] vijānahi .

[1267] dhiratthu taṃ yasalābhaṃ           dhanalābhañca brāhmaṇa    
yā vutti vinipātena         adhammacaraṇena vā .

[1268] api ce pattamādāya         anāgāro paribbajeo    
sāyeva jīvikā seyyo       yā cādhammena esanā .

[1269] api ce pattamādāya         anāgāro paribbaje    
aññaṃ ahiṃsayaṃ loke          api rajjena taṃ varaṃ .

[1270] balaṃ cando balaṃ suriyo        balaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇā    
balaṃ velā samuddassa         balātibalamitthiyo .

[1271] yathā uggatapaṃ santaṃ          isiṃ lomasakassapaṃ    
pitu atthāya candavatī         vājapeyyaṃ ayājayi .

[1272] taṃ lobhapakataṃ kammaṃ           kaṭukaṃ kāmahetukaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 262 -->
<a id="page-262"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 262]*

Tassa mūlaṃ gavesissaṃ          chejjaṃ rāgaṃ sabandhanaṃ .

[1273] dhiratthu kāme subahūpi loke    
tapova seyyo kāmaguṇehi rāja    
tapo karissāmi pahāya kāme    
taveva raṭṭhaṃ candavatī ca hotūti .

Lomasakassapajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^262-8] cakkavākajātakaṃ    
[1274] kāsāyavatthe sakuṇe vadāmi    
duve duve nandamane [^262-1] carante    
kaṃ aṇḍajaṃ aṇḍajamānusesu    
jātiṃ pasaṃsanti tadiṅgha brūtha .

[1275] amhe manussesu manussahiṃsa    
anubbate cakkavāke vadanti    
kalyāṇabhāvamhe dijesu sammatā    
(abhītarūpā [^262-2] vicarāma aṇṇave)    
na ghāsahetupi karoma pāpaṃ [^262-3] .

[1276] kiṃ aṇṇave kāni phalāni bhuñje  
maṃsaṃ kuto khādatha cakkavākā  
kiṃ bhojanaṃ bhuñjatha vo anomā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 263 -->
<a id="page-263"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 263]*

Balañca vaṇṇo ca anapparūpo [^263-1] .

[1277] na aṇṇave santi phalāni dhaṅka    
maṃsaṃ kuto khādituṃ cakkavāke    
sevālabhakkhamha apāṇabhojanā    
(na ghāsahetupi karoma pāpaṃ)    
abhītarūpā vicarāma aṇṇave [^263-2] .

[1278] na me idaṃ ruccati cakkavāke [^263-3]    
asmiṃ bhave bhojanasannikāso    
ahosi pubbe tato me aññathā    
icceva me vimati ettha jātā .

[1279] ahampi maṃsāni phalāni bhuñje    
annāni ca loṇikatelikāni    
rasaṃ manussesu labhāmi bhottuṃ
