[1743] arānaṃ cakkanābhīnaṃ           īsānemirathassa ca    
sabbassa te kammaniyo        ayaṃ hessati phandano .

[1744] iti phandanarukkhopi           devatā [^345-3] ajjhabhāsatha    
mayhampi vacanaṃ atthi          bhāradvāja suṇohi me .

[1745] imassa [^345-4] upakkhandhamhā      ukkacca caturaṅgulaṃ    
tena nemiṃ pariharesi [^345-5]      evaṃ daḷhataraṃ siyā .

[1746] iti phandanarukkhopi           veraṃ kappesi devatā [^345-6]    
jātānañca ajātānaṃ         īsānaṃ dukkhamāvahi .

[1747] iccevaṃ [^345-7] phandano īsaṃ      īso ca pana phandanaṃ    
#[^345-1] ma. Sabbattha isso . Sī. Iso .  [^345-2] sī. Yu. Rukkhova .  [^345-3] ma. Tāvade .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 346 -->
<a id="page-346"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 346]*

Aññamaññaṃ vivādena          aññamaññamaghātayuṃ .

[1748] evameva manussesu [^346-1]       vivādo yattha jāyati    
mayūranaccaṃ naccanti           yathā te īsaphandanā .

[1749] taṃ vo vadāmi bhaddaṃ vo       yāvantettha samāgatā    
sammodatha mā vivadittha [^346-2]     mā hotha īsaphandanā .

[1750] sāmagyameva [^346-3] sikkhetha      buddhehetaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ    
sāmagyarato [^346-4] dhammaṭṭho     yogakkhemā na dhaṃsatīti .

Phandanajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

[^346-3] javanahaṃsajātakaṃ    
[1751] idheva haṃsa nipata            piyaṃ me tava dassanaṃ    
issarosi anuppatto         yaṃ idhatthi pavedaya .

[1752] savanena ekassa piyā bhavanti    
disvā panekassa vineti [^346-5] chando    
disvā ca sutvā ca piyā bhavanti    
kacci nu kho me piyasi dassanena .

[1753] savanena piyo mesi          bhiyyo cāgamma dassanaṃ    
evaṃ piyadassano samāno [^346-6]   vasa haṃsa mama santike [^346-7] .

[1754] vaseyyāma tavāgāre        niccaṃ sakkatapūjitā    
matto ca ekadā majjaṃ [^346-8]    haṃsarājaṃ pacantu me .

#[^346-1] ma. Manussānaṃ .  [^346-2] ma. Vivadatha .  [^346-3] ma. Sāmaggimeva  [^346-4] ma. Sāmaggirato .

#[^346-5] ma. Viyeti . Yu. Viheti . [^346-6] ma. Me .  [^346-7] ma - mamantike . [^346-8] ma. Vajje .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 347 -->
<a id="page-347"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 347]*

[1755] dhiratthu taṃ majjapānaṃ          yaṃ me piyataraṃ tayā    
na cāpi majjaṃ pivissāmi [^347-1]    yāva me vacchasī ghare .

[1756] suvijānaṃ sigālānaṃ           sakuṇānañca [^347-2] vassitaṃ .

Manussavassitaṃ rāja           dubbijānataraṃ tato .

[1757] api ce maññatī poso        ñāti mitto sakhāti vā    
yo pubbe sumano hutvā      pacchā sampajjate diso .

[1758] yasmiṃ mano nivisati           avidūre sahāpi so    
santikepi hi so dūre        yasmā vivasate [^347-3] mano .

[1759] antopi ce [^347-4] hoti pasannacitto    
pāraṃ samuddassa pasannacitto    
antopi so hoti paduṭṭhacitto    
pāraṃ samuddassa paduṭṭhacitto .

[1760] saṃvasantā vivasanti           ye disā te rathesabha    
ārā santo saṃvasanti        manasā raṭṭhavaḍḍhana .

[1761] aticiraṃ nivāsena            piyo bhavati appiyo    
āmanta kho taṃ gacchāmi [^347-5]    purā te homi appiyo [^347-6] .

[1762] evaṃ ce yācamānānaṃ        añjaliṃ nāvabujjhasi    
paricārakānaṃ santānaṃ [^347-7]      vacanaṃ na karosi no    
evantaṃ abhiyācāma          puna kayirāsi pariyāyaṃ .

[1763] evaṃ ce no viharataṃ         antarāyo na hessati    
#[^347-1] ma. Pissāmi . Sī. Yu. Pāyāmi .  [^347-2] sī. Yu. Sakuntānañca .  [^347-3] ma. Yasmiṃ    
#nāvisate . [^347-4] ma. So .  [^347-5] ma. Gacchāma.  [^347-6] ma. Homa appiyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 348 -->
<a id="page-348"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 348]*

Tuyhaṃ vāpi [^348-1] mahārāja      mayhaṃ vā [^348-2] raṭṭhavaḍḍhana    
appevanāma passema         ahorattānamaccayeti .

Javanahaṃsajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^348-4] cullanāradakassapajātakaṃ [^348-3]    
[1764] na te kaṭṭhāni bhinnāni       na te udakamābhataṃ    
aggipi te na hāsito [^348-4]     kinnu mandova jhāyasi .

[1765] na ussahe vane vatthuṃ        kassapāmantayāmi taṃ    
dukkho vāso araññasmiṃ       raṭṭhaṃ icchāmi gantave .

[1766] yathā ahaṃ ito gantvā       yasmiṃ janapade vasaṃ    
ācāraṃ brahme sikkheyya     taṃ dhammaṃ anusāsa maṃ .

[1767] sace araññaṃ hitvāna         vanamūlaphalāni ca    
raṭṭhe rocayase vāsaṃ        taṃ dhammaṃ nisāmehi me .

[1768] visaṃ mā paṭisevittha [^348-5]       papātaṃ parivajjaya    
paṅke ca mā visīdittho       yatto āsīvise [^348-6] care .

[1769] kinnu visaṃ papāto vā        paṅko vā brahmacārinaṃ    
kaṃ tvaṃ āsīvisaṃ brūhi         taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[1770] āsavo tāta lokasmiṃ        surā nāma pavuccati    
manuññā [^348-7] surabhī vaggū       madhukhuddarasūpamā [^348-8]    
visaṃ tadāhu ariyā [9]      brahmacariyassa nārada .

#[^348-1] ma. Cāpi .  [^348-2] ma. Ca .  [^348-3] ma. Cūḷanāradajātakaṃ .  [^348-4] ma. Hāpito .

#[^348-5] ma. Paṭisevittho .  [^348-6] ma. Cāsīvise .  [^348-7] ma. Manuñño .  [^348-8] ma. Sādu -- .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 349 -->
<a id="page-349"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 349]*

[1771] itthiyo tāta lokasmiṃ        pamattaṃ pamathenti tā    
pharanti munino [^349-1] cittaṃ       tūlaṃ bhaṭṭhaṃva māluto    
papāto esa akkhāto       brahmacariyassa nārada .

[1772] lābho siloko sakkāro      pūjā parakulesu ca    
paṅko esova [^349-2] akkhāto   brahmacariyassa nārada .

[1773] mahantā [^349-3] tāta rājāno    āvasanti mahiṃ imaṃ    
te tādise manussinde       mahante tāta nārada .

[1774] issarānaṃ adhipatīnaṃ           na tesaṃ pādato care    
āsīvisoti [^349-4] akkhāto      brahmacariyassa nārada .

[1775] bhattattho bhattakāle ca       yaṃ gehaṃ upasaṅkame    
yadettha kusalaṃ jaññā         tattha ghāsesanaṃ care .

Pavisitvā parakulaṃ            pānattho [^349-5] bhojanāya vā    
mitaṃ khāde mitaṃ bhuñje        na ca rūpe manaṃ kare .

[1776] guṭṭhaṃ majjaṃ kirāsañca [^349-6]      sabhānikiraṇāni [^349-7] ca    
ārakā parivajjehi          yānīva visamaṃ pathanti .

Cullanāradakassapajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

#[^349-1] ma. Haranti yuvino .  [^349-2] ma. Eso ca .  [^349-3] ma. Mahattā .  [^349-4] sī. Yu.

#āsīviso so .  [^349-5] ma. Pānatthaṃ .  [^349-6] ma. Goṭṭhaṃ majjaṃ kirāṭañca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 350 -->
<a id="page-350"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 350]*

[^350-5] dūtajātakaṃ    
[1777] dūte te brahme pāhesiṃ     gaṅgātīrasmi jhāyato    
tesaṃ puṭṭho na byākāsi      dukkhaṃ tuyhaṃ mataṃ [^350-1] nu te .

[1778] sace te dukkhamuppajje       kāsīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhana    
mā kho naṃ tassa akkhāhi      yo taṃ dukkhā na mocaye .

[1779] yo tassa [^350-2] dukkhajātassa     ekaṅgamapi bhāgaso [^350-3]    
vippamoceyya dhammena        kāmaṃ tassa pavedaya [^350-4] .

[1780] suvijānaṃ sigālānaṃ           sakuntānañca [^350-5] vassitaṃ .

Manussavassitaṃ rāja           dubbijānataraṃ tato .

[1781] api ce maññatī poso        ñāti mitto sakhāti vā    
yo pubbe sumano hutvā      pacchā sampajjate diso .

[1782] yo attano dukkhamanānupuṭṭho    
pavedaye jantu akālarūpe    
anandino [^350-6] tassa bhavanti mittā [^350-7]    
hitesino tassa dukkhī bhavanti .

[1783] kālañca ñatvāna tathāvidhassa    
medhāvinaṃ ekamanaṃ viditvā    
akkheyya tippāni [^350-8] parassa dhīro    
saṇhaṃ giraṃ atthavatiṃ pamuñce .

#[^350-1] ma. Guyhamataṃ . Sī. Guyhaṃ mataṃ .  [^350-2] yu. Yo ca tathā .  [^350-3] sī. Yu. Ekantamapi    
#bhāsato .  [^350-4] sī. Pavedaye .  [^350-5] ma. Sakuṇānañca .  [^350-6] ma. Ānandino . [^350-7] sī. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 351 -->
<a id="page-351"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 351]*

[1784] sace ca jaññā avisayhamattano    
na tehi mayhaṃ sukhāgamāya [^351-1]    
ekova tippāni saheyya dhīro    
saccaṃ hirottappamapekkhamāno .

[1785] ahaṃ raṭṭhāni vicaranto        nigame rājadhāniyo    
bhikkhamāno mahārāja         ācariyassa dhanatthiko .

[1786] gahapatī rājapurise           mahāsāle ca brāhmaṇe    
alatthaṃ satta nikkhāni         suvaṇṇassa janādhipa    
te me naṭṭhā mahārāja      tasmā socāmahaṃ bhusaṃ .

[1787] purisā te mahārāja         manasānuvicintitā    
nālaṃ dukkhā pamocetuṃ        tasmā tesaṃ na byāhariṃ .

[1788] tvañca kho me mahārāja      manasānuvicintito    
alaṃ dukkhā pamocetuṃ         tasmā tuyhaṃ pavedayiṃ .

[1789] tassādāsi pasannacitto       kāsīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhano    
jātarūpamaye nikkhe          suvaṇṇassa catuddasāti .

Dūtajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 352 -->
<a id="page-352"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 352]*

[^352-6] kāliṅgabodhijātakaṃ    
[1790] rājā kāliṅgo cakkavatti     dhammena paṭhavimanusāsi [^352-1]    
agamāsi [^352-2] bodhisamīpaṃ        nāgena mahānubhāvena .

[1791] kāliṅgo bhāradvājo ca      rājānaṃ kāliṅgaṃ samaṇakolaññaṃ    
cakkavattayato pariggahetvā [^352-3] pañjalī idamavoca .

[1792] paccoroha mahārāja         bhūmibhāgo yathā samaṇugīto [^352-4]    
idha anadhivarā buddhā         abhisambuddhā virocanti .

[1793] padakkhiṇato āvaṭṭā         tiṇalatā asmiṃ bhūmibhāgasmiṃ    
paṭhaviyā ayaṃ [^352-5] maṇḍo       iti no sutaṃ [6] mahārāja .

[1794] sāgarapariyantāya (medaniyā)   sabbabhūtadharaṇiyā    
paṭhaviyā ayaṃ maṇḍo          orohitvā namo karohi .

[1795] ye te bhavanti nāgā ca      abhijātā ca kuñjarā [^352-7]    
ettāvatā padesaṃ te       nāgā nevamupayanti [^352-8] .

[1796] abhijāto [9] nāgo kāmaṃ   pesehi kuñjaraṃ dantiṃ    
ettāvatā padeso ca 10 na   sakkā nāgena upagantuṃ 1[^352-1] .

[1797] taṃ sutvā rājā kāliṅgo     veyyañjanikavaco nisāmetvā    
sampesesi nāgaṃ uyyāma 1[^352-2]  mayaṃ yathā 1[^352-3] idaṃ vacanaṃ .

[1798] sampesito ca raññā nāgo    koñcova abhinaditvāna    
#[^352-1] ma. Pathavimanusāsaṃ .  [^352-2] ma. Agamā .  [^352-3] yu. Pariṇetvā .  [^352-4] ma. Samaṇuggato .

#[^352-5] ma. Nābhiyaṃ .  [^352-6] ma. Mante .  [^352-7] abhijātā mātito ca pitito cātipi pāṭho .

#[^352-8] nevamussahantītipi .  [^352-9] sī. Yu. Te .  10 ma. Casaddo natthi .  1[^352-1] ma. Sakkā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 353 -->
<a id="page-353"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 353]*

Paṭisakkitvā nisīdi           garubhāraṃ asahamāno .

[1799] kāliṅgo bhāradvājo nāgaṃ khīṇāyukaṃ    
viditvāna rājānaṃ kāliṅgaṃ taramāno    
ajjhabhāsittha aññaṃ saṅkama nāgaṃ    
nāgo khīṇāyuko mahārāja .

[1800] taṃ sutvā kāliṅgo taramāno    
saṅkami nāgaṃ saṅkanteva raññe    
nāgo tattheva pati bhūmyā    
veyyañjanikavaco yathā tathā ahu nāgo .

[1801] kāliṅgabhāradvājaṃ kāliṅgo [^353-1] brāhmaṇaṃ idamavoca    
tvamevāsi sambuddho sabbaññū sabbadassāvī .

[1802] taṃ anadhivāsento kāliṅgo [^353-2] brāhmaṇo idamavoca    
veyyañjanikā hi mayaṃ         buddhā sabbaññuno mahārāja .

[1803] sabbaññū sabbavidū ca          buddhā [3] lakkhaṇena jānanti    
āgamabalasā [^353-4] hi mayaṃ       buddhā sabbaṃ pajānanti .

[1804] pahaṃsitvāna [^353-5] sambodhiṃ       nānāturiyehi vijjamānehi    
mālāvilepanaṃ abhiharitvā [^353-6]   pākāraparikkhepaṃ kāresi    
atha rājā pāyāsi .

[1805] saṭṭhivāhasahassāni           pupphāni sannipātayi    
#[^353-1] ma. Kāliṅgo rājā kāliṅgaṃ .  [^353-2] kāliṅgaṃ .  [^353-3] ma. Na .  [^353-4] yu. Āgamapurisā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 354 -->
<a id="page-354"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 354]*

pūjesi rājā kāliṅgo       bodhimaṇḍamanuttaranti .

Kāliṅgabodhijātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^354-7] akittijātakaṃ    
[1806] akittiṃ disvāna sammantaṃ       sakko bhūtapatī bravi    
kiṃ patthayaṃ mahābrahme        eko sammasi ghammani .

[1807] dukkho punabbhavo sakka        sarīrassa ca bhedanaṃ    
sammohamaraṇaṃ dukkhaṃ           tasmā sammāmi vāsava .

[1808] etasmiṃ te sulapite         paṭirūpe subhāsite    
varaṃ kassapa te dammi         yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[1809] varañce me ado sakka       sabbabhūtānamissara    
yena putte ca dāre ca      dhanadhaññaṃ piyāni ca    
laddhā narā na tappanti       so lobho na mayī vase .

[1810] etasmiṃ te sulapite         paṭirūpe subhāsite    
varaṃ kassapa te dammi         yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[1811] varañce me ado sakka       sabbabhūtānamissara    
khettaṃ vatthuṃ hiraññañca        gavāssaṃ dāsaporisaṃ    
yena jātena jīyanti         so doso na mayī vase .

[1812] etasmiṃ te sulapite         paṭirūpe subhāsite  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 355 -->
<a id="page-355"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 355]*

varaṃ kassapa te dammi         yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[1813] varañce me ado sakka       sabbabhūtānamissara    
bālaṃ na passe na suṇe       na ca bālena saṃvase    
bālenallāpasallāpaṃ         na kare na ca rocaye .

[1814] kinnu te akaraṃ bālo        vada kassapa kāraṇaṃ    
kena kassapa bālassa         dassanaṃ nābhikaṅkhasi .

[1815] anayaṃ nayati dummedho         adhurāyaṃ niyuñjati    
dunnayo seyyaso hoti       sammā vutto pakuppati    
vinayaṃ so na jānāti         sādhu tassa adassanaṃ .

[1816] etasmiṃ te sulapite         paṭirūpe subhāsite    
varaṃ kassapa te dammi         yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[1817] varañce me ado sakka       sabbabhūtānamissara    
dhīraṃ passe suṇe dhīraṃ         dhīrena saha saṃvase    
dhīrenallāpasallāpaṃ          taṃ kare tañca rocaye .

[1818] kinnu te akaraṃ dhīro         vada kassapa kāraṇaṃ    
kena kassapa dhīrassa          dassanaṃ abhikaṅkhasi .

[1819] nayaṃ nayati medhāvī           adhurāyaṃ na yuñjati    
sunayo seyyaso hoti        sammā vutto na kuppati    
vinayaṃ so pajānāti          sādhu tena samāgamo .

[1820] etasmiṃ te sulapite         paṭirūpe subhāsite  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 356 -->
<a id="page-356"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 356]*

Varaṃ kassapa te dammi         yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[1821] varañce me ado sakka       sabbabhūtānamissara    
tato ratyā vivasane [^356-1]      suriyassuggamanaṃ pati    
dibbā bhakkhā pātubhaveyyuṃ     sīlavanto ca yācakā    
dadato me na khīyetha         datvā nānutapeyyahaṃ    
dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādeyyaṃ         etaṃ sakka varaṃ vare .

[1822] etasmiṃ te sulapite         paṭirūpe subhāsite    
varaṃ kassapa te dammi         yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[1823] varañca me ado sakka        sabbabhūtānamissara    
na maṃ puna upeyyāsi         etaṃ sakka varaṃ vare .

[1824] bahūhi vattacariyāhi           narā ca atha nāriyo    
dassanaṃ abhikaṅkhanti           kinnu me dassane bhayaṃ .

[1825] taṃ tādisaṃ devavaṇṇaṃ [^356-2]       sabbakāmasamiddhinaṃ    
disvā tapo pamajjeyyaṃ [^356-3]    etaṃ te dassane bhayanti .

Akittijātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^356-8] takkāriyajātakaṃ  
[1826] ahameva dubbhāsitaṃ bhāsi bālo  
bhekovaraññe ahimavhāyamāno  
takkāriye sobbhamimaṃ patāmi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 357 -->
<a id="page-357"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 357]*

Na kireva sādhu ativelabhāṇī .

[1827] pappoti macco ativelabhāṇī    
bandhaṃ vadhaṃ sokapariddavañca    
attānameva garahāsi ettha [^357-1]    
ācariya taṃ nikkhaṇanti [^357-2] sobbhe .

[1828] kimevāhaṃ tuṇḍilaṃ anupucchiṃ    
kareyyāsaṃ [^357-3] bhātaraṃ kālikāya    
naggovahaṃ vatthayugañca jino    
ayampi attho bahu tādisova .

[1829] yo yujjhamānānaṃ ayujjhamāno    
meṇḍantaraṃ accupatī kuliṅgo    
so piṃsito meṇḍasirehi tattha    
ayampi attho bahu tādisova .

[1830] caturo janā potthakamaggahesuṃ    
ekañca posaṃ anurakkhamānā    
sabbeva te bhinnasirā sayiṃsu    
ayampi attho bahu tādisova .

[1831] ajā yathā veḷugumbasmiṃ bandhā    
avekkhipantī [^357-4] asimajjhagacchi    
#[^357-1] sī. Etto .  [^357-2] ma. Ācera yaṃ taṃ nikkhaṇanti .  [^357-3] kareyya saṅgahantipi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 358 -->
<a id="page-358"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 358]*

Teneva tassā galakaṃ vikantā [^358-1]    
ayampi attho bahu tādisova .

[1832] nayime devā nāpi [^358-2] gandhabbaputtā    
migā ime atthavasābhatā ime [^358-3]    
ekañca naṃ sāyamāse pacantu    
ekaṃ [^358-4] punappātarāse pacantu .

[1833] sataṃ sahassāna dubbhāsitānaṃ [^358-5]    
kalampi nāgghati subhāsitassa    
dubbhāsitaṃ saṅkamāno kileso    
tasmā tuṇhī kiṃpuriso [^358-6] na bālyā .

[1834] yā mesā byākāsi [^358-7] pamuñcathetaṃ    
giriñca naṃ himavantaṃ nayantu    
imañca kho dentu mahānasāya    
pātova naṃ pātarāse pacantu .

[1835] pajjunnanāthā pasavo         pasunāthā ayaṃ pajā    
tvaṃnāthosmi [^358-8] mahārāja     amhanāthā mama bhariyā [^358-9]    
dvinnamaññataraṃ ñatvā         mutto gaccheyya pabbataṃ .

[1836] na ve nindā suparivajjayātha 10    
#[^358-1] ma. Galakāvakantaṃ .  [^358-2] ma. Ime na devā na .  [^358-3] ma. Atthavasaṃ gatā me .

#[^358-4] sī. Yu. Ekañca naṃ pātarāse .  [^358-5] ma. Sahassāni dubhāsitāni .  [^358-6] ma. Kimpurisā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 359 -->
<a id="page-359"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 359]*

Nānājanā sevitabbā janinda    
yeneva eko labhate pasaṃsaṃ    
teneva añño labhate ninditāraṃ .

[1837] sabbo loko paracittena [^359-1] aticitto    
sabbo loko cittavā samhi citte    
paccekacittā puthu sabbasattā    
kassīdha cittassa vase na vatte .

[1838] tuṇhī ahu kiṃpuriso sabhariyo 2-[^359-4]    
yodāni byākāsi bhayassa bhīto    
sodāni mutto sukhito arogo    
vācā kirevatthavatī narānanti .

Takkāriyajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^359-9] rurumigajātakaṃ [^359-3]    
[1839] tassa [^359-4] gāmavaraṃ dammi       nāriyo ca alaṅkatā    
ko [^359-5] me taṃ migamakkhāsi     migānaṃ migamuttamaṃ .

[1840] mayhaṃ gāmavaraṃ dehi          nāriyo ca alaṅkatā    
ahaṃ te migamakkhissaṃ          migānaṃ migamuttamaṃ .

[1841] etasmiṃ vanasaṇḍasmiṃ          ambā sālā ca pupphitā    
#[^359-1] ma. Paricitto .  [^359-2] abhāṇisotipi .  [^359-3] ma. Rurumigarājajātakaṃ .  [^359-4] sī. Yu. Kassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 360 -->
<a id="page-360"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 360]*

Indagopakasañchannā          ettheso tiṭṭhati migo .

[1842] dhanana advejjhaṃ katvāna        usuṃ sannayhupāgami [^360-1]    
migo ca disvā rājānaṃ       dūrato ajjhabhāsatha    
āgamehi mahārāja          mā maṃ vijjhi rathesabha    
ko nu te idamakkhāsi        ettheso tiṭṭhati migo .

[1843] esa pāpacaro poso        samma tiṭṭhati ārakā    
so hi [^360-2] me idamakkhāsi     ettheso tiṭṭhati migo .

[1844] saccaṃ kirevamāhaṃsu           narā ekacciyā idha    
kaṭṭhaṃ nipāvataṃ [^360-3] seyyo     na tvevekacciyo naro .

[1845] kinnu ruru garahasi migānaṃ    
kiṃ pakkhinaṃ kiṃ pana mānusānaṃ    
bhayaṃ hi maṃ vindatinapparūpaṃ    
sutvāna taṃ mānusi [^360-4] bhāsamānaṃ .

[1846] yamuddhariṃ vāhane vuyhamānaṃ    
mahodake salile sīghasote    
tatonidānaṃ bhayamāgataṃ mama    
dukkho have rāja asabbhi saṅgamo .

[1847] sohaṃ catuppattamimaṃ vihaṅgamaṃ    
tanucchidaṃ hadaye osajjāmi    
#[^360-1] sī. Yu. Sandhāyupāgami .  [^360-2] ma. So yaṃ .  [^360-3] sī. Vipalāvitaṃ . Ma. Niplavitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 361 -->
<a id="page-361"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 361]*

Hanāmi taṃ mittadubbhiṃ akiccakāriṃ    
yo tādisaṃ kammakataṃ na jāne .

[1848] dhīrassa bālassa have janinda    
santo vadhaṃ nappasaṃsanti jātu    
kāmaṃ gharaṃ gacchatu pāpadhammo    
yañcassa bhattaṃ tadetassa dehi    
ahañca te kāmakaro bhavāmi .

[1849] addhā ruru aññataro sataṃ so    
yo dubbhino [^361-1] mānusassa na dubbhī    
kāmaṃ gharaṃ gacchatu pāpadhammo    
yañcassa bhattaṃ tadetassa dammi    
ahañca te gāmavaraṃ dadāmi .

[1850] suvijānaṃ sigālānaṃ           sakuntānañca vassitaṃ    
manussavassitaṃ rāja           dubbijānataraṃ tato    
api ce maññatī poso        ñāti mitto sakhāti vā    
yo pubbe sumano hutvā      pacchā sampajjate diso .

[1851] samāgatā jānapadā          negamā ca samāgatā    
migā dhaññāni [^361-2] khādanti     taṃ devo paṭisedhatu .

[1852] kāmaṃ janapado māsi          raṭṭhañcāpi vinassatu    
na tvevāhaṃ ruruṃ dubbhe       datvā abhaya pakkhinaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 362 -->
<a id="page-362"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 362]*

[1853] mā me janapado āsi        mā maṃ janapado ahu [^362-1]    
na tvevāhaṃ migarājassa       varaṃ datvā musā bhaṇeti .

Rurumigajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 sarabhajātakaṃ [^362-2]    
[1854] āsiṃsetheva [^362-3] puriso       na nibbindeyya paṇḍito    
passāmi vohaṃ attānaṃ        yathā icchiṃ tathā ahu .

[1855] āsiṃsetheva [^362-3] puriso       na nibbindeyya paṇḍito    
passāmi vohaṃ attānaṃ        udakā thalamubbhataṃ .

[1856] vāyametheva puriso          na nibbindeyya paṇḍito    
passāmi vohaṃ attānaṃ        yathā icchiṃ tathā ahu .

[1857] vāyametheva puriso          na nibbindeyya paṇḍito    
passāmi vohaṃ attānaṃ        udakā thalamubbhataṃ .

[1858] dukkhūpanītopi naro sapañño    
āsaṃ na chindeyya sukhāgamāya    
bahū hi phassā ahitā hitā ca    
avitakkitāro maccumupapajjanti [^362-4] .

[1859] acintitampi bhavati            cintitampi vinassati    
na hi cintāmayā bhogā       itthiyā purisassa vā .

#[^362-1] ma. Raṭṭhañcāpi vinassatu .  [^362-2] ma. Sarabhamigajātakaṃ .  [^362-3] ma. Āsīsetheva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 363 -->
<a id="page-363"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 363]*

[1860] sarabhaṃ giriduggasmiṃ            yaṃ tvaṃ anussarī pure    
alīnacittassa tuvaṃ            vikkantamanujīvasi .

[1861] yo taṃ viduggā narakā samuddhari    
sīlāya yoggaṃ sarabho karitvā    
dukkhūpanītaṃ maccumukhā pamocayi    
alīnacittaṃ tameva [^363-1] vadesi .

[1862] kiṃ tvaṃ nu tattheva tadā ahosi    
udāhu te koci taṃ etadakkhā    
vivaṭacchado nusi sabbadassī    
ñāṇaṃ nu te brāhmaṇa bhiṃsarūpaṃ .

[1863] na cevāhaṃ tattha tadā ahosiṃ    
na cāpi me koci naṃ etadakkhā    
gāthāpadānañca subhāsitānaṃ    
atthaṃ tadānenti janinda dhīrā .

[1864] ādāya pattaṃ [^363-2] paravīriyaghātiṃ    
cāpe saraṃ kiṃ vicikicchase tuvaṃ    
nuṇṇo saro sarabhaṃ hantu khippaṃ    
annaṃ hi etaṃ varapañña rañño .

[1865] addhā pajānāmi ahaṃpi etaṃ  
annaṃ migo brāhmaṇa khattiyassa  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 364 -->
<a id="page-364"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 364]*

Pubbe katañca apacāyamāno    
tasmā migaṃ sarabhaṃ no hanāmi .

[1866] neso migo mahārāja        asureso disampati    
etaṃ hantvā manussinda       bhavassu amarādhipo .

[1867] sace ca rāja vicikicchase tuvaṃ    
hantuṃ migaṃ sarabhaṃ sahāyakaṃ [^364-1]    
saputtadāro naravīraseṭṭha [^364-2]    
gantvā [^364-3] tuvaṃ vetaraṇiṃ yamassa .

[1868] kāmaṃ ahaṃ jānapadā ca sabbe    
puttā ca dārā ca sahāyasaṅghā    
gacchemu taṃ vetaraṇiṃ yamassa    
na tveva haññe mama pāṇadassa [^364-4] .

[1869] ayaṃ migo kicchagatassa mayhaṃ    
ekassa kattā vivanasmiṃ ghore    
taṃ tādisaṃ pubbakiccaṃ saranto    
jānaṃ mahābrahme kathaṃ haneyyaṃ .

[1870] mittābhirādhī cirameva jīva    
rajjaṃ imaṃ dhammaguṇe pasāsa    
nārīgaṇehi paricārayanto [^364-5]    
modassu raṭṭhe tidiveva vāsavo .

#[^364-1] sī. Yu. Sahāyakaṃ me .  [^364-2] sī. Yu. Naraviriyaseṭṭha .  [^364-3] sī. Yu. Gantā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 365 -->
<a id="page-365"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 365]*

[1871] akkodhano niccapasannacitto    
sabbātithī pāhunake karitvā [^365-1]    
datvā ca bhutvā ca yathānubhāvaṃ    
anindito saggamupehi ṭhānanti .

Sarabhajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

tassuddānaṃ    
varamamba [^365-2] kuṭhāri sahaṃsavaro    
atharaññasmiṃ dūtakapañcamako    
atha bodhi akitti sutakkarinā    
atha rurumigena paro sarabho .

Terasanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 366 -->
<a id="page-366"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 366]*

Pakiṇṇakanipātajātakaṃ    
[^366-1] sālikedārajātakaṃ    
[1872] sampannaṃ sālikedāraṃ         suvā bhuñjanti kosiya    
paṭivedemi te brahme       na te vāretumussahe    
eko ca tattha sakuṇo        yo nesaṃ sabbasundaro    
bhutvā sāliṃ yathākāmaṃ        tuṇḍenādāya gacchati .

[1873] ujjhantu [^366-1] vālapāsāni      yathā bajjhetha so dijo    
jīvañca naṃ gahetvāna         ānayehi [^366-2] mamantike .

[1874] ete bhutvā pivitvā ca      pakkamanti vihaṅgamā    
eko baddhosmi pāsena      kiṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ mayā .

[1875] udaraṃ nūna aññesaṃ           suva accodaraṃ tava    
bhutvā sāliṃ yathākāmaṃ        tuṇḍenādāya gacchasi .

Koṭṭhannu tattha pūresi        suva verannu te mayā    
puṭṭho me samma akkhāhi      kuhiṃ sāliṃ nidheyyasi [^366-3] .

[1876] na me veraṃ tayā saddhiṃ       koṭṭho mayhaṃ na vijjati    
iṇaṃ muñcāmiṇaṃ dammi          sampatto koṭisimbaliṃ [^366-4]    
nidhiṃpi tattha nidahāmi          evaṃ jānāhi kosiya .

[1877] kīdisaṃ te iṇadānaṃ           iṇamokkho ca kīdiso  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 367 -->
<a id="page-367"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 367]*

Nidhinidhānamakkhāhi            atha pāsā pamokkhasi .

[1878] ajātapakkhā taruṇā          puttakā mayhaṃ kosiya    
te maṃ bhatā bharissanti        tasmā tesaṃ iṇaṃ dade .

Mātā pitā ca me vuḍḍhā     jiṇṇakā gatayobbanā    
tesaṃ tuṇḍena hātūna         muñce pubbakataṃ iṇaṃ .

Aññepi tattha sakuṇā         khīṇapakkhā sudubbalā    
tesaṃ puññatthiko dammi        taṃ nidhiṃ āhu paṇḍitā .

Īdisaṃ [^367-1] me iṇadānaṃ        iṇamokkho ca īdiso    
nidhinidhānamakkhāmi            evaṃ jānāhi kosiya .

[1879] bhaddako vatāyaṃ pakkhī         dijo paramadhammiko    
ekaccesu manussesu         ayaṃ dhammo na vijjati .

Bhuñja sāliṃ yathākāmaṃ         saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
punapi suva passemu           piyaṃ me tava dassanaṃ .

[1880] bhuttañca pītañca tavassamamhi    
rattiñca no kosiya te sakāse    
nikkhittadaṇḍesu dadāhi dānaṃ    
jiṇṇe ca mātāpitaro bharassu .

[1881] lakkhī vata me udapādi ajja  
yohaṃ adassaṃ [^367-2] paramaṃ dijānaṃ  
suvassa sutvāna subhāsitāni  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 368 -->
<a id="page-368"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 368]*

Kāhāmi puññāni anappakāni .

[1882] so kosiyo attamano udaggo    
annañca pānañcabhisaṅkharitvā    
annena pānena pasannacitto    
santappayi samaṇabrāhmaṇe cāti .

Sālikedārajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^368-2] candakinnarajātakaṃ [^368-1]    
[1883] upanīyatidaṃ maññe cande lohitamaddane [^368-2]    
ajjāpi [^368-3] vijahāmi jīvitaṃ pāṇā me cande nirujjhanti .

Osadhi [^368-4] me dukkhaṃ hadayaṃ me ḍayhate nitammāmi    
tava candiyā socantiyā na naṃ aññehi sokehi .

Tiṇamiva vanamiva milāyāmi nadī aparipuṇṇāva [^368-5] sussāmi    
tava candiyā socantiyā na naṃ aññehi sokehi .

Vassamiva sare pāde imāni assūni vattare mayhaṃ    
tava candiyā socantiyā na naṃ aññehi sokehi .

[1884] pāpo kho [^368-6] rājaputto yo me icchitapatiṃ varākiyā    
vijjhi vanamūlasmiṃ soyaṃ viddho chamā seti .

Imaṃ mayhaṃ hadayasokaṃ paṭimuñcatu rājaputta tava mātā    
#[^368-1] ma. Candakinnarījātakaṃ .  [^368-2] sī. Yu. Lohitamadena majjāmi vijahāmi jīvitaṃ pāṇā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 369 -->
<a id="page-369"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 369]*

Yo mayhaṃ hadayasoko kiṃpurisaṃ avekkhamānāya .

Imaṃ mayhaṃ hadayasokaṃ paṭimuñcatu rājaputta tava jāyā    
yo mayhaṃ hadayasoko kiṃpurisaṃ avekkhamānāya .

Mā ca puttaṃ [^369-1] mā ca patiṃ addakkhi rājaputta tava mātā    
yo kiṃpurisaṃ avadhi adūsakaṃ mayhaṃ kāmāhi .

Mā ca puttaṃ mā ca patiṃ addakkhi rājaputta tava jāyā    
yo kiṃpurisaṃ avadhi adūsakaṃ mayhaṃ kāmāhi .

[1885] mā tvaṃ cande rodi mā soci vanatimiramattakkhi    
mama tvaṃ hehisi bhariyā rājakule pūjitā nārībhi .

[1886] api nūnāhaṃ marissaṃ nāhaṃ rājaputta tava hessaṃ    
yo kiṃpurisaṃ avadhi adūsakaṃ mayhaṃ kāmāhi .

[1887] api bhīruke api jīvitukāmike kiṃpurisī gaccha himavantaṃ    
tālisataggarabhojanā [^369-2] aññe taṃ migā ramissanti .

[1888] te pabbatā tā ca kandarā tā ca giriguhāyo [3]    
tattha [^369-4] taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ [^369-5] .

Yesu pabbatesu mayaṃ ekato abhiramimhā    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ [^369-6] .

Te paṇṇasanthatā ramaṇīyā vāḷamigehi anuciṇṇā    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

#[^369-1] sī. Yu. Putte .  [^369-2] sī. Yu. Tālissataggarabhojane .  [^369-3] ma. Tatheva tiṭṭhanti .

#[^369-4] ma. Tattheva . Evamuparipi .  [^369-5] ma. Kassaṃ . Evamuparipi .  [^369-6] ayaṃ gāthā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 370 -->
<a id="page-370"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 370]*

Te pupphasanthatā ramaṇīyā vāḷamigehi anuciṇṇā    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ [^370-1] .

Acchā savantī girivananadiyo kusumābhikiṇṇasotāyo    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

Nīlāni himavato pabbatassa kūṭāni dassanīyāni [^370-2]    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

Pītāni himavato pabbatassa kūṭāni dassanīyāni    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

Tambāni himavato pabbatassa kūṭāni dassanīyāni    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

Tuṅgāni himavato pabbatassa kūṭāni dassanīyāni    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

Setāni himavato pabbatassa kūṭāni dassanīyānil    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

Citrāni himavato pabbatassa kūṭāni dassanīyāni    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

Yakkhagaṇasevite gandhamādane osadhehi sañchanne    
tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

Kiṃpurisasevite gandhamādane osadhehi sañchanne    
#[^370-1] ayaṃ gāthā sabbapotthakesu dissati aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana na dissati .  [^370-2] sī. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 371 -->
<a id="page-371"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 371]*

Tattha taṃ apassantī kiṃpurisa kathaṃ ahaṃ kāsaṃ .

[1889] vande te (pāde) ayirabrahme yo me icchitaṃ patiṃ varākiyā    
amatena abhisiñci samāgatasmiṃ [^371-1] piyatamena .

[1890] vicarāmadāni girivananadiyo kusumābhikiṇṇasotāyo    
nānādumavasanāyo piyaṃvadā aññamaññassāti .

Candakinnarajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ    
[^371-3] mahāukkusajātakaṃ    
[1891] ukkā milācā [^371-2] bandhanti dīpe    
pajā mama khādituṃ patthayanti    
mittaṃ sahāyañca vadehi senaka    
ācikkha ñātibyasanaṃ dijānaṃ .

[1892] dijo dijānaṃ pavarosi pakkhi ca [^371-3]    
ukkusarāja saraṇaṃ taṃ upemi [^371-4]    
pajā mama khādituṃ patthayanti    
luddā milācā [^371-5] bhava me sukhāya .

[1893] mittaṃ sahāyañca karonti paṇḍitā    
kāle akāle sukhamesamānā    
karomi te senaka etamatthaṃ    
#[^371-1] ma. Samāgatāsmi .  2-[^371-5] ma. Cilācā .  [^371-3] ma. Pakkhima . Sī. Yu. Casaddo na dissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 372 -->
<a id="page-372"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 372]*

Ariyo hi ariyassa karoti kiccaṃ .

[1894] yaṃ hoti kiccaṃ anukampakena    
ariyassa ariyena kataṃ tayidaṃ    
attānurakkhī bhava mā aḍayhi    
lacchāma putte tayi jīvamāne .

[1895] tameva [^372-1] rakkhāvaraṇaṃ karonto    
sarīrabhedāpi na santasāmi    
karonti hete [^372-2] sakhīnaṃ sakhāro    
pāṇaṃ cajantā [^372-3] satamesa dhammo .

[1896] sudukkaraṃ kammamakāsi          aṇḍajoyaṃ [^372-4] vihaṅgamo    
atthāya kururo putte        aḍḍharatte anāgate .

[1897] cutāpi heke khalitā sakammunā    
mittānukampāya patiṭṭhahanti    
puttā mamaṭṭā gatimāgatosmi    
atthaṃ caretha mama vāricara [^372-5] .

[1898] dhanena dhaññena ca attanā ca    
mittaṃ sahāyañca karonti paṇḍitā    
karomi te senaka etamatthaṃ    
ariyo hi ariyassa karoti kiccaṃ .

[1899] appossukko tāta tuvaṃ nisīda  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 373 -->
<a id="page-373"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 373]*

Putto pitu carati atthacariyaṃ    
ahaṃ carissāmi taveva atthaṃ [^373-1]    
senassa putte parittāyamāno .Ba    
[1900] addhā hi tāta satamesa dhammo    
putto pitu care [^373-2] atthacariyaṃ[^373-5]    
appeva maṃ disvāna pavaḍḍhakāyaṃ    
senassa puttāni na heṭhayeyyuṃ .

[1901] pasū manussā migavīraseṭṭha    
bhayaṭṭitā [^373-3] seṭṭhamupabbajanti    
puttā mamaṭṭā gatimāgatosmi    
tvaṃ nosi rājā bhava me sukhāya .

[1902] karomi te senaka etamatthaṃ    
āyāmi te taṃ disataṃ vadhāya    
kathaṃ hi viññū bahusampajāno    
na vāyame attajanassa guttiyā .

[1903] mittaṃ kayirātha suhadayañca    
ayirañca kayirātha sukhāgamāya    
nivatthakojova sarebhihantvā    
modāma puttehi samaṅgibhūtā .

[1904] sakamittassa kammena          sahāyassāpalāyino    
#[^373-1] ma. Tavetamatthaṃ .  [^373-2] ma. Pituyaṃ care . Sī. Yu. Pitu yañcaretha .  [^373-3] sī. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 374 -->
<a id="page-374"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 374]*

Kujantamupakujanti             lomahaṃsā hadayaṅgamaṃ .

[1905] mittaṃ sahāyaṃ adhigamma paṇḍito    
so bhuñjati puttaṃ pasuṃ dhanaṃ vā    
ahañca puttā ca patī ca mayhaṃ    
mittānukampāya samaṅgibhūtā .

Rājavatā suravatā ca attho    
sampannasakhissa bhavanti hete    
so mittavā yasavā uggatatto    
asmiñca loke modati kāmakāmi .

[1906] karaṇīyāni mittāni           daliddenāpi senaka    
passa mittānukampāya         samaggamhā sañātake .

Sūrena balavantena           yo mittaṃ [^374-1] kurute dijo    
evaṃ so sukhito hoti        yathāhaṃ tvañca senakāti .

Mahāukkusajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ .

[^374-4] uddālakajātakaṃ    
[1907] kharājinā jaṭilā paṅkadantā    
dummakkharūpā ye mantaṃ [^374-2] japanti    
kacci nu te mānusake payoge    
idaṃ vidū parimuttā apāyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 375 -->
<a id="page-375"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 375]*

[1908] pāpāni kammāni kareyya [^375-1] rāja    
bahussuto ce na careyya dhammaṃ    
sahassavedopi na taṃ paṭicca    
dukkhā pamuñce caraṇaṃ apatvā .

[1909] sahassavedopi na taṃ paṭicca    
dukkhā pamuñce caraṇaṃ apatvā    
maññāmi vedā aphalā bhavanti    
sasaṃyamaṃ caraṇaññeva saccaṃ .

[1910] naheva vedā aphalā bhavanti    
sasaṃyamaṃ caraṇaññeva saccaṃ    
kittiñca pappoti adhicca vede    
santiṃ puṇeti [^375-2] caraṇena danto .

[1911] bhaccā mātā pitā bandhu [^375-3]   yena jāto svayeva [^375-4] so    
uddālako ahaṃ bhoto        sotthiyakulavaṃsato [^375-5] .

[1912] kathaṃ bho brāhmaṇo hoti      kathaṃ bhavati kevalī    
kathañca parinibbānaṃ           dhammaṭṭho kinti vuccati .

[1913] niraṃ katvā aggimādāya brāhmaṇo    
āposiñcaṃ yajaṃ usseti yūpaṃ    
evaṅkaro brāhmaṇo hoti khemī    
dhamme ṭhitaṃ tena amāpayiṃsu .

#[^375-1] ma. Karetha .  [^375-2] sī. Punoti . Ma. Puṇāti . Yu. Puneti .  [^375-3] ma. Bandhū .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 376 -->
<a id="page-376"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 376]*

[1914] na suddhi secanena atthi       napi kevalī brāhmaṇo    
na ceva khanti soraccaṃ [^376-1]     napi so parinibbuto .

[1915] kathaṃ bho [^376-2] brāhmaṇo hoti   kathaṃ bhavati kevalī    
kathañca parinibbānaṃ           dhammaṭṭho kinti vuccati .

[1916] akhettabandhu amamo nirāso    
nillobhapāpo bhavalobhakhīṇo    
evaṅkaro brāhmaṇo hoti khemī    
dhamme ṭhitaṃ tena amāpayiṃsu .

[1917] khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā    suddā caṇḍālapukkusā    
sabbeva soratā dantā       sabbeva parinibbutā    
sabbesaṃ sītibhūtānaṃ           atthi seyyotha [^376-3] pāpiyo .

[1918] khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā    suddā caṇḍālapukkusā    
sabbeva soratā dantā       sabbeva parinibbutā    
sabbesaṃ sītibhūtānaṃ           natthi seyyotha [^376-3] pāpiyo .

[1919] khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā    suddā caṇḍālapukkusā    
sabbeva soratā dantā       sabbeva parinibbutā    
sabbesaṃ sītibhūtānaṃ           natthi seyyotha [^376-3] pāpiyo    
pasatthaṃ [^376-4] carasi brahmaññaṃ     sotthiyakulavaṃsataṃ .

[1920] nānārattehi vatthehi        vimānaṃ bhavati chāditaṃ  
na tesaṃ chāyā vatthānaṃ       so rāgo anupajjatha  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 377 -->
<a id="page-377"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 377]*

Evameva manussesu          yadā sujjhanti māṇavā    
na tesaṃ jātiṃ pucchanti [^377-1]     dhammamaññāya subbatāti .

Uddālakajātakaṃ catutthaṃ .

[^377-5] bhisajātakaṃ    
[1921] assaṃ gavaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ    
bhariyañca so idha labhataṃ manāpaṃ    
puttehi dārehi samaṅgi hotu    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1922] mālañca so kāsikacandanañca    
dhāretu puttassa bahū bhavantu    
kāmesu tibbaṃ kurutaṃ apekkhaṃ    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1923] pahūtadhañño kasimā yasassī    
putte gihī dhanimā sabbakāme    
vayaṃ apassaṃ gharamāvasātu    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1924] so khattiyo hotu pasayhakārī  
rājādhirājā balavā yasassī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 378 -->
<a id="page-378"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 378]*

Sa cāturantaṃ mahimāvasātu    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1925] so brāhmaṇo hotu avītarāgo    
muhuttanakkhattapathesu yutto    
pūjetu naṃ raṭṭhapatī yasassī    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1926] ajjhāyakaṃ sabbasamantavedaṃ [^378-1]    
tapassinaṃ maññatu sabbaloko    
pūjentu naṃ jānapadā samecca    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1927] catussadaṃ gāmavaraṃ samiddhaṃ    
dinnañhi so bhuñjatu vāsavena    
avītarāgo maraṇaṃ upetu    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1928] so gāmaṇi hotu sahāyamajjhe    
naccehi gītehi pamodamāno    
mā rājato byasanamalattha [^378-2] kiñci    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1929] yaṃ ekarājā paṭhaviṃ vijitvā    
itthīsahassāna [^378-3] ṭhapetu aggaṃ    
#[^378-1] sī. Sabbasamattavedaṃ . Yu. Sabbasamattavedanaṃ .  [^378-2] ma. So rājato byasanamālattha .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 379 -->
<a id="page-379"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 379]*

Sīmantinīnaṃ pavarā bhavātu    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo [^379-1] ahāsi .

[1930] dāsīnaṃ [^379-2] hi sā sabbasamāgatānaṃ    
bhuñjeyya sāduṃ avikampamānā    
carātu lābhena vikatthamānā    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo [^379-3] ahāsi .

[1931] āvāsiko hotu mahāvihāre    
navakammiko hotu kajaṅgalāyaṃ [^379-4]    
ālokasandhiṃ divasā [^379-5] karotu    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1932] so bajjhatu pāsasatehi chabbhi [^379-6]    
rammā vanā niyyatu rājadhāniṃ    
guttehi so haññatu pācanehi    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1933] alakkamālī tipukaṇṇaviddho    
laṭṭhihato sappamukhaṃ upetu    
sakañca baddho [^379-7] visikhaṃ carātu    
bhisāni te brāhmaṇa yo ahāsi .

[1934] yo ve anaṭṭhaṃ [^379-8] naṭṭhanti āha [^379-9]    
#1-[^379-3] ma. Yā .   [^379-2] ma. Isīnaṃ .    [^379-4] ma. Gajaṅgalāyaṃ .   [^379-5] ma. Divasaṃ .

#[^379-6] sī. Yu. Chamhi .   [^379-7] ma. Sakkacchabandho . Sī. Yu. Sakkaccabaddho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 380 -->
<a id="page-380"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 380]*

Kāmeva so labhatu bhuñjatu ca [^380-1]    
agāramajjhe maraṇaṃ upetu    
yo vā bhonto saṅkati kañcideva .

[1935] yadesamānā vicaranti loke    
iṭṭhañca kantañca bahūnametaṃ    
piyaṃ manuññaṃ idha [^380-2] jīvaloke    
kasmā isayo nappasaṃsanti kāme .

[1936] kāmesu ve haññare bajjhare ca    
kāmesu dukkhañca bhayañca jātaṃ    
kāmesu bhūtādhipatī pamattā    
pāpāni kammāni karonti mohā .

Te pāpadhammā pasavetva pāpaṃ    
kāyassa bhedā nirayaṃ vajanti    
ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇesu disvā    
tasmā isayo nappasaṃsanti kāme .

[1937] vīmaṃsamāno isino bhisāni    
tīre gahetvāna thale nidhesiṃ    
suddhā apāpā isayo vasanti    
etāni te brahmacārī bhisāni .

[1938] na te naṭā no pana kīḷaneyyā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 381 -->
<a id="page-381"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 381]*

Na bandhavā no pana te sahāyā    
kismiṃ paravambhaṃ [^381-1] sahassanetta    
isīhi tuvaṃ kīḷasi devarāja .

[1939] ācariyo mesi pitā ca mayhaṃ    
esā patiṭṭhā khalitassa brahme    
ekāparādhaṃ khama bhūripañña    
na paṇḍitā kodhabalā bhavanti .

[1940] suvāsitaṃ isinaṃ ekarattaṃ    
yaṃ vāsavaṃ bhūtapatiddasāma    
sabbeva bhonto sumanā bhavantu    
yaṃ brāhmaṇo paccupādi bhisāni .

[1941] ahañca sārīputto ca         moggallāno ca kassapo    
anuruddho puṇṇānando        tadāsuṃ satta bhātaro .

Bhaginī uppalavaṇṇā [2]      dāsī khujjuttarā tadā    
citto gahapati dāso         yakkho sātāgiro tadā .

Pālileyyo [^381-3] tadā nāgo   madhudo [^381-4] seṭṭhavānaro    
kāḷudāyī tadā sakko        evaṃ dhāretha jātakanti .

Bhisajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

#[^381-1] sī. Paratthambha . Ma. Vupatthambha .   [^381-2] ma. Ca .    [^381-3] sī. Yu. Pārileyyo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 382 -->
<a id="page-382"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 382]*

[^382-6] surucijātakaṃ    
[1942] mahesī rucino [^382-1] bhariyā      ānītā paṭhamaṃ ahaṃ    
dasa vassasahassāni           yaṃ maṃ suruci ānayi [^382-2] .

Sāhaṃ brāhmaṇa rājānaṃ       vedehaṃ mithilaggahaṃ    
nābhijānāmi kāyena         vācāya uda cetasā    
suruciṃ atimaññittha [^382-3]         āvi vā yadivā raho .

Etena saccavajjena         putto uppajjataṃ ise    
musā me bhaṇamānāya         muddhā phalatu sattadhā .

[1943] bhattu manāpā sassu piyā      mātā pitā ca sassuro [^382-4]    
te maṃ brahme vinetāro     yāva aṭṭhaṃsu jīvitaṃ .

Sāhaṃ ahiṃsāratinī            kāmasā dhammacārinī    
sakkaccaṃ te upaṭṭhāsiṃ        rattindivamatanditā .

Etena saccavajjena         putto uppajjataṃ ise    
musā me bhaṇamānāya         muddhā phalatu sattadhā .

[1944] soḷasitthīsahassāni           sahabhariyāni brāhmaṇa    
tāsu issā vā kodho vā    nāhu mayhaṃ kudācanaṃ .

Hitena tāsaṃ nandāmi         na ca me kāci appiyā    
attānaṃvānukampāmi          sadā sabbā sapattiyo .

Etena saccavajjena         putto upajjataṃ ise    
musā me bhaṇamānāya         muddhā phalatu sattadhā .

#[^382-1] ma. Surucino .  [^382-2] ma. Surucimānayi .   [^382-3] sī. Yu. Atimaññittho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 383 -->
<a id="page-383"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 383]*

[1945] dāse kammakare pose [^383-1]    yecaññe anujīvino    
posemi [^383-2] sahadhammena       sadā pamuditindriyā .

Etena saccavajjena         putto uppajjataṃ ise    
musā me bhaṇamānāya         muddhā phalatu sattadhā .

[1946] samaṇe brāhmaṇe cāpi       aññe cāpi vaṇibbake    
tappemi annapānena         sadā payatapāṇinī .

Etena saccavajjena         putto uppajjataṃ  ise    
musā me bhaṇamānāya         muddhā phalatu sattadhā .

[1947] cātuddasiṃ pañcadasiṃ           yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī [^383-3]    
pāṭihāriyapakkhañca           aṭṭhaṅgasusamāhitaṃ [^383-4]    
uposathaṃ upavasāmi           sadā sīlesu saṃvutā .

Etena saccavajjena         putto uppajjataṃ ise    
musā me bhaṇamānāya         muddhā phalatu sattadhā .

[1948] sabbeva te dhammaguṇā        rājaputti yasassini    
saṃvijjanti tayi bhadde         ye tvaṃ kittesi attani .

Khattiyo jātisampanno        abhijāto yasassimā    
dhammarājā vedehānaṃ [^383-5]     putto upajjate tava .

[1949] dummi [^383-6] rajojalladharo       aghe vehāyasaṇṭhito    
manuññaṃ bhāsase vācaṃ         yaṃ mayhaṃ hadayaṅgamaṃ .

#[^383-1] ma. Pesse .    [^383-2] ma. Pesemi .   [^383-3] sī. Yu. Aṭṭhamiṃ .  [^383-4] ma. Aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 384 -->
<a id="page-384"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 384]*

Devatā nusi saggamhā        isi vāpi [^384-1] mahiddhiko    
ko vāsi tvaṃ anuppatto      attānaṃ me pavedaya .

[1950] yaṃ devasaṅghā vandanti        sudhammāya samāgatā    
sohaṃ sakko sahassakkho       āgatosmi tavantike .

Itthiyo jīvalokasmiṃ          yā honti [^384-2] samacārinī    
medhāvinī sīlavatī            sassudevā patibbatā .

Tādisāya sumedhāya          sucikammāya nāriyā    
devā dassanamāyanti         mānusiyā amānusā .

Tvañca bhadde suciṇṇena       pubbe sucaritena ca    
idha rājakule jātā         sabbakāmasamiddhinī .

Ayañca te rājaputti         ubhayattha kaṭaggaho    
devalokūpapatti ca           kitti ca idha jīvite .

Ciraṃ sumedhe sukhinī           dhammamattani pālaya    
esāhaṃ tidivaṃ yāmi          piyaṃ me tava dassananti .

Surucijātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^384-7] pañcuposathikajātakaṃ  
[1951] appossukkodāni tuvaṃ kapota  
vihaṅgama na tava bhojanattho  
khuddaṃ pipāsaṃ adhivāsayanto  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 385 -->
<a id="page-385"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 385]*

Kasmā bhavaṃposathiko kapota [^385-1] .

[1952] ahaṃ pure giddhigato kapotiyā    
tasmiṃ padesamhi  ubho [^385-2] ramāma    
athaggahī sākuṇiko kapotiṃ    
akāmako tāya vinā ahosiṃ .

Nānābhāvā [^385-3] vippayogena tassā    
manomayaṃ vedana vedayāmi [^385-4]    
tasmā ahaṃposatha [^385-5] pālayāmi    
rāgo mamaṃ mā punarāgamāsi .

[1953] anujjugāmi uraga dujivha    
dāṭhāvudho ghoravisosi sappa    
khuddaṃ pipāsaṃ adhivāsayanto    
kasmā bhavaṃposathiko nu dīgha [^385-6] .

[1954] usabho ahu balavā gāmikassa    
calakkakū vaṇṇabalūpapanno    
so maṃ akkami taṃ kupito aḍaṃsiṃ    
dukkhābhitunno maraṇaṃ upāgā [^385-7] .

Tato janā nikkhamitvāna gāmā    
kanditvā roditvā apakkamiṃsu    
tasmā ahaṃposatha pālayāmi    
#[^385-1] sī. Yu. Kapoto .   [^385-2] ma. Asmiṃ padesasmimubho .   [^385-3] ma. Nānābhavā .

#[^385-4] sī. Yu. Vedanaṃ vediyāmi .  [^385-5] ma. Ahaṃ posathaṃ . Evamuparipi .  [^385-6] sī. Yu. Dīgho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 386 -->
<a id="page-386"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 386]*

Kodho mamaṃ mā punarāgamāsi .

[1955] matāna maṃsāni bahū susāne    
manuññarūpaṃ tava bhojanetaṃ    
khuddaṃ pipāsaṃ adhivāsayanto    
kasmā bhavaṃposathiko sigāla [^386-1] .

[1956] pavissa [^386-2] kucchiṃ mahato gajassa    
kuṇape rato hatthimaṃsesu giddho [^386-3]    
uṇho ca vāto tikhiṇā ca rasmiyo    
te sosayuṃ tassa karīsamaggaṃ .

Kīso ca paṇḍū ca ahaṃ bhadante    
na me ahu nikkhamanāya maggo    
mahā ca megho sahasā pavassi    
so temayi tassa karīsamaggaṃ .

Tato ahaṃ nikkhamisaṃ bhadante    
cando yathā rāhumukhā pamutto    
tasmā ahaṃposatha pālayāmi    
lobho mamaṃ mā punarāgamāsi .

[1957] vammikathūpasmiṃ kipillikāni  
nippothayanto tuvaṃ pure carāsi  
khuddaṃ pipāsaṃ adhivāsayanto  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 387 -->
<a id="page-387"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 387]*

Kasmā bhavaṃposathiko nu accha [^387-1] .

[1958] sakaṃ niketaṃ atiheḷayāno [^387-2]    
atricchatāya malataṃ [^387-3] agacchiṃ    
tato janā nikkhamitvāna gāmā    
kodaṇḍakena paripothayiṃsu maṃ .

So bhinnasīso ruhiramakkhitaṅgo    
paccāgamāsiṃ sasakaṃ [^387-4] niketaṃ    
tasmā ahaṃposatha pālayāmi    
atricchatā mā punarāgamāsi .

[1959] yaṃ no apucchittha tuvaṃ bhadante    
sabbeva byākarimhā yathāpajānaṃ    
mayampi pucchāma tuvaṃ bhadante    
kasmā bhavaṃposathiko nu brahme .

[1960] anūpalitto mama assamamhi    
paccekabuddho muhuttaṃ nisīdi    
so maṃ avedī gatimāgatiñca    
nāmañca gottaṃ caraṇañca sabbaṃ .

Evampihaṃ [^387-5] vandi na tassa pāde    
na cāpihaṃ [^387-6] mānagatena pucchiṃ    
tasmā ahaṃposatha pālayāmi    
#[^387-1] sī. Yu. Accho .   [^387-2] ma. Atihīḷayāno .   [^387-3] ma. Atricchatā mallagāmaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 388 -->
<a id="page-388"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 388]*

Māno mamaṃ mā punarāgamāsīti .

Pañcuposathikajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^388-8] mahāmorajātakaṃ    
[1961] sace hi tyāhaṃ dhanahetu gahito [^388-1]    
mā maṃ avadhi jīvagāhaṃ gahetvā    
rañño ca [^388-2] maṃ samma upanti [^388-3] nehi    
maññe dhanaṃ lacchasinapparūpaṃ .Tha    
[1962] na me ayaṃ tuyha vadhāya ajja    
samāhito cāpavaro [^388-4] khurappo    
pāsañca tyāhaṃ adhipātayissaṃ    
yathāsukhaṃ gacchatu morarājā .

[1963] yaṃ satta vassāni mamānubandhi    
rattindivaṃ khuppipāsaṃ sahanto    
atha kissa maṃ pāsavasūpanītaṃ    
pamuttave icchasi bandhanasmā .

Pāṇātipātā virato nusajja    
abhayannu te sabbabhūtesu dinnaṃ    
yaṃ maṃ tuvaṃ pāsavasūpanītaṃ    
#[^388-1] ma. Gāhito .   [^388-2] sī. Yu. Va .   [^388-3] ma. Upantikaṃ .  [^388-4] thāmadhanūtipi pāṭho . Sī.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 389 -->
<a id="page-389"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 389]*

Pamuttave icchasi bandhanasmā .

[1964] pāṇātipātā viratassa brūhi๛    
abhayañca yo sabbabhūtesu deti    
pucchāmi taṃ morarājetamatthaṃ    
iti [^389-1] cuto kiṃ labhate sukhaṃ so .

[1965] pāṇātipātā viratassa brūmi    
abhayañca yo sabbabhūtesu deti    
diṭṭheva dhamme labhate pasaṃsaṃ    
saggañca so yāti sarīrabhedā .

[1966] na santi devā iti āhu [^389-2] eke    
idheva jīvo vibhavaṃ upeti    
tathā phalaṃ sukatadukkaṭānaṃ    
dattupaññattañca vadanti dānaṃ    
tesaṃ vaco arahataṃ saddahāno    
tasmā ahaṃ sakuṇe bādhayāmi .

[1967] cando ca suriyo ca ubho sudassanā    
gacchanti obhāsayamantalikkhe    
imassa lokassa parassa vā te    
kathannu te āhu manussaloke .

[1968] cando ca suriyo ca ubho sudassanā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 390 -->
<a id="page-390"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 390]*

Gacchanti obhāsayamantalikkhe    
parassa lokassa na te imassa    
devāti te āhu manussaloke .

[1969] ettheva te nīhatā hīnavādā    
ahetukā ye na vadanti kammaṃ    
tathā phalaṃ sukatadukkaṭānaṃ    
dattupaññattaṃ ye ca vadanti dānaṃ .

[1970] addhā hi saccaṃ vacanaṃ tavetaṃ [^390-1]    
kathañhi dānaṃ aphalaṃ bhaveyya [^390-2]    
tathā phalaṃ sukatadukkaṭānaṃ    
dattupaññattañca kathaṃ bhaveyya .

Kathaṅkaro kintikaro kimācaraṃ    
kiṃ sevamāno kena tapoguṇena    
akkhāhi me morarājetamatthaṃ    
yathā ahaṃ no nirayaṃ pateyyaṃ .

[1971] yekeci atthi samaṇā paṭhabyā    
kāsāyavatthā anagāriyaṃ caranti [^390-3]    
pātova piṇḍāya caranti kāle    
vikālacariyā viratā hi santo .

Te tattha kālenupasaṅkamitvā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 391 -->
<a id="page-391"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 391]*

Pucchāhi yaṃ te manaso piyaṃ siyā    
te taṃ [^391-1] pavakkhanti yathāpajānaṃ    
imassa lokassa parassa catthaṃ .

[1972] tacaṃva jiṇṇaṃ urago purāṇaṃ    
paṇḍupalāsaṃ harito dumova    
esappahīno mama luddabhāvo    
jahāmahaṃ luddakabhāvamajja .

[1973] ye cāpi me sakuṇā atthi bandhā    
satāninekāni nivesanasmiṃ    
tesaṃ ahaṃ [^391-2] jīvitamajja dammi    
mokkhañca te accha [^391-3] sakaṃ niketaṃ .

[1974] luddocarī pāsahattho araññe    
bādhetu morādhipatiṃ yasassiṃ    
bandhitvā morādhipatiṃ yasassiṃ    
dukkhā pamuñci [^391-4] yathāhaṃ pamuttoti .

Mahāmorajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^391-9] tacchasūkarajātakaṃ    
[1975] yadesamānā vicarimhā        pabbatāni vanāni ca    
#[^391-1] ma. Te te .   [^391-2] ma. Tesampahaṃ .   [^391-3] sī. Patto . Ma. Pattā . [^391-4] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 392 -->
<a id="page-392"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 392]*

Anvesaṃ vipule [^392-1] ñātī      teme adhigatā mayā .

Bahuṃ cidaṃ mūlaphalaṃ             bhakkho cāyaṃ anappako    
rammā cimā girinajjo [^392-2]     phāsuvāso bhavissati .

Idhevāhaṃ vasissāmi          saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
appossukko nirāsaṅkī       asoko akutobhayo .

[1976] aññañhi leṇaṃ pariyesa        sattu no idha vijjati    
so taccha sūkare hanti        idhāgantvā varaṃ varaṃ .

[1977] ko numhākaṃ idha sattu        ko ñātī susamāgate    
duppadhaṃse [^392-3] padhaṃseti        taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[1978] uddhaggarājī migarājā        balī dāṭhāvudho migo    
so taccha sūkare hanti        idhāgantvā varaṃ varaṃ .

[1979] na no dāṭhā na vijjanti      balaṃ kāye samohitaṃ    
sabbe samaggā hutvāna       vasaṃ kāhāma ekakaṃ .

[1980] hadayaṅgamaṃ kaṇṇasukhaṃ           vācaṃ bhāsasi tacchaka    
yopi yuddhe pālayetha [^392-4]     taṃpi pacchā hanāmase .

[1981] pāṇātipātā virato nusajja [^392-5]    
abhayaṃ nu te sabbabhūtesu dinnaṃ    
dāṭhā nu te migaviriya [^392-6] na santi    
yo saṅghapatto kapaṇova jhāyasi .

#[^392-1] ma. Vicariṃ .   [^392-2] sī. Yu. Girinadiyo .  [^392-3] sī. Yu. Appadhaṃse .   [^392-4] ma. Palāyeyya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 393 -->
<a id="page-393"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 393]*

[1982] na me dāṭhā na vijjanti      balaṃ kāye samohitaṃ    
ñātī ca disvāna samaṅgi ekato    
tasmā ca jhāyāmi vanamhi ekako .

Imassudaṃ yanti disādisaṃ [^393-1] pure    
bhayaṭṭitā leṇagavesino puthū    
tedāni saṅgamma vasanti ekato    
yatthaṭṭhitā duppasahajja te mayā    
pariṇāyakasampannā           sahitā ekavādino    
te maṃ samaggā hiṃseyyuṃ       tasmā nesaṃ na patthaye [^393-2] .

[1983] ekova indo asure jināti    
ekova seno hanti dije pasayha    
ekova byaggho migasaṅghapatto    
varaṃ varaṃ hanti balañhi tādisaṃ .

[1984] naheva indo na seno       napi byaggho migādhipo    
samagge sahite ñātī         byaggho [^393-3] na kurute vase .

[1985] kumbhilakā sakuṇikā [^393-4]        saṅghino gaṇacārino    
sammodamānā ekajjhaṃ        uppatanti uyyanti [^393-5] ca .

Tesañca ḍiyamānānaṃ [^393-6]       ekettha apasakkati [^393-7]    
tañca seno nitāleti        veyyagghiyeva sā gati .

#[^393-1] ma. Disodisaṃ .   [^393-2] yu. Apatthave .  [^393-3] ma. Na byagghe . Sī. Yu. Byagghe ca .

#[^393-4] ma. Sakuṇakā .  [^393-5] ma. Ḍayanti ca .   [^393-6] bandhiyamānānantipi pāṭho . Sī. Ḍayhamānānaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 394 -->
<a id="page-394"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 394]*

[1986] ussāhito jaṭilena          luddenāmisacakkhunā    
dāṭhī dāṭhīsu pakkhandi         maññamāno yathā pure .

[1987] sādhu sambahulā ñātī         api rukkhā araññajā    
sūkarehi samaggehi           byaggho ekāyane hato .

[1988] brāhmaṇañceva byagghañca      ubho hantvāna sūkarā    
ānandino pamoditā [^394-1]      mahānādamanādisuṃ [^394-2] .

[1989] te sudumbaramūlasmiṃ [^394-3]        sūkarā susamāgatā    
tacchakaṃ abhisiñciṃsu            tvaṃ no rājāsi issaroti .

Tacchasūkarajātakaṃ navamaṃ .

10 mahāvāṇijajātakaṃ    
[1990] vāṇijā samitiṃ katvā         nānāraṭṭhato āgatā    
dhanahārā pakkamiṃsu           ekaṃ katvāna gāmaṇiṃ .

Te taṃ kantāramāgamma        appabhakkhaṃ anūdakaṃ [^394-4]    
mahānigrodhamaddakkhuṃ          sītacchāyaṃ manoramaṃ .

Te ca tattha nisīditvā        tassa rukkhassa chādiyā    
vāṇijā samacintesuṃ          bālā mohena pārutā .

Allāyate [^394-5] ayaṃ rukkho     api vārīva [^394-6] sandati    
iṅghassa purimaṃ sākhaṃ          mayaṃ chindāma vāṇijā .

Sā ca chinnāva pagghari        acchaṃ vāri [^394-7] anāvilaṃ    
#[^394-1] ma. Pamuditā .   [^394-2] ma. Mahānādaṃ panādisuṃ .  [^394-3] ma. Te su udumbaramūlasmiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 395 -->
<a id="page-395"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 395]*

Te tattha nhātvā pivitvā    yāvaticchiṃsu vāṇijā .

Dutiyaṃ samacintesuṃ            bālā mohena pārutā    
iṅghassa dakkhiṇaṃ sākhaṃ         mayaṃ chindāma vāṇijā .

Sā ca chinnāva pagghari        sālimaṃsodanaṃ bahuṃ    
appodavaṇṇe kummāse       siṅgividalasūpiyo    
te tattha bhutvā khāditvā     yāvaticchiṃsu vāṇijā .

Tatiyaṃ samacintesuṃ            bālā mohena pārutā    
iṅghassa pacchimaṃ sākhaṃ         mayaṃ chindāma vāṇijā .

Sā ca chinnāva pagghari        nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
vicittavatthābharaṇā           āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā .

Apisu vāṇijā ekā         nāriyo pañcavīsati    
samantā parikariṃsu [^395-1]         tassa rukkhassa chādiyā    
te tāhi parivāretvā [^395-2]    yāvaticchiṃsu vāṇijā .

Catutthaṃ samacintesuṃ           bālā mohena pārutā    
iṅghassa uttaraṃ sākhaṃ         mayaṃ chindāma vāṇijā .

Sā ca chinnāva pagghari        muttā veḷuriyā bahū    
rajataṃ jātarūpañca            kuttiyo paṭiyāni ca    
kāsikāni ca vatthāni         uddhiyāni [^395-3] ca kambalā    
te tattha bhāre bandhitvā     yāvaticchiṃsu vāṇijā .

Pañcamaṃ samacintesuṃ           bālā mohena pārutā  
#[^395-1] ma. Parivāriṃsu .    [^395-2] ma. Paricāretvā .    [^395-3] uddiyāni . Sī. Yu. Uddiyāne ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 396 -->
<a id="page-396"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 396]*

Iṅghassa mūle [^396-1] chindāma     api bhiyyo labhāmase .

Athuṭṭhahi satthavāho          yācamāno katañjali    
nigrodho kiṃ aparajjhati [^396-2]     vāṇijā bhaddamatthu te .

Vāridā purimā sākhā        annapānañca dakkhiṇā    
nāridā pacchimā sākhā       sabbakāme ca uttarā    
nigrodho kiṃ aparajjhati [^396-2]     vāṇijā bhaddamatthu te .

Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya        nisīdeyya sayeyya vā    
na tassa sākhaṃ bhañjeyya       mittadubbho hi pāpako .

Te ca tassa anāditvā [^396-3]    ekassa vacanaṃ bahū    
nisitāhi kuṭhārīhi            mūlato naṃ upakkamama .

[1991] tato nāgā nikkhamiṃsu         sannaddhā pañcavīsati    
dhanuggahānaṃ tisatā           chasahassā ca cammino [^396-4] .

[1992] ete hanatha bandhatha          mā vo muñcittha jīvitaṃ    
ṭhapetvā satthavāhaṃva         sabbe bhasmaṃ karotha ne .

[1993] tasmāhi paṇḍito poso       sampassaṃ atthamattano    
lobhassa na vasaṃ gacche        haneyya disakaṃ manaṃ [^396-5]    
evamādīnavaṃ [^396-6] ñatvā       taṇhā dukkhassa sambhavaṃ    
vītataṇho anādāno         sato bhikkhu paribbajeti .

Mahāvāṇijajātakaṃ dasamaṃ .

#[^396-1] sī. Yu. Mūlaṃ .   [^396-2] sī. Aparajjhatha . Ma. Parajjhati .   [^396-3] ma. Tasasānādiyitvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 397 -->
<a id="page-397"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 397]*

1[^397-1] sādhinarājajātakaṃ    
[1994] abbhūto vata lokasmiṃ         uppajji lomahaṃsano    
dibbo ratho pāturahu         vedehassa yasassino .

[1995] devaputto mahiddhiko         mātali devasārathi    
nimantayittha rājānaṃ          vedehaṃ mithilaggahaṃ    
ehimaṃ rathamāruyha           rāja seṭṭha disampati    
devā dassanakāmā te       tāvatiṃsā saïndakā    
saramānā hi te devā       sudhammāyaṃ samacchare .

Tato ca rājā sādhino       vedeho mithilaggaho [^397-1]    
sahassayuttaṃ abhiruyha [^397-2]       agā devāna santike    
(sahassayuttaṃ hayavāhiṃ         dibbayānamadhiṭṭhito    
yāyamāno mahārājā        addasa devasabhaṃ idaṃ 3-)    
taṃ devā paṭinandiṃsu          disvā rājānamāgataṃ    
svāgatante mahārāja        atho te adurāgataṃ    
nisīdadāni rājisi            devarājassa santike .

Sakkopi paṭinandittha          vedehaṃ mithilaggahaṃ  
nimantayittha [^397-4] kāmehi       āsanena ca vāsavo  
sādhu khosi anuppatto        āvāsaṃ vasavattinaṃ  
#[^397-1] sī. Yu. Pamukho rathamāruhi .   [^397-2] ma. Sahassayuttamāruyha .  [^397-3] ayaṃ gāthā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 398 -->
<a id="page-398"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 398]*

Vasa devesu rājisi          sabbakāmasamiddhisu    
tāvatiṃsesu devesu          bhuñja kāme amānuse .

[1996] ahaṃ pure saggagato ramāmi    
naccehi gītehi ca vāditehi    
sodāni ajja na ramāmi sagge    
āyunnu khīṇaṃ [^398-1] maraṇannu [^398-2] santike    
udāhu muḷhosmi janindaseṭṭha .

[1997] na tāyu khīṇaṃ maraṇañca [^398-3] dūre    
na cāpi muḷho naravīra seṭṭha    
tuyhañca puññāni parittakāni    
yesaṃ vipākaṃ idha vedayittho [^398-4]    
vasa devānubhāvena          rāja seṭṭha disampati    
tāvatiṃsesu devesu          bhuñja kāme amānuse .

[1998] yathā yācitakaṃ yānaṃ          yathā yācitakaṃ dhanaṃ    
evaṃ sampadamevetaṃ          yaṃ parato dānapaccayā    
na cāhametamicchāmi          yaṃ parato dānapaccayā    
sayaṃ katāni puññāni          taṃ me āveṇiyaṃ dhanaṃ    
sohaṃ gantvā manussesu       kāhāmi kusalaṃ bahuṃ    
dānena samacariyāya          saṃyamena damena ca    
yaṃ katvā sukhito hoti        na ca pacchānutappati .

#[^398-1] ma. Khīṇo . [^398-2] maraṇassātipi pāṭho . [^398-3] sī. Yu. Na cāyu khīṇaṃ maraṇaṃ te .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 399 -->
<a id="page-399"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 399]*

[1999] imāni tāni khettāni        imaṃ nikkhaṃ sakuṇḍalaṃ    
imā tā haritānupā         imā najjo savantiyo    
imā tā pokkharaṇiyo [^399-1] rammā cākavākūpakujjitā [^399-2]    
maṇḍālakehi sañchannā        padumuppalakehi ca    
yassimāni mamāyiṃsu           kiṃ nu te disataṃ gatā .

Tāni ca khettāni so bhūmibhāgo    
teyeva ārāmavanūpacārā [^399-3]    
tameva mayhaṃ janataṃ apassato    
suññañca me nārada khāyate disā .

[2000] diṭṭhā mayā vimānāni        obhāsantā catuddisā    
sammukhā devarājassa         tidasānañca sammukhā .

Vutthaṃ me bhavanaṃ dibyaṃ         bhuttā kāmā amānusā    
tāvatiṃsesu devesu          sabbakāmasamiddhisu .

Sohaṃ etādisaṃ hitvā        puññāyamhi idhāgato    
dhammameva carissāmi          nāhaṃ rajjena atthiko .

Adaṇḍāvacaraṃ maggaṃ           sammāsambuddhadesitaṃ    
taṃ maggaṃ paṭipajjissaṃ          yena gacchanti subbatāti .

Sādhinarājajātakaṃ ekādasamaṃ .

#[^399-1] ma. Pokkharaṇī .   [^399-2] ma. Cakkavākapakūjitā . Sī. Yu. Cakkavākūpakūjitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 400 -->
<a id="page-400"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 400]*

1[^400-2] dasabrāhmaṇajātakaṃ    
[2001] rājā avoca vidhūraṃ [^400-1]       dhammakāmo yudhiṭṭhilo .

Brāhmaṇe vidhūra pariyesa      sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2002] dullabhā brāhmaṇā deva      sīlavanto bahussutā    
viratā methunā dhammā        ye te bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ .

Dasa khalu mahārāja           yā tā brāhmaṇajātiyo    
tesaṃ vibhaṅgavicayaṃ            vitthārena suṇohi me .

Pasibbake gahetvāna         puṇṇe mūlassa saṃvute    
osadhikāye [^400-2] ganthenti     nhāpayanti [^400-3] japanti ca .

Tikicchakasamā rāja           tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

[2003] apetā te ca brāhmaññā    (iti rājā ca [^400-4] korabyo)    
na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2004] kiṅkiṇiyo gahetvāna [^400-5]      ghosenti puratopi te    
pesanānipi gacchanti          rathacariyāsu sikkhare .

#[^400-1] ma. Vidhura . Evamuparipi .  [^400-2] ma. Osadhikāyo . [^400-3] sī. Yu. Nahāyanti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 401 -->
<a id="page-401"></a>

Paricārikasamā rāja          tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2006] kamaṇḍalala gahetvāna          vaṅkadaṇḍañca [^401-1] brāhmaṇā    
paccupessanti rājāno       gāmesu nigamesu ca    
nādinne vuṭṭhahessāma       gāmamhi nigamamhi [^401-2] vā    
niggāhakasamā rāja          tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2008] parūḷhakacchanakhalomā          paṅkadantā rajassirā  
okiṇṇā rajareṇūhi          yācakā vicaranti te  
#[^401-1] paṅkadaṇḍañcātipi pāṭho .   [^401-2] ma. Gāmamhi vā vanamhi vā . Sī. Yu. Gāmamhi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 402 -->
<a id="page-402"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 402]*

Khāṇughātasamā rāja          tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

[2009] apetā te ca brāhmaññā    (iti rājā ca korabyo)    
na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2010] harītakaṃ āmalakaṃ             ambaṃ jambaba vibhedakaṃ [^402-1]    
labujaṃ dantapoṇāni           veluvā badarāni ca    
rājāyatanaṃ ucchupūṭaṃ          dhūmanettaṃ madhuñjanaṃ    
uccāvacāni paṇīyāni         vikīṇanti [^402-2] janādhipa    
vāṇijakasamā rāja           tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

[2011] apetā te ca brāhmaññā    (iti rājā ca korabyo)    
na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2012] kasivaṇijjaṃ [^402-3] kārenti       posayanti ajeḷake    
#[^402-1] ma. Vibhītakaṃ . Sī. Yu. Ambajambuvibhīṭakaṃ .  [^402-2] sī. Ma. Vipaṇenti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 403 -->
<a id="page-403"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 403]*

Kumāriyo pavecchanti         vivāhantāvahanti ca    
samā ambaṭṭhavesehi         tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhāte te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

[2013] apetā te ca brāhmaññā    (iti rājā ca korabyo)    
na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2014] nikkhittabhikkhaṃ bhuñjanti         gāmesveke purohitā    
bahū ne paripucchanti          aṇḍacchedā nilañcakā [^403-1]    
pasūpi tattha haññanti          mahiṃsā sūkarā ajā    
goghātakasamā rāja          tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

[2015] apetā te ca brāhmaññā    (iti rājā ca korabyo)    
na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2016] asicammaṃ gahetvāna          khaggaṃ paggayha brāhmaṇā  
vessapathesu tiṭṭhanti         satthaṃ abbāhayantipi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 404 -->
<a id="page-404"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 404]*

Samā gopanisādehi          tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

[2017] apetā te ca brāhmaññā    (iti rājā ca korabyo)    
na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2018] araññe kuṭikaṃ katvā         kūṭāni kārayanti te    
sasaviḷāre bādhenti         āgodhā macchakacchapaṃ    
te luddakasamā rāja [^404-1]      tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

[2019] apetā te ca brāhmaññā    (iti rājā ca korabyo)    
na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2020] aññe dhanassa kāmā hi       heṭṭhā mañce pasakkhitā [^404-2]    
rājāno upari nhāyanti      somayāge upaṭṭhite    
malamajjasamā [^404-3] rāja        tepi vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 405 -->
<a id="page-405"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 405]*

[2021] apetā te ca brāhmaññā    (iti rājā ca korabyo)    
na te vuccanti brāhmaṇā    
aññe vidhūra pariyesa         sīlavante bahussute    
virate methunā dhammā        ye me bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ samma dassāma         yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

[2022] atthi kho brāhmaṇā deva     sīlavanto bahussutā    
viratā methunā dhammā        ye te bhuñjeyyu bhojanaṃ .

Ekañca bhattaṃ bhuñjanti        na ca majjaṃ pivanti te    
akkhātā te mahārāja       tādise nipatāmase .

[2023] ete kho brāhmaṇā vidhūra    sīlavanto bahussutā    
ete vidhūra pariyesa         khippañca ne nimantayāti .

Dasabrāhmaṇajātakaṃ dvādasamaṃ .

1[^405-3] bhikkhāparamparajātakaṃ    
[2024] sukhumālarūpaṃ disvā           raṭṭhā vivanamāgataṃ    
kūṭāgāravarūpetaṃ            mahāsayanamupocitaṃ [^405-1]    
tassa te pemakenāhaṃ        adāsiṃ vaḍḍhamodanaṃ [^405-2]    
sālīnaṃ vicittaṃ bhattaṃ          sucimaṃsūpasecanaṃ [^405-3]    
taṃ tvaṃ bhattaṃ paṭiggayha        brāhmaṇassa adāpayi [^405-4]    
#[^405-1] mahāsayanamupāsitantipi .    [^405-2] sī. Yu. Baddhamodanaṃ .   [^405-3] ma. Suciṃ maṃsūpasecanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 406 -->
<a id="page-406"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 406]*

Attānaṃ anasitvāna          koyaṃ dhammo namatthu te .

[2025] ācariyo brāhmaṇo mayhaṃ     kiccākiccesu vyāvaṭo    
garu ca āmantaniyo ca        dātumarahāmi bhojanaṃ .

[2026] brāhmaṇaṃdāni pucchāmi        gotamaṃ rājapūjitaṃ    
rājā te bhattaṃ pādāsi      sucimaṃsūpasecanaṃ    
taṃ tvaṃ bhattaṃ paṭiggayha        isissa bhojanaṃ adā    
akhettaññūsi dānassa         koyaṃ dhammo namatthu te .

[2027] bharāmi puttadāre ca         gharesu gadhito [^406-1] ahaṃ    
bhuñja mānusake kāme        anusāsāmi rājino    
āraññikassa isino          cirarattaṃ tapassino    
vuḍḍhassa bhāvitattassa         dātumarahāmi bhojanaṃ .

[2028] isiñcadāni pucchāmi          kīsaṃ dhamanisanthataṃ    
parūḷhakacchanakhalomaṃ           paṅkadantaṃ rajassiraṃ    
eko araññe viharasi        nāvakaṅkhasi jīvitaṃ    
bhikkhu kena tayā seyyo      yassa tvaṃ bhojanaṃ adā .

[2029] khaṇamālukalambāni [^406-2]         bilālitakkalāni [^406-3] ca    
dhunaṃ sāmākanivāraṃ           saṃhāriyaṃ pahāriyaṃ [^406-4]    
sākaṃ bhisaṃ madhadha maṃsaṃ           badarāmalakāni ca    
tāni āhatva [^406-5] bhuñjāmi     atthi me so pariggaho    
#[^406-1] sī. Yu. Gathito .   [^406-2] ma. Khaṇantālukalambāni .    [^406-3] sī. Yu. Biḷālitakkaḷāni ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 407 -->
<a id="page-407"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 407]*

Pacanto apacantassa          amamassa sakiñcano    
anādānassa sādāno        dātumarahāmi bhojanaṃ .

[2030] bhikkhuñcadāni pucchāmi         tuṇhimāsīna subbataṃ    
iti te bhattaṃ pādāsi        sucimaṃsūpasecanaṃ    
taṃ tvaṃ bhattaṃ paṭiggayha        tuṇhī bhuñjasi ekako    
nāññaṃ kañci nimantesi        koyaṃ dhammo namatthu te .

[2031] na pacāmi na pācemi         na chindāmi na chedaye    
taṃ maṃ akiñcanaṃ ñatvā         sabbapāpehi ārataṃ    
vāmena bhikkhamādāya         dakkhiṇena kamaṇḍalala    
isi me bhattaṃ pādāsi        sucimaṃsūpasecanaṃ .

Ete hi dātumarahanti        sammā sapariggahā    
paccanīkamahaṃ maññe           yo dātāraṃ nimantaye .

[2032] atthāya vata me ajja        idhāgacchi rathesabho    
yohaṃ ajja pajānāmi [^407-1]      yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ .

Raṭṭhesu giddhā rājāno      kiccākiccesu brāhmaṇā    
isī mūlaphale giddhā          vippamuttā ca bhikkhavoti .

Bhikkhāparamparajātakaṃ terasamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 408 -->
<a id="page-408"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 408]*

tassuddānaṃ    
suva kinnaramukka kharājinaso    
bhisajāta mahesi kapotavaro    
atha mora satacchaka vāṇijako    
atha rāja sabrāhmaṇa bhikkhaparaṃ .

Pakiṇṇakanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 409 -->
<a id="page-409"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 409]*

Vīsatinipātajātakaṃ    
[^409-1] mātaṅgajātakaṃ    
[2033] kuto nu āgacchasi dummavāsī [^409-1]    
otallako paṃsupisācakova    
saṅkāracoḷaṃ paṭimuñca kaṇṭhe    
ko re tuvaṃ hosi [^409-2] adakkhiṇeyyo .

[2034] annaṃ tavayidaṃ pakataṃ yasassinaṃ [^409-3]    
taṃ khajjare bhuñjare piyyare ca    
jānāsi maṃ tvaṃ paradattūpajīvī [^409-4]    
uttiṭṭhapiṇḍaṃ labhataṃ sapāko .

[2035] annaṃ mamayidaṃ [^409-5] kapataṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ    
attatthāya saddahato mamayidaṃ [^409-5]    
apehi etto kimidhaṭṭhitosi    
na mādisā tuyhaṃ dadanti jamma .

[2036] thale ca ninne ca vapanti bījaṃ    
anūpakhette phalamāsiṃsamānā [^409-6]    
etāya saddhāya dadāhi dānaṃ    
appeva ārādhaye dakkhiṇeyye .

[2037] khettāni mayhaṃ viditāni loke    
#[^409-1] sī. Yu. Rummavāsī .   [^409-2] sī. Yu. Hohisi .   [^409-3] ma. - tavedaṃ pakataṃ yasassi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 410 -->
<a id="page-410"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 410]*

Yesāhaṃ bījāni patiṭṭhapemi    
ye brāhmaṇā jātimantūpapannā    
tānīdha khettāni supesalāni .

[2038] jātimado ca atimānatā ca    
lobho ca doso ca mado ca moho    
ete aguṇā yesu ca santi [^410-1] sabbe    
tānīdha khettāni apesalāni .

Jātimado ca atimānitā ca    
lobho ca doso ca mado ca moho    
ete aguṇā yesu na santi sabbe    
tānīdha khettāni supesalāni .

[2039] kvattha [^410-2] gatā upajotiyo ca    
upajjhāyo [^410-3] athavā bhaṇḍakucchi [^410-4]    
imassa daṇḍañca vadhañca datvā    
gale gahetvā galayātha [^410-5] jammaṃ .

[2040] giriṃ nakhena khaṇasi            ayo dantebhi khādasi    
jātavedaṃ padahasi            yo isiṃ paribhāsasi .

[2041] idaṃ vatvāna mātaṅgo        isi saccaparakkamo    
#[^410-1] sī. Yu. Yesu vasanti .  [^410-2] ma. Kvettha .  [^410-3] sabbattha upajjhāyoti dissati .

#aṭṭhakathāyampana upavajjhoti khāyati . Ma. Upajjhāyo ca .     [^410-4] gaṇḍakucchi itipi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 411 -->
<a id="page-411"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 411]*

Antalikkhasmiṃ pakkāmi         brāhmaṇānaṃ udikkhataṃ .

[2042] āvellitaṃ [^411-1] piṭṭhito uttamaṅgaṃ    
bāhuṃ pasāreti akammaneyyaṃ    
setāni akkhīni yathā matassa    
ko me imaṃ puttamakāsi evaṃ .

[2043] idhāgamā samaṇo dummavāsī    
otallako paṃsupisācakova    
saṅkāracoḷaṃ paṭimuñca kaṇṭhe    
so te imaṃ puttamakāsi evaṃ .

[2044] katamaṃ disaṃ agamā bhūripañño    
akkhātha me māṇavā etamatthaṃ    
gantvāna taṃ paṭikaremu accayaṃ    
appeva naṃ puttaṃ [^411-2] labhemu jīvitaṃ .

[2045] vehāyasaṃ agamā bhūripañño    
pathaddhuno paṇṇaraseva cando    
apicāpi so purimaṃ disaṃ agacchi    
saccappaṭiñño isi sādhurūpo .

[2046] āvellitaṃ piṭṭhito uttamaṅgaṃ  
bāhuṃ pasāreti akammaneyyaṃ  
setāni akkhīni yathā matassa  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 412 -->
<a id="page-412"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 412]*

Ko me imaṃ puttamakāsi evaṃ .

[2047] yakkhā have santi mahānubhāvā    
anvāgatā isayo sādhurūpā    
te duṭṭhacittaṃ kupitaṃ viditvā    
yakkhā hi te puttamakaṃsu evaṃ .

[2048] yakkhā ca me puttamakaṃsu evaṃ    
tvaññeva me mā kuddho brahmacārī    
tumheva pāde saraṇaṃ gatāsmi    
anvāgatā puttasokena bhikkhu .

[2049] tadeva hi etarahi ca mayhaṃ    
manopadoso na mamatthi koci    
putto ca te vedamadena matto    
atthaṃ na jānāti adhicca vede .

[2050] addhā have bhikkhu muhuttakena    
sammuyhateva purisassa saññā    
ekāparādhaṃ khama bhūripañña    
na paṇḍitā kodhabalā bhavanti .

[2051] idañca mayhaṃ uttiṭṭhapiṇḍaṃ  
tava maṇḍabyo bhuñjatu appapañño  
yakkhā ca te puttaṃ [^412-1] na viheṭhayeyyuṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 413 -->
<a id="page-413"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 413]*

Putto ca te hessati [^413-1] so arogo .

[2052] maṇḍabya bālosi parittapañño    
yo puññakhettānamakovidosi    
mahakkasāvesu dadāsi dānaṃ    
kiliṭṭhakammesu asaññatesu .

Jaṭā ca kesā ajinā nivatthā    
jarūdapānaṃva mukhaṃ paruḷhaṃ    
pajaṃ imaṃ passatha dummarūpaṃ [^413-2]    
na jaṭājinaṃ tāyate appapaññaṃ .

Yesaṃ rāgo ca doso ca      avijjā ca virājitā    
khīṇāsavā arahanto          tesu dinnaṃ mahapphalanti .

[2053] upahacca manaṃ mejjho         mātaṅgasmiṃ yasassine    
sapārisajjo ucchinno        mejjharaññaṃ tadā ahūti [^413-3] .

Mātaṅgajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

[^413-2] cittasambhūtajātakaṃ  
[2054] sabbaṃ narānaṃ saphalaṃ suciṇṇaṃ  
na kammunā kiñcana moghamatthi  
passāmi sambhūtaṃ mahānubhāvaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 414 -->
<a id="page-414"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 414]*

Sakammunā puññaphalūpapannaṃ .

Sabbaṃ narānaṃ saphalaṃ suciṇṇaṃ    
na kammunā kiñcana moghamatthi    
kaccinnu cittassapi evameva [^414-1]    
iddho mano tassa yathāpi mayhaṃ .

[2055] sabbaṃ narānaṃ saphalaṃ suciṇṇaṃ    
na kammunā kiñcana moghamatthi    
cittaṃpi jānāhi [^414-2] tatheva deva    
iddho mano tassa yathāpi tuyhaṃ .

[2056] bhavaṃ nu citto sutamaññato te    
udāhu te koci naṃ etadakkhā    
gāthā sugītā na mamatthi kaṅkhā    
dadāmi te gāmavaraṃ satañca .

[2057] na cāhaṃ citto sutamaññato me    
isi ca me etamatthaṃ asaṃsi    
gantvāna rañño paṭigāyi [^414-3] gāthaṃ    
api nu [^414-4] te varaṃ attamano dadeyya [^414-5] .

[2058] yojentu me rājarathe       sukate cittasibbane    
kacchaṃ nāgānaṃ bandhatha         gīveyyaṃ paṭimuñcatha .

#[^414-1] ma. Evamevaṃ .   [^414-2] sī. Yu. Cittaṃ vijānāhi .   [^414-3] ma. Paṭigāhi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 415 -->
<a id="page-415"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 415]*

Āhaññare bherimudiṅgasaṅkhā [^415-1]    
sīghāni yānāni ca yojayantu    
ajjevahaṃ assamataṃ [^415-2] gamissaṃ    
yattheva dakkhissamisiṃ nisinnaṃ .

[2059] suladdhalābho vata me ahosi    
gāthā sugītā parisāya majjhe    
sohaṃ isiṃ sīlavatūpapannaṃ    
disvā patīto sumanohamasmi .

[2060] āsanaṃ udakaṃ pajjaṃ           paṭiggaṇhātu no bhavaṃ    
agghe bhavantaṃ pucchāma        agghaṃ kurutu no bhavaṃ .

[2061] rammañca te āvasathaṃ karontu    
nārīgaṇehi paricārayassu    
karohi okāsamanuggahāya    
ubhopimaṃ issariyaṃ karoma .

[2062] disvā phalaṃ duccaritassa rāja    
atho suciṇṇassa mahāvipākaṃ    
attānameva paṭisaṃyamissaṃ    
na patthaye puttapasuṃ [^415-3] dhanaṃ vā .

Dasevimā vassadasā            maccānaṃ idha jīvitaṃ    
#[^415-1] haññantu bho bherimudiṅgasaṅkhetipi . Ma. Āhaññantu bherimudiṅgasaṅkhe .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 416 -->
<a id="page-416"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 416]*

Appattaññeva taṃ odhiṃ          naḷo chinnova sussati .

Tattha kā nandi kā khiḍḍā       kā rati kā dhanesanā    
kiṃ me puttehi dārehi         rāja muttosmi bandhanā .

Sohaṃ evaṃ pajānāmi [^416-1]        maccu me nappamajjati    
antakenādhipannassa            kā rati kā dhanesanā .

Jāti narānaṃ adhamā janinda    
caṇḍālayoni dvipadakaniṭṭhā [^416-2]    
sakehi kammehi supāpakehi    
caṇḍāligabbhe [^416-3] avasimha pubbe .

Caṇḍālāhumhāvantīsu           migā nerañjaraṃ pati    
ukkusā rammadātīre [^416-4]        tayajja brāhmaṇakhattiyā .

[2063] upaniyyati jīvitamappamāyuṃ    
jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā    
karohi pañcāla mameva [^416-5] vākyaṃ    
mākāsi kammāni dukkhudrayāni .

Upaniyyati jīvitamappamāyuṃ    
jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā    
karohi pañcāla mameva vākyaṃ    
mākāsi kammāni dukkhapphalāni .

Upaniyyati jīvitamappamāyuṃ    
#[^416-1] sī. Yu. So ahaṃ suppajānāmi .    [^416-2] ma. Dvipadākaniṭṭhā .  [^416-3] ma. Caṇḍālagabbhe .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 417 -->
<a id="page-417"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 417]*

Jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā    
karohi pañcāla mameva vākyaṃ    
mākāsi kammāni rajassirāni .

Upaniyyati jīvitamappamāyuṃ    
vaṇṇaṃ jarā hanti narassa jiyyato    
karohi pañcāla mameva vākyaṃ    
mākāsi kammaṃ nirayūpapattiyā .

[2064] addhā hi saccaṃ vacanaṃ tavedaṃ    
yathā isī bhāsasi evametaṃ    
kāmā ca me santi anapparūpā    
te duccajā mādisakena bhikkhu .

Nāgo yathā paṅkamajjhe byasanno    
sayaṃ [^417-1] thalaṃ nābhisambhoti gantuṃ    
evamahaṃ [^417-2] kāmapaṅke byasanno    
na bhikkhuno maggamanubbajāmi .

Yathāpi mātā ca pitā ca puttaṃ    
anusāsare kinti sukhī bhaveyya    
evaṃpi maṃ tvaṃ anusāsa bhante    
yathā ciraṃ [^417-3] pecca sukhī bhaveyyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 418 -->
<a id="page-418"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 418]*

[2065] no ce tuvaṃ ussahase janinda    
kāme ime mānusake pahātuṃ    
dhammiṃ [^418-1] baliṃ paṭṭhapayassu rāja    
adhammakāro ca te [^418-2] māhu raṭṭhe .

Dūtā vidhāvantu disā catasso    
nimantakā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ    
te annapānena upaṭṭhahassu    
vatthena senāsanapaccayena ca .

Annena pānena pasannacitto    
santappaya samaṇabrāhmaṇe ca    
datvā ca bhutvā ca yathānubhāvaṃ    
anindito saggamupehi ṭhānaṃ .

Sace ca taṃ rāja mado saheyya    
nārīgaṇehi parivārayantaṃ [^418-3]    
imameva gāthaṃ manasikarohi    
bhāsehi cetaṃ [^418-4] parisāya majjhe .

Abbhokāsasayo jantu           vajantyā khīrapāyito    
parikiṇṇo suvānehi [^418-5]         svājja rājāti vuccatīti .

Cittasambhūtajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ .

#[^418-1] sī. Yu. Dhammaṃ .   [^418-2] ma. Tava .  [^418-3] ma. Paricārayantaṃ .  [^418-4] ma. Bhāsesi cenaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 419 -->
<a id="page-419"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 419]*

[^419-3] sīvirājajātakaṃ    
[2066] dūre apassaṃ therova         cakkhuṃ yācitumāgato    
ekanettā bhavissāma        cakkhuṃ me dehi yācito .

[2067] kenānusiṭṭho idhamāgatosi    
vanibbaka cakkhupathāni yācituṃ    
suduccajaṃ yācasi uttamaṅgaṃ    
yamāhu nettaṃ purisena duccajaṃ .

[2068] yamāhu devesu sujampatīti    
maghavāti naṃ āhu manussaloke    
tenānusiṭṭho idhamāgatosmi    
vanibbako cakkhupathāni yācituṃ .

Vanibbako [^419-1] mayha vaṇiṃ anuttaraṃ    
dadāhi me [^419-2] cakkhupathāni yācito    
dadāhi me cakkhupathaṃ anuttaraṃ    
yamāhu nettaṃ purisena duccajaṃ .

Yena atthena āgañchi        yamatthamabhipatthayaṃ    
te te ijjhantu saṅkappā     labha cakkhūni brāhmaṇa .

Ekante yācamānassa        ubhayāni dadāmahaṃ    
sa cakkhumā gaccha janassa pekkhato    
yadicchase tvaṃ tadate samijjhatu .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 420 -->
<a id="page-420"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 420]*

[2069] mā no deva adā cakkhuṃ      mā no sabbe parakkari [^420-1]    
dhanaṃ dehi mahārāja          muttā veḷuriyā bahū .

Yutte deva rathe dehi       ājāniye alaṅkate [^420-2]    
nāge dehi mahārāja        hemakappanivāsase .

Yathā taṃ sīviyo sabbe        sayoggā sarathā sadā    
samantā parikareyyuṃ          evaṃ dehi rathesabha .

[2070] yo ve dassanti vatvāna      adāne kurute mano    
bhūmyaṃ so patitaṃ pāsaṃ         gīvāyaṃ paṭimuñcati .

Yo ve dassanti vatvāna      adāne kurute mano    
pāpā pāpataro hoti        sampatto yamasādhanaṃ .

Yañhi yāce tañhi dade       yaṃ na yāce na taṃ dade    
svāhaṃ tameva dassāmi        yaṃ maṃ yācati brāhmaṇo .

[2071] āyuṃ nu vaṇṇaṃ nu sukhaṃ balaṃ nu    
kiṃ patthayāno nu janinda desi    
kathañhi rājā sivinaṃ anuttaro    
cakkhūni dajjā paralokahetu .

[2072] navāhametaṃ yasasā dadāmi    
na puttamicche na dhanaṃ na raṭṭhaṃ    
satañca dhammo carito purāṇo    
icceva dāne ramati [^420-3] mano mama .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 421 -->
<a id="page-421"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 421]*

[2073] na [^421-1] me dessā ubho cakkhū  attānaṃ me na dessiyaṃ    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ          tasmā cakkhumadāsihaṃ [^421-1] .

[2074] sakhā ca mitto ca mamāsi sīvika    
susikkhito sādhu karohi me vaco    
uddharitvā cakkhūni mama jigiṃsato    
hatthesu ṭhapehi vanibbakassa .

[2075] codito sīvirājena          sīviko vacanaṃ karo    
rañño cakkhūni uddharitvā      brāhmaṇassūpanāmayi[^421-4]    
sacakkhu brāhmaṇo āsi       andho rājā upāvisi .

[2076] tato so katipāhassa         uparūḷhesu cakkhusu    
sutaṃ āmantayi rājā         sīvīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhano .

Yojehi sārathi yānaṃ         yuttañca paṭivedaya    
uyyānabhūmiṃ gacchāma          pokkharaññe vanāni ca .

So ca pokkharaṇītīre         pallaṅkena upāvisi    
tassa sakko pāturahu         devarājā sujampati .

[2077] sakkohamasmi devindo        āgatosmi tavantike    
varaṃ varassu rājisi           yaṅkiñci manasicchasi .

[2078] pahutaṃ [^421-2] me dhanaṃ sakka       balaṃ koso canappako    
andhassa me satodāni        maraṇaññeva ruccati .

#1-[^421-1] ayaṃ gāthā sī. Potthakeceva aṭṭhakathāpotthake ca dissati . Ma. Ayampana gāthā natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 422 -->
<a id="page-422"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 422]*

[2079] yāni saccāni dipadinda        tāni bhāsassu khattiya    
saccaṃ te bhaṇamānassa         puna cakkhu bhavissati .

[2080] ye maṃ yācitumāyanti         nānāgottā vanibbakā    
yopi maṃ yācate tattha        sopi me manaso piyo    
etena saccavajjena         cakkhu me upapajjatha .

[2081] yaṃ maṃ so yācituṃ āgā       dehi cakkhunti brāhmaṇo    
tassa cakkhūni pādāsiṃ         brāhmaṇassa vanibbato [^422-1] .

Bhiyyo maṃ āvisi pīti         somanassañcanappakaṃ    
etena saccavajjena         dutiyaṃ me upapajjatha .

[2082] dhammena bhāsitā gāthā       sīvīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhana    
etāni tava nettāni        dibyāni [^422-2] paṭidissare .
